    for extra in attack.get('extra_damage') or []:
        text += f" + {extra['dice_count']}{extra['dice_type']}"
        if extra.get('modifier'):
            text += f"{extra['modifier']:+d}"
        if extra.get('damage_type'):
            text += f" {extra['damage_type']}"
    return text


def extract_gallery(html=None, base_url='https://dnd.su', soup=None, problems=None):
    """Картинки монстра, крупные первыми. soup переиспользуется, если передан."""
    from urllib.parse import urljoin
    big, small, seen = [], [], set()
    try:
        if soup is None:
            soup = make_soup(html or '')

        def push(raw):
            if not raw:
                return
            raw = raw.strip().split()[0]
            if raw.startswith('data:'):
                return
            url = urljoin(base_url, raw)
            if not EXT_RE_IMG.search(url) or url in seen:
                return
            low = url.lower()
            if any(s in low for s in GALLERY_SKIP):
                return
            if not any(h in low for h in GALLERY_HINT):
                return
            seen.add(url)
            (small if any(t in low for t in THUMB_HINT) else big).append(url)

        for a in soup.find_all('a', href=True):
            push(a['href'])
        for tag in soup.find_all('img'):
            for attr in ('data-original', 'data-full', 'data-src', 'data-lazy-src'):
                if tag.get(attr):
                    push(tag[attr])
                    break
            srcset = tag.get('srcset') or ''
            best, best_w = None, -1
            for part in srcset.split(','):
                bits = part.strip().split()
                if not bits:
                    continue
                w = 0
                if len(bits) > 1 and bits[1].endswith('w'):
                    try:
                        w = int(bits[1][:-1])
                    except ValueError:
                        w = 0
                if w > best_w:
                    best, best_w = bits[0], w
            if best:
                push(best)
            push(tag.get('src'))
    except Exception as exc:
        if problems is not None:
            problems.append(f'галерея не разобрана: {exc}')
        return []
    return big + small


def _blank_monster(url):
    return {
        'name': '', 'hp': None, 'ac': None, 'attacks': [], 'regen': 0, 'speed': None,
        'source_url': url, 'multiattack': None, 'warnings': [], 'errors': [],
        'legendary_actions': None, 'legendary_resistance': None, 'lair_actions': None,
        'special_actions': [], 'bonus_actions': [], 'reactions': [],
        'ability_scores': {}, 'ability_mods': {}, 'saves': {}, 'skills': {}, 'senses': {},
        'languages': [], 'vulnerabilities': [], 'resistances': [], 'immunities': [],
        'condition_immunities': [], 'traits': [], 'cr': None, 'prof_bonus': None,
        'size': None, 'type': None,
    }


def _monster_name(soup):
    node = soup.select_one('h2.card-title [data-copy], h2.card-title span, h1')
    name = node.get_text(' ', strip=True) if node else ''
    if not name and soup.title and soup.title.string:
        name = soup.title.string.split('/')[0].strip()
    name = re.sub(r'\s*\[.*?\]', '', name)
    name = re.split(r'\s+(?:MM\d+|FTD|PBSO|MPMM)\b', name)[0]
    name = re.split(r'[—–-]\s*Бестиарий', name)[0]
    name = re.sub(r'\s*\([A-Za-z]{2,6}\)\s*[—–-].*$', '', name)
    return clean_text(name) or 'Без имени'


def _apply_card(card, monster):
    for key in ('hp', 'ac', 'speed', 'cr', 'prof_bonus', 'size', 'type',
                'ability_scores', 'ability_mods', 'saves', 'skills', 'senses',
                'languages', 'vulnerabilities', 'resistances', 'immunities',
                'condition_immunities'):
        if card.get(key) not in (None, {}, []):
            monster[key] = card[key]
    monster['traits'] = list(card.get('traits') or [])
    monster['regen'] = _regen_from_traits(monster['traits'])
    attacks, multi, specials = parse_action_blocks(card.get('actions'), monster['warnings'])
    monster['attacks'] = attacks
    monster['multiattack'] = multi
    monster['special_actions'] = specials
    monster['bonus_actions'] = [item for item in
                                (_attach_effect(_named_action(text), monster['warnings'])
                                 for text in card.get('bonus') or [])
                                if item]
    monster['reactions'] = [item for item in
                            (_attach_effect(_named_action(text), monster['warnings'])
                             for text in card.get('reactions') or [])
                            if item]
    monster['legendary_actions'] = parse_legendary(
        card.get('legendary'), attacks, monster['warnings'])
    monster['legendary_resistance'] = parse_legendary_resistance(monster['traits'])
    if card.get('lair_items') or card.get('lair_intro'):
        monster['lair_actions'] = {
            'intro': card.get('lair_intro') or '',
            'actions': list(card.get('lair_items') or []),
        }


def _parse_loose(soup, monster):
    """Запасной путь, если карточки нет: тот же разбор атак, но по тексту страницы."""
    ref = extract_statblock_text(soup)
    battle = extract_battle_text(soup)
    speed = re.search(r'Скорость\s*(\d+)', ref, re.I)
    if speed:
        monster['speed'] = int(speed.group(1))
    hp = re.search(r'(?:Хиты|Hit\s+Points)[^\d]{0,20}(\d+)', ref, re.I)
    if hp:
        monster['hp'] = int(hp.group(1))
    ac = re.search(r'(?:Класс\s+(?:Доспеха|Защиты)|Armor\s+Class)[^\d]{0,20}(\d+)', ref, re.I)
    if ac:
        monster['ac'] = int(ac.group(1))
    chars = parse_characteristics(soup, ref)
    errors = chars.pop('errors', None)
    if errors:
        monster['errors'].extend(errors)
    monster.update(chars)
    monster['regen'] = _regen_from_traits(monster.get('traits')) or monster.get('regen') or 0
    attacks, multi, specials = parse_action_blocks(
        [piece for piece in re.split(r'\n+', battle) if piece.strip()], monster['warnings'])
    monster['attacks'] = attacks
    monster['multiattack'] = multi
    monster['special_actions'] = specials
    monster['legendary_resistance'] = parse_legendary_resistance(monster.get('traits'))


def _validate(monster):
    warnings = monster.setdefault('warnings', [])
    errors = monster.setdefault('errors', [])
    if monster.get('hp') is None:
        warnings.append('хиты не распознаны')
        monster['hp'] = 10
    if monster.get('ac') is None:
        warnings.append('класс доспеха не распознан')
        monster['ac'] = 10
    if monster.get('speed') is None:
        warnings.append('скорость не распознана')
        monster['speed'] = 30
    scores = monster.get('ability_scores') or {}
    if len(scores) < 6:
        warnings.append('характеристики распознаны не полностью')
    if not monster.get('attacks'):
        warnings.append('атаки не распознаны')
    for attack in monster.get('attacks') or []:
        die = int(str(attack.get('damage_dice_type', 'd0')).split('d')[-1] or 0)
        if attack.get('damage_dice_count', 0) < 1 or die not in _VALID_DICE:
            warnings.append(f"некорректная кость урона: {attack.get('name')}")
        for extra in attack.get('extra_damage') or []:
            extra_die = int(str(extra.get('dice_type', 'd0')).split('d')[-1] or 0)
            if extra.get('dice_count', 0) < 1 or extra_die not in _VALID_DICE:
                warnings.append(f"некорректная кость урона: {attack.get('name')}")
    monster['warnings'] = list(dict.fromkeys(warnings))
    monster['errors'] = list(dict.fromkeys(errors))


def _mark_magic_weapons(monster):
    if any(MAGIC_TRAIT_RE.search(f"{trait.get('name', '')} {trait.get('desc', '')}")
           for trait in (monster.get('traits') or [])):
        for attack in monster['attacks']:
            attack['magical'] = True


def parse_statblock(html, url='https://dnd.su'):
    """Статблок -> словарь. warnings/errors заполнены явно, заглушки атаки нет."""
    monster = _blank_monster(url)
    try:
        soup = make_soup(html or '')
        monster['name'] = _monster_name(soup)
        card = extract_card(soup)
        if card.get('found'):
            _apply_card(card, monster)
        else:
            monster['warnings'].append('карточка статблока не найдена, разбор по тексту страницы')
            _parse_loose(soup, monster)
        _mark_magic_weapons(monster)
        monster['portraits'] = extract_gallery(base_url=url, soup=soup, problems=monster['warnings'])
    except Exception as exc:
        monster['errors'].append(f'разбор прерван: {exc}')
    _validate(monster)
    return monster


def search_monsters(html, query):
    soup = make_soup(html)
    out, seen = [], set()
    q = query.lower()
    for link in soup.find_all('a', href=True):
        href = link['href']
        if not MONSTER_URL_RE.search(href):
            continue
        name = link.get_text(strip=True)
        if not name or q not in name.lower() or href in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(href)
        url = href if href.startswith('http') else f'https://dnd.su{href}'
        out.append({'name': name, 'url': url})
    out.sort(key=lambda e: (not e['name'].lower().startswith(q), e['name'].lower()))
    return out


def fetch(url, params=None, attempts=3):
    """GET с парой повторов на обрыв связи и 429/5xx. Строка поиска — в params."""
    last = None
    tries = max(1, int(attempts))
    for attempt in range(tries):
        try:
            response = requests.get(url, headers=HEADERS, timeout=(6, 20), params=params)
            response.encoding = 'utf-8'
            if response.status_code in RETRY_STATUS and attempt + 1 < tries:
                time.sleep(RETRY_BACKOFF[min(attempt, len(RETRY_BACKOFF) - 1)])
                continue
            if response.status_code != 200:
                raise RuntimeError(f'Сайт ответил кодом {response.status_code}')
            return response.text
        except (requests.Timeout, requests.ConnectionError) as exc:
            last = exc
            if attempt + 1 >= tries:
                raise
            time.sleep(RETRY_BACKOFF[min(attempt, len(RETRY_BACKOFF) - 1)])
    if last:
        raise last
    raise RuntimeError('Сайт не ответил')


def _cache_key(url, params=None):
    if not params:
        return url
    return requests.Request('GET', url, params=params).prepare().url


def _disk_file(key):
    digest = hashlib.sha256(key.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
    return CACHE_DIR / f'{digest}.html'


def fetch_cached(url, params=None):
    """Память, затем небольшой диск-кэш рядом с модулем, затем сеть."""
    key = _cache_key(url, params)
    hit = _PAGES.get(key)
    if hit is not None:
        _PAGES.move_to_end(key)
        return hit
    path = _disk_file(key)
    try:
        if path.is_file():
            return _remember(_PAGES, key, path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    except OSError:
        pass
    text = fetch(url, params=params)
    try:
        CACHE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
    except OSError:
        pass
    return _remember(_PAGES, key, text)


def fetch_search(query):
    return fetch_cached('https://dnd.su/bestiary/', params={'search': query})


def load_monster(url):
    """Полный цикл для фонового потока: страница (кэш) -> разбор (кэш)."""
    hit = _MONSTERS.get(url)
    if hit is not None:
        _MONSTERS.move_to_end(url)
        return hit
    return _remember(_MONSTERS, url, parse_statblock(fetch_cached(url), url))


def preview_text(m):
    lines = []
    for error in m.get('errors') or []:
        lines.append(f'⛔ {error}')
    for warning in m.get('warnings') or []:
        lines.append(f'⚠ {warning}')
    lines.append(f"👾 {m['name']}")
    head = [m[k] for k in ('size', 'type') if m.get(k)]
    if m.get('cr'):
        head.append(f"CR {m['cr']}")
    if m.get('prof_bonus'):
        head.append(f"Бонус мастерства {m['prof_bonus']}")
    if head:
        lines.append(' · '.join(head))
    lines.append(f"❤️ Хиты: {m['hp']}   🛡️ КД: {m['ac']}   👟 Скорость: {m.get('speed', 30)} фт")
    if m.get('regen'):
        lines.append(f"💚 Регенерация: {m['regen']}")
    sc, md = m.get('ability_scores') or {}, m.get('ability_mods') or {}
    if sc:
        cells = []
        for k in ABILITY_ORDER:
            if k in sc:
                sign = '+' if md.get(k, 0) >= 0 else ''
                cells.append(f"{ABILITY_LABEL[k]} {sc[k]} ({sign}{md.get(k, 0)})")
        lines.append(' · '.join(cells))
    for label, key in (('Спасброски', 'saves'), ('Навыки', 'skills'), ('Чувства', 'senses')):
        d = m.get(key) or {}
        if d:
            lines.append(f"{label}: " + ', '.join(f'{k} {v}'.strip() for k, v in d.items()))
    for label, key in (('Уязвимости', 'vulnerabilities'), ('Сопротивления', 'resistances'),
                       ('Иммунитеты', 'immunities'), ('Иммун. к условиям', 'condition_immunities'),
                       ('Языки', 'languages')):
        lst = m.get(key) or []
        if lst:
            lines.append(f"{label}: " + ', '.join(lst))
    if m.get('multiattack'):
        lines.append('⚔️ Мультиатака: ' + ' + '.join(
            f"{s['attack']}×{s.get('count', 1)}" for s in m['multiattack']))
    lines.append('⚔️ Атаки:' if m.get('attacks') else '⚔️ Атаки: нет')
    for attack in m.get('attacks') or []:
        mark = ' ✦маг.' if attack.get('magical') else ''
        lines.append(f"  • {attack['name']}{mark} ({attack['attack_bonus']:+d}, "
                     f"{format_damage(attack)}) · {attack.get('range_ft', 5)} фт")
    legendary = m.get('legendary_actions') or None
    if legendary:
        lines.append(f"👑 Легендарные действия: {legendary.get('per_round')} за раунд")
        for option in legendary.get('options') or []:
            mark = ' → бой' if option.get('usable') else ''
            lines.append(f"  • {option['name']} (стоит {option.get('cost', 1)}){mark}")
    resist = m.get('legendary_resistance')
    if resist:
        lines.append(f"👑 Легендарное сопротивление: {resist.get('count')}/день")
    lair = m.get('lair_actions') or None
    if lair and lair.get('actions'):
        lines.append(f"🕳 Действия логова: {len(lair['actions'])} (в бою не выполняются)")
    for trait in (m.get('traits') or []):
        lines.append(f"✨ {trait['name']}. {trait['desc']}")
    return '\n'.join(lines)


==================== combat_core.py ====================

"""Чистый слой боевого расчёта: без интерфейса и без состояния окна.

modify_damage уважает УСЛОВНЫЕ иммунитеты: запись «дробящий от немагического
оружия» не блокирует атаку с magical=True (RAW). Семантика защиты берётся из
decision_engine — единый источник истины, чтобы мозг и исполнитель не расходились.
"""
from decision_engine import (dmg_canon, active_defenses, is_nonmagical_only)
from battle_strategies import distance_ft_between, is_healing_attack
from creature_size import space_distance_ft
from combat_log import CombatEvent, DamagePart

# Как стол удваивает крит. 'double_all' — историческое поведение и дефолт.
CRIT_DOUBLE_ALL = 'double_all'
CRIT_DICE_ONLY = 'dice_only'
DEFAULT_CRIT_MODE = CRIT_DOUBLE_ALL
CRIT_MODE_LABELS = {
    CRIT_DOUBLE_ALL: 'Удваивать весь урон',
    CRIT_DICE_ONLY: 'Удваивать только кости',
}


def normalize_crit_mode(value):
    """Ключ режима крита. Нет ключа, пусто или неизвестное значение → дефолт."""
    if value in CRIT_MODE_LABELS:
        return value
    for key, label in CRIT_MODE_LABELS.items():
        if value == label:
            return key
    return DEFAULT_CRIT_MODE


def resolve_strikes(attacker, target):
    if target is not None and getattr(target, 'side', None) == getattr(attacker, 'side', None):
        attack = attacker.strategy.select_attack(attacker, target)
        return [attack] if attack else []
    if getattr(attacker, 'multiattack', None):
        by_name = {a.name: a for a in attacker.attacks}
        strikes = []
        for s in attacker.multiattack:
            atk = by_name.get(s['attack'])
            if atk is None:
                continue
            strikes.extend([atk] * max(1, int(s.get('count', 1))))
        if strikes:
            return strikes
    a = attacker.strategy.select_attack(attacker, target)
    return [a] if a else []


def strike_reach(strikes):
    if not strikes:
        return 5
    return min(getattr(a, 'range_ft', 5) for a in strikes)


def in_range_of(attacker, target, atk, battle_map):
    need = getattr(atk, 'range_ft', 5)
    if battle_map is not None and battle_map.get_pos(attacker) \
            and battle_map.get_pos(target):
        return battle_map.distance_ft(attacker, target) <= need
    return space_distance_ft(attacker, target) <= need


def nearest_in_range(attacker, atk, ignore, sides, battle_map):
    need = getattr(atk, 'range_ft', 5)
    has_map = battle_map is not None and battle_map.get_pos(attacker) is not None
    best, best_d = None, None
    for side_idx, side_enemies in enumerate(sides):
        if side_idx == attacker.side:
            continue
        if side_idx in ignore or side_idx in attacker.ignore_sides:
            continue
        for e in side_enemies:
            if not e.alive:
                continue
            if not has_map:
                return e
            if battle_map.get_pos(e) is None:
                continue
            d = battle_map.distance_ft(attacker, e)
            if d <= need and (best_d is None or d < best_d):
                best, best_d = e, d
    return best


def _roll_component(attacker, comp, critical, crit_mode):
    """Дополнительный урон тем же режимом крита, что и основной бросок."""
    import random
    rng = getattr(attacker, '_rng', None) or random
    sides = int(str(comp.get('dice_type') or 'd6').split('d')[-1] or 6)
    count = int(comp.get('dice_count') or 0)
    modifier = int(comp.get('modifier') or 0)
    rolls = max(0, count)
    if critical and crit_mode == 'dice_only':
        rolls *= 2
    total = sum(rng.randint(1, max(1, sides)) for _ in range(rolls)) + modifier
    if critical and crit_mode != 'dice_only':
        total *= 2
    return max(0, total)


def _typed_damage(target, raw, damage_type, magical=False):
    """Иммунитет, уязвимость и сопротивление типа. Без окаменения."""
    canon = dmg_canon(damage_type)
    if not canon or raw <= 0:
        return raw, ''
    raw_imm = list(getattr(target, 'immunities', []) or [])
    imm = active_defenses(raw_imm, magical)
    vul = {dmg_canon(x) for x in (getattr(target, 'vulnerabilities', []) or [])}
    res = active_defenses(getattr(target, 'resistances', []) or [], magical)

    if canon in imm:
        return 0, f" → 0 (иммунитет: {damage_type})"
    if canon in vul:
        final = raw * 2
        return final, f" → {final} (уязвимость: {damage_type})"
    if canon in res:
        final = raw // 2
        return final, f" → {final} (сопротивление: {damage_type})"
    if magical and any(is_nonmagical_only(x) and dmg_canon(x) == canon for x in raw_imm):
        return raw, " ✦ магическое оружие пробило иммунитет к немагическому"
    return raw, ''


def _petrified_damage(target, raw, final, note, canon):
    """Окаменение: иммунитет к яду и сопротивление любому урону (не стакается)."""
    from conditions import has_condition
    if not has_condition(target, 'petrified') or final <= 0:
        return final, note
    if canon == 'poison':
        return 0, f'{note} → 0 (окаменение: иммунитет к яду)'.strip()
    if 'сопротивление' in (note or ''):
        return final, note
    if 'уязвимость' in (note or ''):
        return raw, f'{note} → {raw} (окаменение гасит уязвимость)'
    halved = final // 2
    return halved, f'{note} → {halved} (окаменение: сопротивление)'.strip()


def modify_damage(target, raw, damage_type, magical=False):
    """Чистая функция: считает итоговый урон, НЕ наносит его.
    magical=True снимает условные иммунитеты/сопротивления «от немагического»."""
    final, note = _typed_damage(target, raw, damage_type, magical)
    return _petrified_damage(target, raw, final, note, dmg_canon(damage_type))


def _apply_heal(attacker, target, atk):
    if hasattr(attacker, 'perform_heal'):
        amount = attacker.perform_heal(atk)
    else:
        amount = 0
    if hasattr(target, 'heal'):
        return target.heal(amount)
    if not getattr(target, 'alive', False):
        return 0
    before = target.hp
    cap = getattr(target, 'max_hp', before)
    target.hp = min(cap, before + max(0, int(amount)))
    return target.hp - before


def _roll_details(roll, attack):
    """5 значений контракта perform_attack и, если есть, натуральная кость."""
    hit, damage, total, critical, info = roll
    natural = getattr(roll, 'natural', None)
    modifier = getattr(roll, 'modifier', None)
    if modifier is None:
        modifier = getattr(attack, 'attack_bonus', None)
    return hit, damage, total, critical, info, natural, modifier


def _strike_result(hit, critical, natural):
    if critical:
        return 'crit'
    if natural == 1:
        return 'nat1'
    if hit:
        return 'hit'
    return 'miss'


def _actor_fields(attacker, target, battle_map):
    def pos_of(creature):
        if creature is None:
            return None
        pos = None
        if battle_map is not None and hasattr(battle_map, 'get_pos'):
            pos = battle_map.get_pos(creature)
        if not pos:
            pos = getattr(creature, 'pos', None)
        if not pos:
            return None
        return (pos[0], pos[1])

    return {
        'actor': getattr(attacker, 'name', None),
        'actor_id': id(attacker) if attacker is not None else None,
        'actor_side': getattr(attacker, 'side', None),
        'target': getattr(target, 'name', None) if target is not None else None,
        'target_id': id(target) if target is not None else None,
        'target_side': getattr(target, 'side', None) if target is not None else None,
        'actor_pos': pos_of(attacker),
        'target_pos': pos_of(target),
    }


def execute_strikes(attacker, target, strikes, sides, ignore_sides, battle_map,
                    crit_mode=DEFAULT_CRIT_MODE, *, round_no=None, events=None,
                    legendary=False):
    """Удары и лечение. Возврат — прежний (строки, было_ли_убийство).

    Если передан events, туда дописываются CombatEvent. Строки собраны из
    event.text, поэтому текст лога не расходится со структурой.
    """
    if target is None:
        return [], False
    crit_mode = normalize_crit_mode(crit_mode)
    produced, any_killed = [], False
    ignore = ignore_sides.get(getattr(attacker, 'side', None), set())
    for atk in strikes:
        if is_healing_attack(atk):
            produced.extend(_heal_strike(
                attacker, target, atk, battle_map, round_no, legendary))
            continue
        if target is None or not target.alive:
            target = nearest_in_range(attacker, atk, ignore, sides, battle_map)
            if target is None:
                break
        if not in_range_of(attacker, target, atk, battle_map):
            alt = nearest_in_range(attacker, atk, ignore, sides, battle_map)
            if alt is None:
                continue
            target = alt
        hit_events, killed = _attack_strike(
            attacker, target, atk, battle_map, crit_mode, round_no, legendary, sides)
        produced.extend(hit_events)
        any_killed = any_killed or killed
    if events is not None:
        events.extend(produced)
    lines = [event.text for event in produced if event.text is not None]
    return lines, any_killed


def _heal_strike(attacker, target, atk, battle_map, round_no, legendary):
    common = _actor_fields(attacker, target, battle_map)
    common.update(round=round_no, legendary=legendary, action=getattr(atk, 'name', None))
    if (target is None or not getattr(target, 'alive', False)
            or getattr(target, 'side', None) != getattr(attacker, 'side', None)):
        text = f'{attacker.name} не применяет {atk.name}: лечить некого'
        return [CombatEvent(kind='heal', result='no_target', text=text, **common)]
    if not in_range_of(attacker, target, atk, battle_map):
        text = (f'{attacker.name} не может достать {target.name} лечением '
                f'({atk.name})')
        return [CombatEvent(kind='heal', result='out_of_reach', text=text, **common)]
    before = getattr(target, 'hp', None)
    gained = _apply_heal(attacker, target, atk)
    after = getattr(target, 'hp', None)
    text = (f'{attacker.name} → {target.name} ({atk.name}) - ЛЕЧЕНИЕ! '
            f'+{gained} HP ({target.hp}/{target.max_hp})')
    return [CombatEvent(
        kind='heal', result='heal', text=text, heal_amount=gained,
        hp_before=before, hp_after=after,
        max_hp=getattr(target, 'max_hp', None), **common)]


def _advantage_mark(roll):
    mode = getattr(roll, 'advantage', None) or 'normal'
    dice = list(getattr(roll, 'naturals', None) or [])
    if mode == 'advantage' and len(dice) == 2:
        return f' [преимущество {dice[0]}/{dice[1]}]'
    if mode == 'disadvantage' and len(dice) == 2:
        return f' [помеха {dice[0]}/{dice[1]}]'
    if mode == 'cancel':
        return ' [преимущество и помеха гасятся]'
    return ''


def _attack_distance(attacker, target, battle_map):
    if (battle_map is not None and battle_map.get_pos(attacker)
            and battle_map.get_pos(target)):
        return battle_map.distance_ft(attacker, target)
    return distance_ft_between(attacker, target)


def _attack_strike(attacker, target, atk, battle_map, crit_mode, round_no, legendary,
                   sides=None):
    from conditions import attack_modifiers
    advantage, disadvantage, force_crit = attack_modifiers(
        attacker, target, _attack_distance(attacker, target, battle_map))
    roll = attacker.perform_attack(
        atk, target.ac, crit_mode=crit_mode, advantage=advantage,
        disadvantage=disadvantage, force_crit=force_crit)
    hit, damage, attack_roll, critical, damage_info, natural, modifier = _roll_details(roll, atk)
    result = _strike_result(hit, critical, natural)
    common = _actor_fields(attacker, target, battle_map)
    before = getattr(target, 'hp', None)
    if hit:
        magical = bool(getattr(atk, 'magical', False))
        final, dmg_note = modify_damage(target, damage, atk.damage_type, magical=magical)
        target.take_damage(final)
        parts = [DamagePart(getattr(atk, 'damage_type', '') or '', damage, final, dmg_note, False)]
        extra_notes = []
        for extra in getattr(atk, 'extra_damage', None) or []:
            raw = _roll_component(attacker, extra, critical, crit_mode)
            extra_final, note = modify_damage(target, raw, extra.get('damage_type', ''),
                                              magical=magical)
            target.take_damage(extra_final)
            label = extra.get('damage_type') or 'доп.'
            extra_notes.append(f"+ {extra_final} ({label}){note}")
            parts.append(DamagePart(label, raw, extra_final, note, True))
        crit_text = " КРИТИЧЕСКИЙ УДАР!" if critical else ""
        extra_text = (' ' + ' '.join(extra_notes)) if extra_notes else ''
        mark = _advantage_mark(roll)
        text = (f"{attacker.name} → {target.name} ({atk.name}: "
                f"{attack_roll} vs AC {target.ac}){mark} - ПОПАДАНИЕ!{crit_text} "
                f"{damage_info}{dmg_note}{extra_text}")
        after = getattr(target, 'hp', None)
        event = CombatEvent(
            kind='attack', result=result, crit=bool(critical), text=text,
            action=getattr(atk, 'name', None), natural=natural, modifier=modifier,
            naturals=list(getattr(roll, 'naturals', None) or []),
            advantage=getattr(roll, 'advantage', None),
            attack_total=attack_roll, ac=getattr(target, 'ac', None),
            damages=parts, hp_before=before, hp_after=after,
            max_hp=getattr(target, 'max_hp', None),
            round=round_no, legendary=legendary, **common)
        produced = [event]
        killed = not getattr(target, 'alive', True)
        if not killed:
            from effects import apply_on_hit
            produced.extend(apply_on_hit(
                attacker, target, atk, sides, round_no, legendary=legendary))
        if killed:
            produced.append(CombatEvent(
                kind='death', result='death', text=f"☠️ {target.name} УНИЧТОЖЕН!",
                action=getattr(atk, 'name', None), hp_before=before, hp_after=after,
                max_hp=getattr(target, 'max_hp', None),
                round=round_no, legendary=legendary, **common))
            from effects import on_creature_down
            produced.extend(on_creature_down(target, sides, round_no, actor=attacker))
        return produced, killed
    mark = _advantage_mark(roll)
    text = (f"{attacker.name} → {target.name} ({atk.name}: "
            f"{attack_roll} vs AC {target.ac}){mark} - ПРОМАХ!")
    event = CombatEvent(
        kind='attack', result=result, crit=False, text=text,
        action=getattr(atk, 'name', None), natural=natural, modifier=modifier,
        naturals=list(getattr(roll, 'naturals', None) or []),
        advantage=getattr(roll, 'advantage', None),
        attack_total=attack_roll, ac=getattr(target, 'ac', None),
        damages=[], hp_before=before, hp_after=before,
        max_hp=getattr(target, 'max_hp', None),
        round=round_no, legendary=legendary, **common)
    return [event], False


def refresh_legendary_pool(enemy, reset=False):
    """В начале хода существа пул полный; до первого хода — тоже, если ещё не задан."""
    block = getattr(enemy, 'legendary_actions', None)
    if not block:
        return
    if reset or getattr(enemy, 'legendary_remaining', None) is None:
        enemy.legendary_remaining = int(block.get('per_round') or 0)


def legendary_after_turn(just_acted, sides, ignore_sides, battle_map,
                         crit_mode=DEFAULT_CRIT_MODE, *, round_no=None, events=None):
    """Конец чужого хода: каждое другое легендарное существо тратит одно действие.

    Атака, которая достаёт цель, или разбираемое действие со спасброском.
    Перемещение и неразобранный текст по-прежнему пропускаются.
    Недееспособное существо легендарное действие не тратит.
    Возврат — список прежних текстовых строк. events, если передан, получает
    те же CombatEvent.
    """
    produced = []
    for side in sides:
        for enemy in side:
            if enemy is just_acted or not getattr(enemy, 'alive', False):
                continue
            produced.extend(_spend_legendary(
                enemy, sides, ignore_sides, battle_map, crit_mode, round_no=round_no))
    if events is not None:
        events.extend(produced)
    return [event.text for event in produced if event.text is not None]


def _best_legendary_effect(enemy, block, remaining, ignore, sides, battle_map):
    from conditions import HARD_DISABLE
    from effects import average_damage, targets_for
    best = None
    for option in block.get('options') or []:
        effect = option.get('effect') if isinstance(option, dict) else None
        if not isinstance(effect, dict):
            continue
        cost = int(option.get('cost') or 1)
        if cost > remaining:
            continue
        group = targets_for(enemy, effect, sides, ignore, battle_map)
        if not group:
            continue
        score = average_damage(effect.get('damage')) * len(group)
        conditions = effect.get('conditions') or []
        if any(item in HARD_DISABLE for item in conditions):
            score += 8 * len(group)
        elif conditions:
            score += 3 * len(group)
        if best is None or score > best[0]:
            best = (score, option, effect, cost)
    return best


def _spend_legendary(enemy, sides, ignore_sides, battle_map, crit_mode, round_no=None):
    from battle_strategies import damage_score
    from conditions import is_incapacitated
    block = getattr(enemy, 'legendary_actions', None)
    if not block or is_incapacitated(enemy):
        return []
    refresh_legendary_pool(enemy, reset=False)
    remaining = int(getattr(enemy, 'legendary_remaining', 0) or 0)
    if remaining <= 0:
        return []
    ignore = ignore_sides.get(getattr(enemy, 'side', None), set())
    by_name = {attack.name: attack for attack in getattr(enemy, 'attacks', []) or []}
    best = None
    for option in block.get('options') or []:
        if not option.get('usable') or not option.get('attack'):
            continue
        cost = int(option.get('cost') or 1)
        if cost > remaining:
            continue
        attack = by_name.get(option['attack'])
        if attack is None:
            continue
        target = nearest_in_range(enemy, attack, ignore, sides, battle_map)
        if target is None:
            continue
        score = damage_score(attack) * max(1, int(option.get('count') or 1))
        if best is None or score > best[0]:
            best = (score, option, attack, target, cost)
    effect_best = _best_legendary_effect(
        enemy, block, remaining, ignore, sides, battle_map)
    attack_score = best[0] if best is not None else -1
    if effect_best is not None and effect_best[0] > attack_score:
        _score, option, effect, cost = effect_best
        from effects import consume_recharge, execute_effect
        produced = execute_effect(
            enemy, effect, sides, ignore, battle_map, round_no=round_no,
            legendary=True)
        if effect.get('recharge'):
            consume_recharge(enemy, option.get('name') or effect.get('name'))
        enemy.legendary_remaining = remaining - cost
        for event in produced:
            event.legendary = True
            if not event.reason:
                event.reason = option.get('name')
        return produced
    if best is None:
        return []
    _score, option, attack, target, cost = best
    count = max(1, int(option.get('count') or 1))
    strikes = []
    execute_strikes(
        enemy, target, [attack] * count, sides, ignore_sides, battle_map,
        crit_mode=crit_mode, round_no=round_no, events=strikes, legendary=True)
    enemy.legendary_remaining = remaining - cost
    for event in strikes:
        if not event.reason:
            event.reason = option['name']
    header_text = (f"👑 {enemy.name} — легендарное действие «{option['name']}» "
                   f"(стоит {cost})")
    header = CombatEvent(
        kind='legendary', round=round_no, legendary=True, cost=cost,
        action=option['name'], text=header_text, reason=option.get('desc') or None,
        actor=getattr(enemy, 'name', None), actor_id=id(enemy),
        actor_side=getattr(enemy, 'side', None),
        target=getattr(target, 'name', None), target_id=id(target),
        target_side=getattr(target, 'side', None),
    )
    return [header, *strikes]


==================== combat_log.py ====================

"""Структурированный журнал боя. Без привязки к интерфейсу.

run_round собирает CombatEvent и рисует текстовый лог уже из них
(render_log), чтобы тесты читали те же строки.

Поля hp_before / hp_after / max_hp отделены от факта попадания. conceal_enemy_hp
прячет хиты чужой стороны — заготовка «вида игрока», в интерфейсе её нет.
"""
import json
import os
import re
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field, fields

LOG_VERSION = 1

# Хиты, которые будущий вид игрока скрывает у чужих существ.
GM_ONLY_FIELDS = ('hp_before', 'hp_after', 'max_hp')

_HP_FRACTION = re.compile(r'\s*\(\d+/\d+\)')
_POS_FIELDS = ('from_pos', 'to_pos', 'actor_pos', 'target_pos')


@dataclass
class DamagePart:
    """Урон одного типа: raw — до иммунитета/сопротивления/уязвимости, final — после."""

    damage_type: str
    raw: int
    final: int
    note: str = ''
    extra: bool = False

    def to_dict(self):
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        return cls(
            damage_type=data.get('damage_type') or '',
            raw=int(data.get('raw') or 0),
            final=int(data.get('final') or 0),
            note=data.get('note') or '',
            extra=bool(data.get('extra') or False),
        )


@dataclass
class InitiativeRoll:
    name: str
    side: int | None
    actor_id: int | None
    natural: int
    modifier: int
    total: int
    tie_roll: int
    dex: int | None = None

    def to_dict(self):
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        return cls(
            name=data.get('name') or '',
            side=data.get('side'),
            actor_id=data.get('actor_id'),
            natural=int(data.get('natural') or 0),
            modifier=int(data.get('modifier') or 0),
            total=int(data.get('total') or 0),
            tie_roll=int(data.get('tie_roll') or 0),
            dex=data.get('dex'),
        )


@dataclass
class CombatEvent:
    kind: str
    round: int | None = None
    actor: str | None = None
    actor_id: int | None = None
    actor_side: int | None = None
    target: str | None = None
    target_id: int | None = None
    target_side: int | None = None
    action: str | None = None
    natural: int | None = None
    modifier: int | None = None
    attack_total: int | None = None
    ac: int | None = None
    result: str | None = None
    crit: bool = False
    damages: list = field(default_factory=list)
    heal_amount: int | None = None
    hp_before: int | None = None
    hp_after: int | None = None
    max_hp: int | None = None
    from_pos: tuple | None = None
    to_pos: tuple | None = None
    actor_pos: tuple | None = None
    target_pos: tuple | None = None
    reason: str | None = None
    text: str | None = None
    legendary: bool = False
    rolls: list = field(default_factory=list)
    cost: int | None = None
    dc: int | None = None
    save_ability: str | None = None
    condition: str | None = None
    naturals: list = field(default_factory=list)
    advantage: str | None = None
    resistance_used: bool = False
    area_cells: list = field(default_factory=list)
    area_origin: tuple | None = None
    area_ids: list = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_dict(self):
        data = {}
        for item in fields(self):
            data[item.name] = _dump(getattr(self, item.name))
        return data

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        kwargs = {}
        for item in fields(cls):
            if item.name not in data:
                continue
            value = data[item.name]
            if item.name == 'damages':
                value = [DamagePart.from_dict(part) for part in (value or [])]
            elif item.name == 'rolls':
                value = [InitiativeRoll.from_dict(part) for part in (value or [])]
            elif item.name == 'naturals':
                value = [int(part) for part in (value or []) if part is not None]
            elif item.name == 'area_cells':
                value = [_as_pos(part) for part in (value or []) if _as_pos(part)]
            elif item.name == 'area_ids':
                value = [int(part) for part in (value or []) if part is not None]
            elif item.name in _POS_FIELDS or item.name == 'area_origin':
                value = _as_pos(value)
            kwargs[item.name] = value
        return cls(**kwargs)


class InitiativeEntry:
    """Строка инициативы. Распаковка и [0]..[4] — как у прежнего кортежа из 5 полей."""

    __slots__ = ('total', 'dex', 'tie', 'side_idx', 'enemy', 'natural', 'modifier')

    def __init__(self, total, dex, tie, side_idx, enemy, natural, modifier):
        self.total = total
        self.dex = dex
        self.tie = tie
        self.side_idx = side_idx
        self.enemy = enemy
        self.natural = natural
        self.modifier = modifier

    def __iter__(self):
        yield self.total
        yield self.dex
        yield self.tie
        yield self.side_idx
        yield self.enemy

    def __len__(self):
        return 5

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        return (self.total, self.dex, self.tie, self.side_idx, self.enemy)[index]


class RoundResult:
    """Итог раунда. Распаковка `outcome, text = run_round(...)` сохранена."""

    __slots__ = ('outcome', 'text', 'events')

    def __init__(self, outcome, text, events):
        self.outcome = outcome
        self.text = text
        self.events = list(events)

    def __iter__(self):
        yield self.outcome
        yield self.text

    def __len__(self):
        return 2

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        return (self.outcome, self.text)[index]


def _dump(value):
    if hasattr(value, 'to_dict'):
        return value.to_dict()
    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return [_dump(part) for part in value]
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_dump(part) for part in value]
    return value


def _as_pos(value):
    if not value or not isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) or len(value) < 2:
        return None
    return (value[0], value[1])


def format_pos(pos):
    if not pos:
        return '?'

    def one(value):
        try:
            number = float(value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return str(value)
        if number.is_integer():
            return str(int(number))
        return str(round(number, 2))

    return f'({one(pos[0])}, {one(pos[1])})'


def render_log(events) -> str:
    """Прежний текстовый журнал: строки событий через перевод строки.

    text is None в склейку не входит. Пустая строка (разделитель раунда) входит.
    """
    return '\n'.join(event.text for event in events if event.text is not None)


def detail_lines(event: CombatEvent) -> list:
    """Разбор, которого нет в однострочном тексте: кость, защиты, хиты, причина."""
    lines = []
    if event.kind == 'initiative':
        for roll in event.rolls:
            lines.append(
                f'{roll.name}: d20 {roll.natural} {roll.modifier:+d} = {roll.total}, '
                f'ничья d20 {roll.tie_roll}')
        return lines
    if event.kind == 'attack' and event.natural is not None:
        mod = event.modifier or 0
        label = {
            'crit': 'крит',
            'hit': 'попадание',
            'miss': 'промах',
            'nat1': 'натуральная 1, промах',
        }.get(event.result, event.result or '')
        lines.append(
            f'бросок d20 {event.natural} {mod:+d} = {event.attack_total} '
            f'против КД {event.ac} ({label})')
        if event.advantage == 'advantage' and len(event.naturals) == 2:
            lines.append(
                f'преимущество: кости {event.naturals[0]} и {event.naturals[1]}, '
                f'взята {event.natural}')
        elif event.advantage == 'disadvantage' and len(event.naturals) == 2:
            lines.append(
                f'помеха: кости {event.naturals[0]} и {event.naturals[1]}, '
                f'взята {event.natural}')
        elif event.advantage == 'cancel':
            lines.append('преимущество и помеха взаимно гасятся')
    elif event.kind == 'attack' and event.attack_total is not None:
        lines.append(f'итог атаки {event.attack_total} против КД {event.ac}')
    for part in event.damages:
        prefix = 'доп. ' if part.extra else ''
        note = part.note or ''
        lines.append(
            f'{prefix}{part.damage_type or "урон"}: {part.raw} до защит → {part.final} после{note}')
    if event.kind == 'save':
        ability = event.save_ability or ''
        if event.advantage == 'auto_fail' or event.natural is None:
            lines.append(f'спасбросок {ability}: автоматический провал, Сл {event.dc}')
        elif event.natural is not None:
            mod = event.modifier or 0
            lines.append(
                f'спасбросок {ability}: d20 {event.natural} {mod:+d} = {event.attack_total} '
                f'против Сл {event.dc}')
        if event.resistance_used:
            lines.append('легендарное сопротивление потрачено')
    if event.kind in ('condition', 'recharge', 'lair') and event.condition:
        lines.append(f'состояние: {event.condition}')
    if event.kind == 'heal' and event.heal_amount is not None:
        lines.append(f'лечение +{event.heal_amount}')
    if event.hp_before is not None and event.hp_after is not None and event.kind != 'initiative':
        lines.append(f'HP: {event.hp_before} → {event.hp_after}')
    if event.kind == 'move' and event.from_pos and event.to_pos:
        lines.append(f'с {format_pos(event.from_pos)} на {format_pos(event.to_pos)}')
    if event.legendary and event.kind != 'legendary':
        lines.append('легендарное действие')
    if event.reason and event.reason not in (event.text or ''):
        lines.append(f'причина: {event.reason}')
    if event.area_cells:
        lines.append(f'клеток области: {len(event.area_cells)}')
    return lines


def render_markdown(events) -> str:
    out = ['# Журнал боя', '']
    last_round = object()
    opened = False
    for event in events:
        if event.kind == 'spacer' or not event.text:
            continue
        if event.kind == 'round_start' and event.round != last_round:
            last_round = event.round
            title = f'Раунд {event.round}' if event.round else 'Раунд'
            if opened:
                out.append('')
            out.append(f'## {title}')
            out.append('')
            opened = True
        body = ' '.join(event.text.split())
        out.append(f'- {body}')
        for line in detail_lines(event):
            out.append(f'  - {line}')
    out.append('')
    return '\n'.join(out)


def document_from_events(events) -> dict:
    return {
        'version': LOG_VERSION,
        'gm_only_fields': list(GM_ONLY_FIELDS),
        'events': [event.to_dict() for event in events],
    }


def render_json(events, *, indent=2) -> str:
    return json.dumps(document_from_events(events), ensure_ascii=False, indent=indent)


def events_from_json(payload) -> list:
    if isinstance(payload, str):
        payload = json.loads(payload)
    if isinstance(payload, list):
        raw_events = payload
    else:
        raw_events = payload.get('events') or []
    return [CombatEvent.from_dict(item) for item in raw_events]


def dump_log(events, path) -> str:
    """Пишет .json / .md / иначе текст. Возвращает записанную строку."""
    path = str(path)
    ext = os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower()
    if ext == '.json':
        payload = render_json(events)
    elif ext in ('.md', '.markdown'):
        payload = render_markdown(events)
    else:
        payload = render_log(events)
    parent = os.path.dirname(path)
    if parent:
        os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
        handle.write(payload)
    return payload


def load_events(path) -> list:
    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
        return events_from_json(handle.read())


def _strip_hp_fraction(text: str) -> str:
    return _HP_FRACTION.sub('', text)


def _hp_subject_side(event: CombatEvent):
    """Чьи хиты записаны в событии. Регенерация — хиты действующего."""
    if event.kind == 'heal' and event.action == 'регенерация':
        return event.actor_side
    if event.kind in ('attack', 'death', 'heal'):
        if event.target_side is not None:
            return event.target_side
        return event.actor_side
    return None


def conceal_enemy_hp(event: CombatEvent, viewer_side: int) -> CombatEvent:
    """Копия для будущего вида игрока: точные хиты чужой стороны убраны.

    Попадание, промах, крит, имена и типы урона остаются. В интерфейсе
    переключатель не подключён.
    """
    clone = CombatEvent.from_dict(event.to_dict())
    subject = _hp_subject_side(event)
    if subject is None or subject == viewer_side:
        return clone
    clone.hp_before = None
    clone.hp_after = None
    clone.max_hp = None
    if clone.text:
        clone.text = _strip_hp_fraction(clone.text)
    return clone


def _creature_ids(event: CombatEvent) -> set:
    found = set()
    if event.actor_id is not None:
        found.add(event.actor_id)
    if event.target_id is not None:
        found.add(event.target_id)
    for roll in event.rolls:
        if roll.actor_id is not None:
            found.add(roll.actor_id)
    return found


def _sides(event: CombatEvent) -> set:
    found = set()
    if event.actor_side is not None:
        found.add(event.actor_side)
    if event.target_side is not None:
        found.add(event.target_side)
    for roll in event.rolls:
        if roll.side is not None:
            found.add(roll.side)
    return found


def _kind_ok(event: CombatEvent, kind: str) -> bool:
    if not kind or kind == 'all':
        return True
    if kind == 'hit':
        return event.result in ('hit', 'crit')
    if kind == 'crit':
        return event.result == 'crit'
    if kind == 'miss':
        return event.result in ('miss', 'nat1')
    if kind == 'nat1':
        return event.result == 'nat1'
    if kind == 'death':
        return event.kind == 'death'
    if kind == 'heal':
        return event.kind == 'heal' or event.result == 'heal'
    if kind == 'move':
        return event.kind == 'move'
    if kind == 'legendary':
        return bool(event.legendary) or event.kind == 'legendary'
    if kind == 'initiative':
        return event.kind == 'initiative'
    if kind == 'conditions':
        return event.kind in ('save', 'condition', 'recharge', 'lair')
    return True


def _haystack(event: CombatEvent) -> str:
    chunks = [event.text, event.actor, event.target, event.action, event.reason,
              event.kind, event.result, event.condition, event.save_ability,
              event.advantage]
    for part in event.damages:
        chunks.append(part.damage_type)
        chunks.append(part.note)
    for roll in event.rolls:
        chunks.append(roll.name)
    return ' '.join(str(chunk) for chunk in chunks if chunk)


def event_visible(event, *, side=None, creature_id=None, kind='all', query='') -> bool:
    """Фильтр панели: сторона, существо, тип события, подстрока поиска."""
    if event is None:
        return False
    if side is not None and side not in _sides(event):
        return False
    if creature_id is not None and creature_id not in _creature_ids(event):
        return False
    if not _kind_ok(event, kind):
        return False
    needle = (query or '').strip().casefold()
    if needle and needle not in _haystack(event).casefold():
        return False
    return True


def map_mark(event: CombatEvent, radius_of=None, *, include_moves=False):
    """Один отрезок в координатах клеток или None. Стиль: hit/miss/crit/nat1/heal/move."""

    def radius(creature_id, default=0.45):
        if radius_of is None or creature_id is None:
            return default
        try:
            value = float(radius_of(creature_id))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return default
        return value if value > 0 else default

    if event.kind == 'move':
        if not include_moves or not event.from_pos or not event.to_pos:
            return None
        reach = radius(event.actor_id)
        return _segment(event.from_pos, event.to_pos, reach, reach, 'move')
    if event.kind == 'attack' and event.actor_pos and event.target_pos:
        style = event.result if event.result in ('hit', 'miss', 'crit', 'nat1') else 'hit'
        return _segment(event.actor_pos, event.target_pos,
                        radius(event.actor_id), radius(event.target_id), style)
    if (event.kind == 'heal' and event.action != 'регенерация'
            and event.actor_pos and event.target_pos):
        return _segment(event.actor_pos, event.target_pos,
                        radius(event.actor_id), radius(event.target_id), 'heal')
    return None


def map_marks(events, radius_of=None, *, include_moves=False) -> list:
    marks = []
    for event in events:
        mark = map_mark(event, radius_of, include_moves=include_moves)
        if mark is not None:
            marks.append(mark)
    return marks


def _segment(start, end, r1, r2, style):
    return {
        'x1': start[0], 'y1': start[1],
        'x2': end[0], 'y2': end[1],
        'r1': r1, 'r2': r2,
        'style': style,
    }



==================== conditions.py ====================

"""Состояния 5e и спасброски. Без привязки к интерфейсу.

Натуральные 20 и 1 на спасброске ничего не значат (RAW). Преимущество и помеха
на броске атаки — два d20; если есть и то и другое, они гасятся и кидается
один d20.
"""
import random
import uuid

ABILITY_RU = {
    'str': 'Силы',
    'dex': 'Ловкости',
    'con': 'Телосложения',
    'int': 'Интеллекта',
    'wis': 'Мудрости',
    'cha': 'Харизмы',
}

ABILITY_WORDS = {
    'str': ('сил', 'str'),
    'dex': ('лов', 'dex'),
    'con': ('тел', 'con'),
    'int': ('инт', 'int'),
    'wis': ('мдр', 'муд', 'wis'),
    'cha': ('хар', 'cha'),
}

# Слова из «Иммунитет к состоянию» на dnd.su.
IMMUNITY_WORDS = {
    'blinded': ('ослеп', 'blind'),
    'charmed': ('очарован', 'charm'),
    'deafened': ('оглох', 'глухот', 'deaf'),
    'frightened': ('испуг', 'fright'),
    'grappled': ('схват', 'захват', 'grappl'),
    'incapacitated': ('недееспособ', 'incapac'),
    'invisible': ('невидим', 'invis'),
    'paralyzed': ('паралич', 'paralyz'),
    'petrified': ('окамен', 'petrif'),
    'poisoned': ('отрав', 'poison'),
    'prone': ('сбиван', 'prone'),
    'restrained': ('опутан', 'удержан', 'restrain'),
    'stunned': ('ошелом', 'stun'),
    'unconscious': ('без сознания', 'бессознат', 'unconscious'),
    'exhaustion': ('истощен', 'истощён', 'exhaust'),
    'speed_zero': ('скорость 0',),
}

SPEED_ZERO = frozenset({
    'grappled', 'restrained', 'paralyzed', 'petrified', 'stunned',
    'unconscious', 'speed_zero',
})
INCAPACITATED = frozenset({
    'incapacitated', 'paralyzed', 'petrified', 'stunned', 'unconscious',
})
AUTO_FAIL_STR_DEX = frozenset({
    'paralyzed', 'petrified', 'stunned', 'unconscious',
})
HARD_DISABLE = frozenset({
    'paralyzed', 'petrified', 'stunned', 'unconscious', 'incapacitated',
    'restrained', 'frightened',
})

# id, русское имя, короткая плашка, цвет, прятать ли из ручного меню.
CATALOG = (
    ('blinded', 'ослеплён', 'ОСЛ', '#7f8c8d', False),
    ('charmed', 'очарован', 'ОЧ', '#e84393', False),
    ('deafened', 'оглохший', 'ГЛХ', '#95a5a6', False),
    ('frightened', 'испуган', 'ИСП', '#8e44ad', False),
    ('grappled', 'схвачен', 'СХВ', '#d35400', False),
    ('incapacitated', 'недееспособен', 'НД', '#2c3e50', False),
    ('invisible', 'невидимый', 'НВД', '#16a085', False),
    ('paralyzed', 'парализован', 'ПАР', '#2980b9', False),
    ('petrified', 'окаменевший', 'КАМ', '#7f8c8d', False),
    ('poisoned', 'отравлен', 'ЯД', '#27ae60', False),
    ('prone', 'сбит с ног', 'ЛЕЖ', '#b9770e', False),
    ('restrained', 'опутан', 'ОПУ', '#6e2c00', False),
    ('stunned', 'ошеломлён', 'ОШ', '#1a5276', False),
    ('unconscious', 'без сознания', 'БС', '#1c2833', False),
    ('exhaustion', 'истощение', 'ИС', '#922b21', False),
    # Не состояние PHB: «скорость становится 0» из дыхания и похожих эффектов.
    ('speed_zero', 'скорость 0', '0', '#34495e', True),
)

_BY_ID = {row[0]: row for row in CATALOG}
MANUAL_IDS = tuple(row[0] for row in CATALOG if not row[4] and row[0] != 'exhaustion')


def catalog_row(condition_id):
    return _BY_ID.get(condition_id)


def condition_name(condition_id):
    row = _BY_ID.get(condition_id)
    return row[1] if row else str(condition_id or '')


def condition_short(condition_id, level=None):
    row = _BY_ID.get(condition_id)
    if row is None:
        return '?'
    if condition_id == 'exhaustion' and level:
        return f'{row[2]}{int(level)}'
    return row[2]


def condition_color(condition_id):
    row = _BY_ID.get(condition_id)
    return row[3] if row else '#555555'


def ability_of(label):
    low = str(label or '').strip().lower()
    if not low:
        return None
    for key, words in ABILITY_WORDS.items():
        if any(low.startswith(word) for word in words):
            return key
    return None


def creature_uid(creature):
    if creature is None:
        return None
    uid = getattr(creature, 'uid', None)
    if not uid:
        uid = uuid.uuid4().hex
        try:
            creature.uid = uid
        except (AttributeError, TypeError):
            return None
    return uid


def active_conditions(creature):
    raw = getattr(creature, 'conditions', None) or []
    if not isinstance(raw, list):
        return []
    return raw


def _ids(creature):
    return {item.get('id') for item in active_conditions(creature) if isinstance(item, dict)}


def has_condition(creature, condition_id):
    return condition_id in _ids(creature)


def exhaustion_level(creature):
    for item in active_conditions(creature):
        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get('id') == 'exhaustion':
            try:
                return int(item.get('level') or 0)
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                return 0
    return 0


def is_incapacitated(creature):
    if not getattr(creature, 'alive', True):
        return True
    if _ids(creature) & INCAPACITATED:
        return True
    return exhaustion_level(creature) >= 6


def effective_speed(creature):
    try:
        speed = int(getattr(creature, 'speed', 30) or 0)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        speed = 30
    if _ids(creature) & SPEED_ZERO:
        return 0
    level = exhaustion_level(creature)
    if level >= 5:
        return 0
    if level >= 2:
        speed //= 2
    return max(0, speed)


def effective_max_hp(creature):
    try:
        cap = int(getattr(creature, 'max_hp', 0) or 0)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        cap = 0
    if exhaustion_level(creature) >= 4:
        cap = max(1, cap // 2)
    return cap


def is_condition_immune(creature, condition_id):
    if condition_id == 'poisoned' and has_condition(creature, 'petrified'):
        return True
    blob = ' '.join(str(item).lower() for item in
                    (getattr(creature, 'condition_immunities', None) or []))
    if not blob:
        return False
    for needle in IMMUNITY_WORDS.get(condition_id, ()):
        if needle in blob:
            return True
    return False


def _same_source(cond, creature):
    if creature is None or not isinstance(cond, dict):
        return False
    uid = getattr(creature, 'uid', None)
    if uid and cond.get('source_uid') == uid:
        return True
    name = getattr(creature, 'name', None)
    return bool(name) and cond.get('source_uid') is None and cond.get('source_name') == name


def find_condition(creature, condition_id, source=None):
    for item in active_conditions(creature):
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or item.get('id') != condition_id:
            continue
        if source is not None and not _same_source(item, source):
            continue
        return item
    return None


def permanent_duration():
    return {'kind': 'permanent', 'whose': 'target', 'rounds': None,
            'ability': None, 'dc': None}


def duration_text(duration, condition_id=None):
    duration = duration or {}
    kind = duration.get('kind') or 'permanent'
    rounds = duration.get('rounds')
    ability = ABILITY_RU.get(duration.get('ability') or '', '')
    dc = duration.get('dc')
    if kind == 'repeat_save':
        base = 'повтор спасброска в конце хода'
        if ability and dc:
            base = f'повтор спасброска {ability} Сл {dc} в конце хода'
        if rounds:
            return f'{base}, ещё до {rounds} раунд.'
        return base
    if kind == 'rounds':
        return f'{int(rounds or 0)} раунд.'
    if kind == 'start':
        who = 'источника' if duration.get('whose') == 'source' else 'цели'
        return f'до начала хода {who}'
    if kind == 'end':
        who = 'источника' if duration.get('whose') == 'source' else 'цели'
        return f'до конца хода {who}'
    if condition_id == 'grappled' and duration.get('escape_dc'):
        return f'Сл высвобождения {duration.get("escape_dc")}'
    return 'пока не снято'


def _copy_duration(duration, escape_dc=None):
    base = permanent_duration()
    if isinstance(duration, dict):
        base.update(duration)
    if escape_dc is not None:
        base['escape_dc'] = escape_dc
    return base


def _stackable(condition_id):
    return condition_id in ('frightened', 'charmed', 'grappled')


def apply_condition(target, condition_id, *, source=None, duration=None,
                    level=None, note='', immunity_key=None, escape_dc=None):
    """Вешает состояние. None, если иммунитет или состояние неизвестно.

    Одинаковое состояние от того же источника обновляет длительность.
    Истощение копится уровнем. Без сознания роняет существо (prone),
    если к сбиванию с ног нет иммунитета.
    """
    if target is None or not catalog_row(condition_id):
        return None
    if not getattr(target, 'alive', True) and condition_id != 'unconscious':
        return None
    if is_condition_immune(target, condition_id):
        return None
    if not hasattr(target, 'conditions') or not isinstance(target.conditions, list):
        try:
            target.conditions = []
        except (AttributeError, TypeError):
            return None

    if condition_id == 'exhaustion':
        gain = 1 if level is None else int(level)
        current = find_condition(target, 'exhaustion')
        if current is None:
            current = {
                'id': 'exhaustion',
                'level': 0,
                'source_uid': creature_uid(source),
                'source_name': getattr(source, 'name', None),
                'duration': permanent_duration(),
                'note': note or '',
                'immunity_key': immunity_key,
            }
            target.conditions.append(current)
        current['level'] = max(0, min(6, int(current.get('level') or 0) + gain))
        _after_exhaustion(target)
        return current

    source_uid = creature_uid(source)
    existing = None
    for item in target.conditions:
        if item.get('id') != condition_id:
            continue
        if _stackable(condition_id):
            if source_uid and item.get('source_uid') == source_uid:
                existing = item
                break
            continue
        existing = item
        break
    payload = {
        'id': condition_id,
        'level': None,
        'source_uid': source_uid,
        'source_name': getattr(source, 'name', None),
        'duration': _copy_duration(duration, escape_dc),
        'note': note or '',
        'immunity_key': immunity_key,
    }
    if existing is None:
        target.conditions.append(payload)
        stored = payload
    else:
        existing.update(payload)
        stored = existing
    if condition_id == 'unconscious':
        apply_condition(target, 'prone', source=source,
                        duration=permanent_duration(), note='падает ничком')
    return stored


def remove_condition(target, condition_id=None, *, source=None, cond=None, reason=''):
    """Снимает одно состояние или все с указанным id. Возвращает снятые записи."""
    items = active_conditions(target)
    if not items:
        return []
    removed = []
    kept = []
    for item in items:
        drop = False
        if cond is not None and item is cond:
            drop = True
        elif condition_id and item.get('id') == condition_id:
            if source is None or _same_source(item, source):
                drop = True
        if drop:
            if reason:
                item['removed_because'] = reason
            removed.append(item)
        else:
            kept.append(item)
    if removed:
        target.conditions = kept
    for item in removed:
        key = item.get('immunity_key')
        if key:
            bag = getattr(target, 'save_immunities', None)
            if not isinstance(bag, list):
                try:
                    target.save_immunities = []
                    bag = target.save_immunities
                except (AttributeError, TypeError):
                    bag = None
            if isinstance(bag, list) and key not in bag:
                bag.append(key)
    return removed


def clear_conditions(target):
    removed = list(active_conditions(target))
    if hasattr(target, 'conditions'):
        target.conditions = []
    return removed


def _after_exhaustion(target):
    cap = effective_max_hp(target)
    if getattr(target, 'hp', 0) > cap:
        target.hp = cap
        notify = getattr(target, 'notify', None)
        if notify:
            notify()
    if exhaustion_level(target) >= 6 and getattr(target, 'alive', False):
        target.hp = 0
        target.alive = False
        clear_conditions(target)
        notify = getattr(target, 'notify', None)
        if notify:
            notify()


def set_exhaustion(target, level):
    """Ставит точный уровень истощения. 0 снимает состояние."""
    level = max(0, min(6, int(level or 0)))
    remove_condition(target, 'exhaustion')
    if level <= 0:
        return None
    stored = apply_condition(target, 'exhaustion', level=0)
    if stored is None:
        return None
    stored['level'] = level
    _after_exhaustion(target)
    return stored


def release_grapples_from(source, sides):
    """Схватка спадает, если источник мёртв или недееспособен. Список снятых."""
    if source is None or sides is None:
        return []
    if getattr(source, 'alive', False) and not (_ids(source) & INCAPACITATED):
        return []
    removed = []
    for creature in iter_creatures(sides):
        if creature is source:
            continue
        for item in list(active_conditions(creature)):
            if item.get('id') == 'grappled' and _same_source(item, source):
                gone = remove_condition(
                    creature, cond=item, reason='источник не может держать')
                removed.extend((creature, piece) for piece in gone)
    return removed


def iter_creatures(sides):
    for side in sides or []:
        for creature in side or []:
            yield creature


def auto_fail_save(creature, ability):
    if ability in ('str', 'dex') and (_ids(creature) & AUTO_FAIL_STR_DEX):
        return True
    return False


def save_disadvantage(creature, ability):
    if exhaustion_level(creature) >= 3:
        return True
    if ability == 'dex' and has_condition(creature, 'restrained'):
        return True
    return False


def ability_modifier(creature, ability):
    mods = getattr(creature, 'ability_mods', None) or {}
    if isinstance(mods, dict) and mods.get(ability) is not None:
        try:
            return int(mods[ability])
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            pass
    scores = getattr(creature, 'ability_scores', None) or {}
    raw = scores.get(ability) if isinstance(scores, dict) else None
    if raw is None:
        return 0
    try:
        return (int(raw) - 10) // 2
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0


def save_bonus(creature, ability):
    """Бонус спасброска: число из статблока, если спас броска указан, иначе мод."""
    saves = getattr(creature, 'saves', None) or {}
    if isinstance(saves, dict):
        for key, value in saves.items():
            if ability_of(key) != ability:
                continue
            text = str(value).replace('+', '').replace(' ', '')
            try:
                return int(text)
            except ValueError:
                break
    return ability_modifier(creature, ability)


def legendary_resistance_remaining(creature):
    block = getattr(creature, 'legendary_resistance', None)
    if not isinstance(block, dict):
        return 0
    current = getattr(creature, 'legendary_resistance_remaining', None)
    if current is None:
        current = int(block.get('count') or 0)
        try:
            creature.legendary_resistance_remaining = current
        except (AttributeError, TypeError):
            return int(current)
    try:
        return int(current)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0


def worth_legendary_resistance(creature, *, disabling, damage_fail, damage_success):
    """Простая эвристика: жёсткое состояние или большой урон, если успех реально лучше."""
    try:
        hp = int(getattr(creature, 'hp', 0) or 0)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        hp = 0
    damage_fail = max(0, int(damage_fail or 0))
    damage_success = max(0, int(damage_success or 0))
    if damage_success >= hp > 0 and not disabling:
        return False
    if disabling:
        return True
    if damage_fail <= 0:
        return False
    if hp > 0 and hp - damage_fail <= 0 and damage_success < hp:
        return True
    if damage_fail >= max(10, hp // 2) and damage_success < damage_fail:
        return True
    return False


class SaveRoll:
    """Результат спасброска. success уже учитывает легендарное сопротивление."""

    __slots__ = ('ability', 'natural', 'naturals', 'modifier', 'total', 'dc',
                 'success', 'auto_fail', 'advantage', 'resistance_used',
                 'resistance_left')

    def __init__(self, ability, natural, naturals, modifier, total, dc, success,
                 auto_fail, advantage, resistance_used, resistance_left):
        self.ability = ability
        self.natural = natural
        self.naturals = list(naturals or [])
        self.modifier = modifier
        self.total = total
        self.dc = dc
        self.success = bool(success)
        self.auto_fail = bool(auto_fail)
        self.advantage = advantage
        self.resistance_used = bool(resistance_used)
        self.resistance_left = resistance_left


def _roll_d20(rng, advantage, disadvantage):
    """Пара (кости, режим, взятая). 20 и 1 здесь не особые случаи."""
    if advantage and disadvantage:
        return [rng.randint(1, 20)], 'cancel', None
    if advantage or disadvantage:
        first = rng.randint(1, 20)
        second = rng.randint(1, 20)
        kept = max(first, second) if advantage else min(first, second)
        mode = 'advantage' if advantage else 'disadvantage'
        return [first, second], mode, kept
    natural = rng.randint(1, 20)
    return [natural], 'normal', natural


def saving_throw(creature, ability, dc, *, rng=None, advantage=False,
                 disadvantage=False, allow_legendary=True, disabling=False,
                 damage_fail=0, damage_success=0):
    """d20 + бонус против Сл. Натуральные 20 и 1 не автоуспех и не автопровал."""
    ability = ability_of(ability) or ability or 'dex'
    source = rng or getattr(creature, '_rng', None) or random
    disadvantage = bool(disadvantage) or save_disadvantage(creature, ability)
    auto = auto_fail_save(creature, ability)
    modifier = save_bonus(creature, ability)
    try:
        dc_value = int(dc)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        dc_value = 10
    if auto:
        roll = SaveRoll(ability, None, [], modifier, None, dc_value, False,
                        True, 'auto_fail', False, legendary_resistance_remaining(creature))
    else:
        dice, mode, kept = _roll_d20(source, bool(advantage), disadvantage)
        natural = dice[0] if mode == 'cancel' else kept
        total = int(natural) + int(modifier)
        roll = SaveRoll(ability, natural, dice, modifier, total, dc_value,
                        total >= dc_value, False, mode, False,
                        legendary_resistance_remaining(creature))
    if (not roll.success and allow_legendary
            and legendary_resistance_remaining(creature) > 0
            and worth_legendary_resistance(
                creature, disabling=disabling, damage_fail=damage_fail,
                damage_success=damage_success)):
        creature.legendary_resistance_remaining = legendary_resistance_remaining(creature) - 1
        roll.success = True
        roll.resistance_used = True
        roll.resistance_left = creature.legendary_resistance_remaining
    return roll


def _distance_ft(attacker, target, distance_ft):
    if distance_ft is not None:
        return float(distance_ft)
    from battle_strategies import distance_ft_between
    return float(distance_ft_between(attacker, target))


def attack_modifiers(attacker, target, distance_ft=None):
    """(преимущество, помеха, автокрит в 5 футах) для броска атакующего по цели.

    Любое число источников с каждой стороны схлопывается в один флаг.
    Оба флага сразу означают, что бросок обычный (RAW).
    """
    dist = _distance_ft(attacker, target, distance_ft)
    near = dist <= 5
    attacker_ids = _ids(attacker)
    target_ids = _ids(target)
    advantage = False
    disadvantage = False
    if 'invisible' in attacker_ids:
        advantage = True
    if target_ids & {'blinded', 'paralyzed', 'petrified', 'restrained', 'stunned', 'unconscious'}:
        advantage = True
    if 'prone' in target_ids and near:
        advantage = True
    if 'blinded' in attacker_ids or 'poisoned' in attacker_ids or 'prone' in attacker_ids:
        disadvantage = True
    if 'restrained' in attacker_ids:
        disadvantage = True
    if exhaustion_level(attacker) >= 3:
        disadvantage = True
    if _frightened_disadvantage(attacker, target):
        disadvantage = True
    if 'invisible' in target_ids:
        disadvantage = True
    if 'prone' in target_ids and not near:
        disadvantage = True
    force_crit = near and bool(target_ids & {'paralyzed', 'unconscious'})
    return advantage, disadvantage, force_crit


def _frightened_disadvantage(attacker, target):
    """Помеха на броски атаки, пока существо испугано.

    Линию видимости до источника симулятор не строит. Ослеплённый источник
    страха не видит, поэтому от испуга помехи нет (слепота даёт свою).
    """
    del target
    if has_condition(attacker, 'blinded'):
        return False
    return has_condition(attacker, 'frightened')


def fear_sources(creature, sides):
    found = []
    seen = set()
    for item in active_conditions(creature):
        if item.get('id') != 'frightened':
            continue
        source = _resolve_source(item, sides)
        if source is None or not getattr(source, 'alive', False):
            continue
        key = id(source)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        found.append(source)
    return found


def charm_blocks(attacker, target):
    if target is None:
        return False
    for item in active_conditions(attacker):
        if item.get('id') != 'charmed':
            continue
        if _same_source(item, target):
            return True
    return False


def _resolve_source(cond, sides):
    uid = cond.get('source_uid')
    name = cond.get('source_name')
    for creature in iter_creatures(sides):
        if uid and getattr(creature, 'uid', None) == uid:
            return creature
    if name:
        for creature in iter_creatures(sides):
            if getattr(creature, 'name', None) == name:
                return creature
    return None


def condition_marks(creature):
    parts = []
    for item in active_conditions(creature):
        parts.append(condition_short(item.get('id'), item.get('level')))
    return ' '.join(parts)


def condition_tooltip(creature):
    lines = []
    name = getattr(creature, 'name', '') or 'существо'
    hp = getattr(creature, 'hp', '?')
    max_hp = getattr(creature, 'max_hp', '?')
    lines.append(f'{name}: {hp}/{max_hp} хитов')
    items = active_conditions(creature)
    if not items:
        lines.append('Состояний нет')
        return '\n'.join(lines)
    for item in items:
        label = condition_name(item.get('id'))
        if item.get('id') == 'exhaustion':
            label = f'{label} {item.get("level")}'
        extra = duration_text(item.get('duration'), item.get('id'))
        source = item.get('source_name')
        if source:
            extra = f'{extra}; источник {source}'
        if item.get('duration', {}).get('escape_dc'):
            extra = f'{extra}; Сл высвобождения {item["duration"]["escape_dc"]}'
        lines.append(f'{condition_short(item.get("id"), item.get("level"))} {label} — {extra}')
    speed = effective_speed(creature)
    base = getattr(creature, 'speed', None)
    if base is not None and speed != base:
        lines.append(f'Скорость сейчас {speed} фт (база {base})')
    return '\n'.join(lines)


def describe_applied(target, cond):
    label = condition_name(cond.get('id'))
    if cond.get('id') == 'exhaustion':
        label = f'{label} {cond.get("level")}'
    source = cond.get('source_name')
    how = duration_text(cond.get('duration'), cond.get('id'))
    tail = how
    if source:
        tail = f'{how}, источник {source}'
    return f'{getattr(target, "name", "Цель")}: состояние «{label}» ({tail})'


def describe_removed(target, cond, reason):
    label = condition_name(cond.get('id'))
    if cond.get('id') == 'exhaustion':
        label = f'{label} {cond.get("level")}'
    why = reason or cond.get('removed_because') or 'снято'
    return f'{getattr(target, "name", "Цель")}: состояние «{label}» снято ({why})'


def save_text(creature, roll, *, title=None):
    ability = ABILITY_RU.get(roll.ability, roll.ability or '')
    name = getattr(creature, 'name', 'Существо')
    head = f'{name}: спасбросок {ability}'
    if title:
        head = f'{name}: {title}, спасбросок {ability}'
    if roll.auto_fail:
        body = f'{head} — автоматический провал'
    else:
        dice = ''
        if roll.advantage == 'advantage' and len(roll.naturals) == 2:
            dice = f', преимущество {roll.naturals[0]}/{roll.naturals[1]}'
        elif roll.advantage == 'disadvantage' and len(roll.naturals) == 2:
            dice = f', помеха {roll.naturals[0]}/{roll.naturals[1]}'
        elif roll.advantage == 'cancel':
            dice = ', преимущество и помеха гасятся'
        mod = roll.modifier or 0
        body = (f'{head} {roll.total} против Сл {roll.dc} '
                f'(d20 {roll.natural} {mod:+d}{dice}) — '
                f'{"успех" if roll.success and not roll.resistance_used else "провал"}')
    if roll.resistance_used:
        left = roll.resistance_left
        body = (f'{body}. Легендарное сопротивление: успех вместо провала '
                f'(осталось {left})')
    elif roll.success and not roll.auto_fail:
        pass
    return body


def turn_bounds(creature, sides, moment):
    """Снимает состояния «до начала/конца хода» этого существа. moment: start|end."""
    removed = []
    uid = getattr(creature, 'uid', None)
    why = 'начало хода' if moment == 'start' else 'конец хода'
    for other in iter_creatures(sides):
        for item in list(active_conditions(other)):
            duration = item.get('duration') or {}
            if duration.get('kind') != moment:
                continue
            whose = duration.get('whose') or 'target'
            if whose == 'target' and other is not creature:
                continue
            if whose == 'source' and (not uid or item.get('source_uid') != uid):
                continue
            removed.append((other, item))
            remove_condition(other, cond=item, reason=why)
    return removed


def tick_rounds_and_saves(creature, sides, round_no=None):
    """Конец хода цели: повторный спасбросок, затем минус раунд.

    Возвращает список (событие-словарь) для журнала: kind, text и поля броска.
    """
    notes = []
    for item in list(active_conditions(creature)):
        duration = item.get('duration') or {}
        if duration.get('kind') != 'repeat_save':
            continue
        if not getattr(creature, 'alive', False):
            break
        ability = duration.get('ability') or 'wis'
        dc = duration.get('dc') or 10
        disabling = item.get('id') in HARD_DISABLE
        roll = saving_throw(creature, ability, dc, disabling=disabling)
        success = roll.success
