        return NotImplemented

    def __repr__(self):
        return (f'AttackResult(hit={self.hit}, damage={self.damage}, total={self.total}, '
                f'critical={self.critical}, info={self.info!r}, natural={self.natural}, '
                f'modifier={self.modifier}, naturals={self.naturals}, '
                f'advantage={self.advantage})')


class Enemy(Subject):
    """Существо в битве. rng — внедряемая зависимость (DI): None => модульный random."""

    def __init__(self, name: str, hp: int, ac: int, attacks: List[Attack], side: int,
                 regen=0, speed=30, rng=None):
        super().__init__()
        self.name = name
        self.max_hp = hp
        self.hp = hp
        self.ac = ac
        self.attacks = attacks
        self.side = side
        self.alive = True
        self.ignore_sides = set()
        self.strategy: BattleStrategy = RandomStrategy()
        self.regen = regen
        self.speed = speed
        self.pos = None
        self.moved_ft = 0
        self.multiattack = None
        self.ability_scores = {}
        self.ability_mods = {}
        self.saves = {}
        self.skills = {}
        self.senses = {}
        self.vulnerabilities = []
        self.resistances = []
        self.immunities = []
        self.condition_immunities = []
        self.languages = []
        self.cr = None
        self.prof_bonus = None
        self.size = None
        self.type = None
        self.traits = []
        self.legendary_actions = None
        self.legendary_resistance = None
        self.lair_actions = None
        self.special_actions = []
        self.bonus_actions = []
        self.reactions = []
        self.legendary_remaining = None
        self.legendary_resistance_remaining = None
        self.conditions = []
        self.recharge_state = {}
        self.save_immunities = []
        self.lair_index = 0
        self.lair_warned = False
        self._rng = rng if rng is not None else random

    def take_damage(self, damage: int):
        actual_damage = max(0, damage)
        previous_hp = self.hp
        self.hp -= actual_damage
        if self.hp <= 0:
            self.alive = False
            self.hp = 0
            from conditions import clear_conditions
            clear_conditions(self)
            self.notify()
            return False
        elif self.hp != previous_hp:
            self.notify()
        return self.alive

    def perform_attack(self, attack: Attack, target_ac: int,
                       crit_mode: str = 'double_all', *, advantage=False,
                       disadvantage=False, force_crit=False) -> Tuple[bool, int, int, bool, str]:
        """Бросок атаки. crit_mode:
        'double_all' (по умолчанию, так играет стол) — крит удваивает весь урон,
        включая модификатор; 'dice_only' — кости дважды, модификатор один раз.
        Натуральная 1 всегда промах, натуральная 20 всегда попадание.
        Преимущество и помеха кидают два d20; вместе они гасятся в один бросок.
        force_crit — попадание в упор по парализованному или без сознания
        становится критом, даже если кость не 20.

        Возврат по-прежнему распаковывается в 5 значений. Натуральная кость
        и модификатор лежат в полях natural / modifier (AttackResult).
        Кости преимущества — в naturals, режим — в advantage.
        """
        from conditions import _roll_d20
        dice, mode, kept = _roll_d20(self._rng, bool(advantage), bool(disadvantage))
        natural = dice[0] if mode == 'cancel' else kept
        modifier = attack.attack_bonus
        total_attack = natural + modifier
        critical = (natural == 20)
        if natural == 1:
            hit = False
        else:
            hit = critical or total_attack >= target_ac
        if hit and force_crit:
            critical = True
        damage_info = ""
        damage = 0
        if hit:
            dice_type = int(attack.damage_dice_type.split('d')[1])
            rolls = attack.damage_dice_count
            if critical and crit_mode == 'dice_only':
                rolls *= 2
            damage = sum(self._rng.randint(1, dice_type)
                         for _ in range(rolls)) + attack.damage_modifier
            if critical and crit_mode != 'dice_only':
                damage *= 2
            damage = max(0, damage)
            damage_info = f"{damage} урона"
            if attack.damage_type:
                damage_info += f" ({attack.damage_type})"
            if getattr(attack, 'magical', False):
                damage_info += " ✦маг."
        return AttackResult(hit, damage, total_attack, critical, damage_info,
                            natural, modifier, dice, mode)

    def perform_heal(self, attack: Attack) -> int:
        """Сколько хитов восстанавливает лечение: кости + модификатор, без броска атаки."""
        dice_type = int(str(attack.damage_dice_type).split('d')[-1])
        count = max(0, int(attack.damage_dice_count))
        rolled = sum(self._rng.randint(1, dice_type) for _ in range(count))
        return max(0, rolled + int(attack.damage_modifier))

    def heal(self, amount: int) -> int:
        """Лечит живое существо, не выше max_hp. Возвращает фактически полученное."""
        if not self.alive:
            return 0
        from conditions import effective_max_hp
        amount = max(0, int(amount))
        before = self.hp
        self.hp = min(effective_max_hp(self), self.hp + amount)
        gained = self.hp - before
        if gained:
            self.notify()
        return gained

    def regenerate(self):
        if self.alive and self.regen > 0:
            prev_hp = self.hp
            self.hp = min(self.max_hp, self.hp + self.regen)
            if self.hp != prev_hp:
                self.notify()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            'name': self.name, 'max_hp': self.max_hp, 'hp': self.hp, 'ac': self.ac,
            'attacks': [a.to_dict() for a in self.attacks],
            'side': self.side, 'alive': self.alive,
            'ignore_sides': list(self.ignore_sides),
            'strategy_type': self.strategy.__class__.__name__,
            'regen': self.regen, 'speed': self.speed, 'pos': self.pos,
            'multiattack': self.multiattack,
            'ability_scores': self.ability_scores, 'ability_mods': self.ability_mods,
            'saves': self.saves, 'skills': self.skills, 'senses': self.senses,
            'vulnerabilities': self.vulnerabilities, 'resistances': self.resistances,
            'immunities': self.immunities, 'condition_immunities': self.condition_immunities,
            'languages': self.languages, 'cr': self.cr, 'prof_bonus': self.prof_bonus,
            'size': self.size, 'type': self.type, 'traits': self.traits,
            'legendary_actions': self.legendary_actions,
            'legendary_resistance': self.legendary_resistance,
            'lair_actions': self.lair_actions,
            'special_actions': self.special_actions,
            'bonus_actions': self.bonus_actions,
            'reactions': self.reactions,
            'legendary_remaining': self.legendary_remaining,
            'legendary_resistance_remaining': self.legendary_resistance_remaining,
            'conditions': [dict(item) for item in (self.conditions or [])],
            'recharge_state': {key: dict(value) for key, value in
                               (self.recharge_state or {}).items()},
            'save_immunities': list(self.save_immunities or []),
            'lair_index': int(self.lair_index or 0),
            'lair_warned': bool(self.lair_warned),
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        enemy = cls(data['name'], data['max_hp'], data['ac'],
                    [Attack.from_dict(a) for a in data['attacks']],
                    data['side'], data.get('regen', 0))
        enemy.hp = data['hp']
        enemy.alive = data['alive']
        enemy.ignore_sides = set(data['ignore_sides'])
        enemy.speed = data.get('speed', 30)
        enemy.pos = data.get('pos')
        enemy.multiattack = data.get('multiattack')
        enemy.ability_scores = dict(data.get('ability_scores') or {})
        enemy.ability_mods = dict(data.get('ability_mods') or {})
        enemy.saves = dict(data.get('saves') or {})
        enemy.skills = dict(data.get('skills') or {})
        enemy.senses = dict(data.get('senses') or {})
        enemy.vulnerabilities = list(data.get('vulnerabilities') or [])
        enemy.resistances = list(data.get('resistances') or [])
        enemy.immunities = list(data.get('immunities') or [])
        enemy.condition_immunities = list(data.get('condition_immunities') or [])
        enemy.languages = list(data.get('languages') or [])
        enemy.cr = data.get('cr')
        enemy.prof_bonus = data.get('prof_bonus')
        enemy.size = data.get('size')
        enemy.type = data.get('type')
        enemy.traits = [dict(tr) for tr in (data.get('traits') or [])]
        enemy.legendary_actions = data.get('legendary_actions')
        enemy.legendary_resistance = data.get('legendary_resistance')
        enemy.lair_actions = data.get('lair_actions')
        enemy.special_actions = list(data.get('special_actions') or [])
        enemy.bonus_actions = list(data.get('bonus_actions') or [])
        enemy.reactions = list(data.get('reactions') or [])
        enemy.legendary_remaining = data.get('legendary_remaining')
        enemy.legendary_resistance_remaining = data.get('legendary_resistance_remaining')
        enemy.conditions = [dict(item) for item in (data.get('conditions') or [])]
        enemy.recharge_state = {
            key: dict(value) for key, value in (data.get('recharge_state') or {}).items()
        }
        enemy.save_immunities = list(data.get('save_immunities') or [])
        enemy.lair_index = int(data.get('lair_index') or 0)
        enemy.lair_warned = bool(data.get('lair_warned') or False)

        strategy_type = data.get('strategy_type', 'RandomStrategy')
        from battle_strategies import (AggressiveStrategy, DefensiveStrategy,
                                       RandomStrategy, SupportStrategy)
        enemy.strategy = {
            'AggressiveStrategy': AggressiveStrategy,
            'DefensiveStrategy': DefensiveStrategy,
            'SupportStrategy': SupportStrategy,
        }.get(strategy_type, RandomStrategy)()
        return enemy


==================== factory.py ====================

import logging

from entities import Enemy, Attack

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _creature_def(hp, ac, attacks, speed=30, regen=0, multiattack=None, *,
                  ability_scores=None, ability_mods=None, saves=None, skills=None,
                  senses=None, vulnerabilities=None, resistances=None, immunities=None,
                  condition_immunities=None, languages=None, cr=None, prof_bonus=None,
                  size=None, ctype=None, traits=None):
    """Конструктор записи реестра с дефолтами по схеме (чтобы не писать {} / [] везде).
    Боевые поля обязательны; справочные опциональны — отсутствие = пустой контейнер/None."""
    return {
        'hp': hp, 'ac': ac, 'attacks': attacks, 'speed': speed, 'regen': regen,
        'multiattack': multiattack,
        'ability_scores': ability_scores or {}, 'ability_mods': ability_mods or {},
        'saves': saves or {}, 'skills': skills or {}, 'senses': senses or {},
        'vulnerabilities': vulnerabilities or [], 'resistances': resistances or [],
        'immunities': immunities or [], 'condition_immunities': condition_immunities or [],
        'languages': languages or [], 'cr': cr, 'prof_bonus': prof_bonus,
        'size': size, 'type': ctype, 'traits': traits or [],
    }


class EnemyFactory:
    """Фабрика для создания врагов разных типов"""
    CUSTOM_TYPES = {}    # импортированные/кастомные
    CREATURE_DEFS = {}   # канонические встроенные типы

                     {'name': 'Огненное дыхание', 'attack_bonus': 0, 'damage_dice_count': 3,
                      'damage_dice_type': 'd6', 'damage_modifier': 0, 'damage_type': 'огневой',
                      'range_ft': 15}],
            ability_scores={'str': 14, 'dex': 12, 'con': 14, 'int': 6, 'wis': 10, 'cha': 10},
            ability_mods={'str': 2, 'dex': 1, 'con': 2, 'int': -2, 'wis': 0, 'cha': 0},
            senses={'Тёмное зрение': '60', 'Пассивное восприятие': '10'},
            languages=[], cr='1', prof_bonus='+2', size='Средний', ctype='дракон'),
    }

    @staticmethod
    def register_type(type_name, hp, ac, attacks, speed=30, regen=0, multiattack=None, **extra):
        EnemyFactory.CUSTOM_TYPES[type_name] = {
            'hp': hp, 'ac': ac, 'attacks': attacks,
            'speed': speed, 'regen': regen,
            'multiattack': multiattack,
            **extra,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _make_attack(data):
        """Создаёт Attack из словаря, аккуратно вынимая range_ft"""
        data = dict(data)
        range_ft = data.pop('range_ft', 5)
        attack = Attack(**data)
        attack.range_ft = range_ft
        return attack

    @staticmethod
    def _finish(enemy, speed=30, ranges=()):
        """Гарантирует speed; range_ft уже проставлен _make_attack из dict реестра,
        поэтому ranges здесь больше не нужен (оставлен для совместимости сигнатуры)."""
        enemy.speed = speed
        for idx, attack in enumerate(enemy.attacks):
            attack.range_ft = ranges[idx] if idx < len(ranges) else getattr(attack, 'range_ft', 5)
        return enemy

    @staticmethod
    def _apply_def(enemy, t):
        """МОСТ между схемой реестра и объектом Enemy: проставляет ВСЕ справочные поля
        с дефолтами. Единая точка для CREATURE_DEFS и CUSTOM_TYPES — поэтому парсер
        (этап 1) и форма (этап 3), наполняющие dict, подхватятся без правок фабрики."""
        enemy.ability_scores = dict(t.get('ability_scores') or {})
        enemy.ability_mods = dict(t.get('ability_mods') or {})
        enemy.saves = dict(t.get('saves') or {})
        enemy.skills = dict(t.get('skills') or {})
        enemy.senses = dict(t.get('senses') or {})
        enemy.vulnerabilities = list(t.get('vulnerabilities') or [])
        enemy.resistances = list(t.get('resistances') or [])
        enemy.immunities = list(t.get('immunities') or [])
        enemy.condition_immunities = list(t.get('condition_immunities') or [])
        enemy.languages = list(t.get('languages') or [])
        enemy.cr = t.get('cr')
        enemy.prof_bonus = t.get('prof_bonus')
        enemy.size = t.get('size')
        enemy.type = t.get('type')
        enemy.traits = [dict(tr) for tr in (t.get('traits') or [])]
        enemy.legendary_actions = t.get('legendary_actions')
        enemy.legendary_resistance = t.get('legendary_resistance')
        enemy.lair_actions = t.get('lair_actions')
        enemy.special_actions = list(t.get('special_actions') or [])
        enemy.bonus_actions = list(t.get('bonus_actions') or [])
        enemy.reactions = list(t.get('reactions') or [])
        return enemy

    @staticmethod
    def create_enemy(enemy_type, name, side, registry=None):
        reg = registry if registry is not None else PRODUCTION_REGISTRY
        t = reg.lookup(enemy_type)
        enemy = Enemy(name=name, hp=t['hp'], ac=t['ac'],
                      attacks=[EnemyFactory._make_attack(a) for a in t['attacks']],
                      side=side, regen=t.get('regen', 0))
        enemy = EnemyFactory._finish(enemy, speed=t.get('speed', 30))
        enemy.multiattack = t.get('multiattack')
        EnemyFactory._apply_def(enemy, t)
        return enemy


class CreatureRegistry:
    def __init__(self, defs=None, custom=None):
        self.defs = defs if defs is not None else {}
        self.custom = custom if custom is not None else {}

    def lookup(self, type_name):
        if type_name in self.custom:
            return self.custom[type_name]
        if type_name in self.defs:
            return self.defs[type_name]
        logger.warning(
            "Неизвестный тип существа %r — используется «обычный»", type_name)
        return self.defs.get('обычный')

    def register(self, type_name, **data):
        self.custom[type_name] = data

PRODUCTION_REGISTRY = CreatureRegistry(
    defs=EnemyFactory.CREATURE_DEFS,
    custom=EnemyFactory.CUSTOM_TYPES,
)


==================== main.py ====================

"""Точка входа: приложение на Qt.

faulthandler включён: при жёстком вылете на уровне C++ (access violation,
fail-fast) в консоль печатается Python-стек — код выхода больше не будет
единственной уликой.
"""
import faulthandler
import sys
import traceback

faulthandler.enable()


def _excepthook(kind, value, tb):
    traceback.print_exception(kind, value, tb)


sys.excepthook = _excepthook


def main():
    from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    from qt_async import install
    from qt_app import MainWindow
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    install(app)
    win = MainWindow()
    win.showMaximized()
    sys.exit(app.exec())


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()



==================== monte_carlo.py ====================

"""Пакетная симуляция боя: много независимых прогонов одной сцены.

Каждый бой начинается с глубокой копии стартового снимка (хиты, состояния,
перезарядка, легендарные действия и сопротивление, карта). Сид боя зависит
только от базового сида и номера, поэтому серия воспроизводится и в нескольких
процессах. Журнал не собирается: раунд идёт с quiet=True.

Запуск без интерфейса: python -m monte_carlo -o бой.csv
"""
import argparse
import copy
import csv
import json
import math
import multiprocessing
import os
import random
import signal
import statistics
import sys
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from battle_history import capture_battle
from battle_map_model import BattleMap
from battle_round import run_round
from battle_strategies import (AggressiveStrategy, DefensiveStrategy,
                               RandomStrategy, SupportStrategy)
from combat_core import normalize_crit_mode
from decision_engine import DecisionEngine
from entities import Enemy
from factory import EnemyFactory
from rng_channel import bound

_STRATS = {
    'случайная': RandomStrategy,
    'агрессивная': AggressiveStrategy,
    'защитная': DefensiveStrategy,
    'поддержка': SupportStrategy,
}

_OUTCOME_RU = {
    'victory': 'победа',
    'draw': 'ничья',
    'timeout': 'таймаут',
}

_WORKER = None


class _Scene:
    """То, что capture_battle читает у окна. Без Qt."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.sides = []
        self.side_names = []
        self.ignore_sides = {}
        self.battle_map = None
        self.engine = DecisionEngine()
        self.round_no = 0
        self.battle_events = []
        self.alliance_settings = []
        self.selected = None
        self.setup = []


@dataclass
class CreatureEnd:
    uid: str
    name: str
    side_index: int
    side_name: str
    hp: int
    alive: bool
    dealt: int
    taken: int
    resistance_remaining: int | None
    recharge_ready: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class BattleRow:
    number: int
    seed: int
    winner: str
    winner_index: int
    rounds: int
    outcome: str
    creatures: list


@dataclass
class RateStat:
    name: str
    count: int
    rate: float
    ci_low: float
    ci_high: float


@dataclass
class CreatureStat:
    uid: str
    name: str
    side_name: str
    side_index: int
    survival: float
    mean_hp: float
    mean_hp_alive: float | None
    mean_dealt: float
    mean_taken: float


@dataclass
class BatchReport:
    battles_requested: int
    battles_done: int
    seed: int
    max_rounds: int
    workers: int
    cancelled: bool
    sides: list
    draw: RateStat
    timeout: RateStat
    mean_rounds: float
    median_rounds: float
    round_histogram: list
    creatures: list
    battles: list


def derive_seed(base_seed, index):
    """Независимый сид боя. Хватает 31 бита, чтобы Excel не округлял номер.

    Строка, а не кортеж: в 3.12 Random принимает только int/float/str/bytes.
    """
    mixer = random.Random(f'{int(base_seed)}:{int(index)}')
    return mixer.randrange(1, 2 ** 31 - 1)


def wilson(successes, total, z=1.96):
    """95% интервал Уилсона для доли. При нуле боёв — (0, 0)."""
    successes = int(successes)
    total = int(total)
    if total <= 0:
        return 0.0, 0.0
    successes = min(total, max(0, successes))
    if successes == 0:
        low_bound = 0.0
    else:
        low_bound = None
    if successes == total:
        high_bound = 1.0
    else:
        high_bound = None
    proportion = successes / total
    z2 = z * z
    denom = 1.0 + z2 / total
    centre = (proportion + z2 / (2.0 * total)) / denom
    margin = z * math.sqrt((proportion * (1.0 - proportion) + z2 / (4.0 * total)) / total) / denom
    low = 0.0 if low_bound is not None else max(0.0, centre - margin)
    high = 1.0 if high_bound is not None else min(1.0, centre + margin)
    return low, high


def creature_uid(data, side_index, index):
    if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get('uid'):
        return str(data['uid'])
    return f'{side_index}:{index}'


def side_names_of(snapshot):
    names = [str(name) for name in (snapshot.get('side_names') or [])]
    count = len(snapshot.get('sides') or [])
    while len(names) < count:
        names.append(f'Сторона {len(names) + 1}')
    return names[:count] if count else names


def roster_of(snapshot):
    names = side_names_of(snapshot)
    roster = []
    for side_index, row in enumerate(snapshot.get('sides') or []):
        side_name = names[side_index]
        for index, data in enumerate(row or []):
            data = data or {}
            roster.append({
                'uid': creature_uid(data, side_index, index),
                'name': str(data.get('name') or f'{side_name}_{index + 1}'),
                'side_index': side_index,
                'side_name': side_name,
            })
    return roster


def _is_cancel(cancel):
    if cancel is None:
        return False
    is_set = getattr(cancel, 'is_set', None)
    if callable(is_set):
        return bool(is_set())
    if callable(cancel):
        return bool(cancel())
    return bool(cancel)


def _rate(name, count, total):
    low, high = wilson(count, total)
    rate = (count / total) if total else 0.0
    return RateStat(name, int(count), rate, low, high)


def aggregate(rows, roster, *, requested, seed, max_rounds, workers, cancelled,
              side_names):
    """Сводка по уже посчитанным боям. rows можно передать в любом порядке."""
    ordered = sorted(rows, key=lambda row: row.number)
    done = len(ordered)
    names = list(side_names)
    wins = [0] * len(names)
    draws = 0
    timeouts = 0
    for row in ordered:
        if row.outcome == 'victory' and 0 <= row.winner_index < len(wins):
            wins[row.winner_index] += 1
        elif row.outcome == 'timeout':
            timeouts += 1
        else:
            draws += 1
    rounds = [row.rounds for row in ordered]
    counts = {}
    for value in rounds:
        counts[value] = counts.get(value, 0) + 1
    if counts:
        histogram = [(number, counts.get(number, 0))
                     for number in range(min(counts), max(counts) + 1)]
    else:
        histogram = []
    by_uid = {}
    for row in ordered:
        for creature in row.creatures:
            by_uid.setdefault(creature.uid, []).append(creature)
    creatures = []
    for item in roster:
        sample = by_uid.get(item['uid'], [])
        if not sample or done == 0:
            creatures.append(CreatureStat(
                item['uid'], item['name'], item['side_name'], item['side_index'],
                0.0, 0.0, None, 0.0, 0.0))
            continue
        alive = [creature for creature in sample if creature.alive]
        mean_alive = (statistics.fmean(creature.hp for creature in alive)
                      if alive else None)
        creatures.append(CreatureStat(
            item['uid'], item['name'], item['side_name'], item['side_index'],
            len(alive) / done,
            statistics.fmean(creature.hp for creature in sample),
            None if mean_alive is None else float(mean_alive),
            statistics.fmean(creature.dealt for creature in sample),
            statistics.fmean(creature.taken for creature in sample),
        ))
    return BatchReport(
        battles_requested=int(requested),
        battles_done=done,
        seed=int(seed),
        max_rounds=int(max_rounds),
        workers=int(workers),
        cancelled=bool(cancelled),
        sides=[_rate(name, wins[index], done) for index, name in enumerate(names)],
        draw=_rate('ничья', draws, done),
        timeout=_rate('таймаут', timeouts, done),
        mean_rounds=float(statistics.fmean(rounds)) if rounds else 0.0,
        median_rounds=float(statistics.median(rounds)) if rounds else 0.0,
        round_histogram=histogram,
        creatures=creatures,
        battles=ordered,
    )


def _progress_payload(rows, total, side_names):
    wins = [0] * len(side_names)
    draws = 0
    timeouts = 0
    for row in rows:
        if row.outcome == 'victory' and 0 <= row.winner_index < len(wins):
            wins[row.winner_index] += 1
        elif row.outcome == 'timeout':
            timeouts += 1
        else:
            draws += 1
    return {
        'done': len(rows),
        'total': int(total),
        'wins': wins,
        'side_names': list(side_names),
        'draws': draws,
        'timeouts': timeouts,
    }


def spawn_scene(snapshot, rng):
    """Новые существа и новая карта. Снимок не меняется."""
    names = side_names_of(snapshot)
    sides = []
    for side_index, row in enumerate(snapshot.get('sides') or []):
        side = []
        for index, data in enumerate(row or []):
            payload = copy.deepcopy(data)
            enemy = Enemy.from_dict(payload)
            enemy.uid = creature_uid(data, side_index, index)
            enemy.moved_ft = payload.get('moved_ft') or 0
            enemy._rng = rng
            enemy._observers = []
            side.append(enemy)
        sides.append(side)
    ignore = {}
    for key, value in (snapshot.get('ignore_sides') or {}).items():
        ignore[int(key)] = {int(item) for item in value}
    battle_map = None
    map_state = snapshot.get('map')
    if map_state:
        battle_map = BattleMap(int(map_state['width']), int(map_state['height']))
        positions = map_state.get('positions') or {}
        for side in sides:
            for enemy in side:
                pos = positions.get(enemy.uid)
                if pos is None:
                    continue
                battle_map.place(enemy, pos[0], pos[1])
    return sides, names, ignore, battle_map


def _add_damage(events, id_to_uid, dealt, taken):
    for event in events:
        total = 0
        for part in getattr(event, 'damages', None) or []:
            try:
                total += int(getattr(part, 'final', 0) or 0)
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                continue
        if total <= 0:
            continue
        actor = id_to_uid.get(getattr(event, 'actor_id', None))
        target = id_to_uid.get(getattr(event, 'target_id', None))
        if actor is not None:
            dealt[actor] = dealt.get(actor, 0) + total
        if target is not None:
            taken[target] = taken.get(target, 0) + total


def _living_sides(sides):
    return [index for index, side in enumerate(sides)
            if any(getattr(enemy, 'alive', False) for enemy in side)]


def _resistance_left(enemy):
    block = getattr(enemy, 'legendary_resistance', None)
    if not isinstance(block, dict):
        return None
    current = getattr(enemy, 'legendary_resistance_remaining', None)
    if current is None:
        return None
    try:
        return int(current)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def _recharge_ready(enemy):
    state = getattr(enemy, 'recharge_state', None) or {}
    ready = {}
    for name, slot in state.items():
        if isinstance(slot, dict):
            ready[str(name)] = bool(slot.get('ready', True))
    return ready


def run_one(snapshot, *, seed, number=1, max_rounds=40, auto_move=True,
            crit_mode='double_all', quiet=True):
    """Один бой. seed — уже выведенный сид этого боя, не базовый."""
    if int(max_rounds) < 1:
        raise ValueError('лимит раундов должен быть не меньше 1')
    rng = random.Random(int(seed))
    crit_mode = normalize_crit_mode(crit_mode)
    dealt = {}
    taken = {}
    rounds = 0
    outcome = 'timeout'
    winner_index = -1
    with bound(rng):
        sides, names, ignore, battle_map = spawn_scene(snapshot, rng)
        id_to_uid = {}
        roster = []
        for side_index, side in enumerate(sides):
            side_name = names[side_index] if side_index < len(names) else f'Сторона {side_index + 1}'
            for enemy in side:
                id_to_uid[id(enemy)] = enemy.uid
                roster.append((side_index, side_name, enemy))
        engine = DecisionEngine()
        engine.explain = False
        while rounds < int(max_rounds):
            result = run_round(
                sides, names, ignore, battle_map, engine,
                auto_move=bool(auto_move), crit_mode=crit_mode, rng=rng,
                round_no=rounds + 1, quiet=bool(quiet))
            if result.outcome == 'empty':
                outcome = 'draw'
                break
            rounds += 1
            _add_damage(result.events, id_to_uid, dealt, taken)
            living = _living_sides(sides)
            if len(living) <= 1:
                if len(living) == 1:
                    winner_index = living[0]
                    outcome = 'victory'
                else:
                    winner_index = -1
                    outcome = 'draw'
                break
        else:
            living = _living_sides(sides)
            if len(living) == 1:
                winner_index = living[0]
                outcome = 'victory'
            elif len(living) == 0:
                winner_index = -1
                outcome = 'draw'
            else:
                winner_index = -1
                outcome = 'timeout'
        creatures = []
        for side_index, side_name, enemy in roster:
            creatures.append(CreatureEnd(
                uid=enemy.uid,
                name=enemy.name,
                side_index=side_index,
                side_name=side_name,
                hp=int(getattr(enemy, 'hp', 0) or 0),
                alive=bool(getattr(enemy, 'alive', False)),
                dealt=int(dealt.get(enemy.uid, 0)),
                taken=int(taken.get(enemy.uid, 0)),
                resistance_remaining=_resistance_left(enemy),
                recharge_ready=_recharge_ready(enemy),
            ))
    winner = names[winner_index] if 0 <= winner_index < len(names) else ''
    return BattleRow(int(number), int(seed), winner, winner_index, rounds, outcome, creatures)


def _init_worker(snapshot, max_rounds, auto_move, crit_mode):
    global _WORKER
    _WORKER = (snapshot, int(max_rounds), bool(auto_move), crit_mode)


def _chunk_task(jobs):
    snapshot, max_rounds, auto_move, crit_mode = _WORKER
    return [
        run_one(snapshot, seed=seed, number=number, max_rounds=max_rounds,
                auto_move=auto_move, crit_mode=crit_mode)
        for number, seed in jobs
    ]


def _chunk_size(total, workers):
    pieces = max(1, int(workers) * 4)
    return max(1, min(10, total // pieces or 1))


def _slim(snapshot):
    data = dict(snapshot)
    data['events'] = []
    data['engine_log'] = []
    return data


def _run_parallel(snapshot, jobs, workers, max_rounds, auto_move, crit_mode,
                  cancel, on_rows):
    context = multiprocessing.get_context('spawn')
    size = _chunk_size(len(jobs), workers)
    chunks = [jobs[index:index + size] for index in range(0, len(jobs), size)]
    rows = []
    cancelled = False
    pool = context.Pool(
        processes=int(workers),
        initializer=_init_worker,
        initargs=(_slim(snapshot), int(max_rounds), bool(auto_move),
                  normalize_crit_mode(crit_mode)),
    )
    try:
        iterator = pool.imap_unordered(_chunk_task, chunks)
        pending = len(chunks)
        while pending:
            if _is_cancel(cancel):
                cancelled = True
                break
            try:
                part = iterator.next(0.2)
            except (TimeoutError, multiprocessing.TimeoutError):
                continue
            except StopIteration:
                break
            pending -= 1
            rows.extend(part)
            on_rows(rows)
        if _is_cancel(cancel):
            cancelled = True
    finally:
        pool.terminate()
        pool.join()
    return rows, cancelled


def run_batch(snapshot, *, battles=1000, seed=1, max_rounds=40, auto_move=True,
              crit_mode='double_all', workers=1, progress=None, cancel=None):
    """N боёв. progress(dict) зовётся из того же потока, что и run_batch."""
    battles = int(battles)
    max_rounds = int(max_rounds)
    if battles < 1:
        raise ValueError('число боёв должно быть не меньше 1')
    if max_rounds < 1:
        raise ValueError('лимит раундов должен быть не меньше 1')
    if not snapshot or not (snapshot.get('sides') or []):
        raise ValueError('в сцене нет сторон')
    if workers is None or int(workers) < 1:
        workers = os.cpu_count() or 1
    workers = int(workers)
    names = side_names_of(snapshot)
    roster = roster_of(snapshot)
    jobs = [(index + 1, derive_seed(seed, index)) for index in range(battles)]
    stride = 1 if battles <= 400 else 10
    rows = []
    cancelled = False

    def emit(force=False):
        if progress is None:
            return
        done = len(rows)
        if force or done == 1 or done == battles or done % stride == 0:
            progress(_progress_payload(rows, battles, names))

    if _is_cancel(cancel):
        cancelled = True
    elif workers == 1:
        for number, battle_seed in jobs:
            if _is_cancel(cancel):
                cancelled = True
                break
            rows.append(run_one(
                snapshot, seed=battle_seed, number=number, max_rounds=max_rounds,
                auto_move=auto_move, crit_mode=crit_mode))
            emit()
    else:
        def on_rows(current):
            rows[:] = current
            emit()

        parallel_rows, cancelled = _run_parallel(
            snapshot, jobs, workers, max_rounds, auto_move, crit_mode, cancel, on_rows)
        rows = parallel_rows
    if progress is not None:
        progress(_progress_payload(rows, battles, names))
    return aggregate(
        rows, roster, requested=battles, seed=seed, max_rounds=max_rounds,
        workers=workers, cancelled=cancelled, side_names=names)


def make_scene(groups, ignore_sides=None):
    """Снимок из списка {'name', 'type', 'count', 'strategy'}."""
    scene = _Scene()
    for index, group in enumerate(groups):
        name = str(group.get('name') or f'Сторона {chr(65 + index)}')
        kind = str(group.get('type') or 'обычный')
        count = int(group.get('count') if group.get('count') is not None else 1)
        strategy_name = str(group.get('strategy') or 'случайная')
        strategy_cls = _STRATS.get(strategy_name, RandomStrategy)
        scene.side_names.append(name)
        side = []
        for number in range(max(0, count)):
            enemy = EnemyFactory.create_enemy(kind, f'{name}_{number + 1}', index)
            enemy.strategy = strategy_cls()
            side.append(enemy)
        scene.sides.append(side)
        scene.setup.append({
            'name': name, 'type': kind, 'strategy': strategy_name, 'count': count,
        })
    if scene.sides:
        width = max(24, 4 * len(scene.sides))
        zone = max(1, width // len(scene.sides))
        tallest = max((len(side) for side in scene.sides), default=0)
        height = max(16, (tallest + zone - 1) // zone + 2)
        scene.battle_map = BattleMap(width, height)
        scene.battle_map.formation_placement(scene.sides)
    if ignore_sides:
        parsed = {int(key): {int(item) for item in value} for key, value in ignore_sides.items()}
        scene.ignore_sides = parsed
        for side_index, banned in parsed.items():
            if side_index < len(scene.sides):
                for enemy in scene.sides[side_index]:
                    enemy.ignore_sides.update(banned)
    return capture_battle(scene)


def demo_snapshot():
    """Типичная сцена: четыре орка против четырёх гоблинов, оба отряда агрессивны."""
    return make_scene([
        {'name': 'Орки', 'type': 'орк', 'count': 4, 'strategy': 'агрессивная'},
        {'name': 'Гоблины', 'type': 'гоблин', 'count': 4, 'strategy': 'агрессивная'},
    ])


def snapshot_from_sides(sides, side_names, battle_map=None, ignore_sides=None):
    scene = _Scene()
    scene.sides = sides
    scene.side_names = list(side_names)
    scene.battle_map = battle_map
    scene.ignore_sides = ignore_sides or {}
    return capture_battle(scene)


def load_snapshot(path):
    """JSON настроек окна (ключ battle) или сам снимок, или состав сторон."""
    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
        data = json.load(handle)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise ValueError('файл настроек должен быть объектом JSON')
    battle = data.get('battle')
    if isinstance(battle, dict) and battle.get('sides'):
        snap = copy.deepcopy(battle)
        if data.get('custom_types') and not snap.get('custom_types'):
            snap['custom_types'] = copy.deepcopy(data['custom_types'])
        return snap
    if data.get('sides') and 'side_names' in data:
        return copy.deepcopy(data)
    if data.get('army_types') or data.get('side_names') or data.get('num_sides'):
        return _snapshot_from_settings(data)
    raise ValueError('в файле нет ни сцены боя, ни состава сторон')


def _snapshot_from_settings(data):
    names = list(data.get('side_names') or [])
    counts = list(data.get('side_counts') or [])
    kinds = list(data.get('army_types') or [])
    strategies = list(data.get('strategy_types') or [])
    total = int(data.get('num_sides') or len(names) or len(kinds) or 0)
    groups = []
    for index in range(total):
        groups.append({
            'name': names[index] if index < len(names) and names[index] else f'Сторона {chr(65 + index)}',
            'type': kinds[index] if index < len(kinds) and kinds[index] else 'обычный',
            'count': int(counts[index]) if index < len(counts) and counts[index] is not None else 1,
            'strategy': strategies[index] if index < len(strategies) and strategies[index] else 'случайная',
        })
    saved = dict(EnemyFactory.CUSTOM_TYPES)
    try:
        for name, body in (data.get('custom_types') or {}).items():
            EnemyFactory.CUSTOM_TYPES[name] = body
        return make_scene(groups, data.get('ignore_sides') or None)
    finally:
        EnemyFactory.CUSTOM_TYPES.clear()
        EnemyFactory.CUSTOM_TYPES.update(saved)


def _cell(value):
    if isinstance(value, float):
        text = f'{value:.6f}'.rstrip('0').rstrip('.')
        return text if text not in ('', '-0') else '0'
    return value


def survivors_text(row):
    parts = []
    for creature in row.creatures:
        if creature.alive:
            parts.append(f'{creature.name}={creature.hp}')
    return '; '.join(parts)


def write_csv(path, report):
    """UTF-8 с BOM, разделитель «;» — так файл открывается колонками в Excel."""
    with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as handle:
        writer = csv.writer(handle, delimiter=';')
        writer.writerow(['сводка', 'значение'])
        for key, value in (
            ('боёв', report.battles_done),
            ('запрошено', report.battles_requested),
            ('базовый_сид', report.seed),
            ('лимит_раундов', report.max_rounds),
            ('процессы', report.workers),
            ('отменено', 'да' if report.cancelled else 'нет'),
            ('среднее_раундов', report.mean_rounds),
            ('медиана_раундов', report.median_rounds),
        ):
            writer.writerow([key, _cell(value)])
        writer.writerow([])
        writer.writerow(['исход', 'количество', 'доля', 'интервал_95_низ', 'интервал_95_верх'])
        for stat in list(report.sides) + [report.draw, report.timeout]:
            writer.writerow([stat.name, stat.count, _cell(stat.rate),
                             _cell(stat.ci_low), _cell(stat.ci_high)])
        writer.writerow([])
        writer.writerow(['существо', 'сторона', 'выживание', 'средние_хиты',
                         'хиты_если_выжил', 'урон_нанесён', 'урон_получен'])
        for creature in report.creatures:
            alive = '' if creature.mean_hp_alive is None else _cell(creature.mean_hp_alive)
            writer.writerow([
                creature.name, creature.side_name, _cell(creature.survival),
                _cell(creature.mean_hp), alive,
                _cell(creature.mean_dealt), _cell(creature.mean_taken),
            ])
        writer.writerow([])
        writer.writerow(['раундов', 'боёв'])
        for rounds, count in report.round_histogram:
            writer.writerow([rounds, count])
        writer.writerow([])
        writer.writerow(['номер', 'сид', 'победитель', 'раунды', 'исход', 'хиты_выживших'])
        for row in report.battles:
            writer.writerow([
                row.number, row.seed, row.winner, row.rounds,
                _OUTCOME_RU.get(row.outcome, row.outcome), survivors_text(row),
            ])


def summary_lines(report):
    lines = []
    tail = ' (отменено)' if report.cancelled else ''
    lines.append(f'Боёв: {report.battles_done} из {report.battles_requested}{tail}')
    for side in report.sides:
        lines.append(
            f'{side.name}: {side.count} ({side.rate * 100:.1f}%, '
            f'95% {side.ci_low * 100:.1f}–{side.ci_high * 100:.1f}%)')
    lines.append(f'Ничьи: {report.draw.count} ({report.draw.rate * 100:.1f}%)')
    lines.append(f'Таймауты: {report.timeout.count} ({report.timeout.rate * 100:.1f}%)')
    lines.append(
        f'Раунды: среднее {report.mean_rounds:.2f}, медиана {report.median_rounds:.2f}')
    return lines


def cli(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog='python -m monte_carlo',
        description=('Пакетная симуляция боя без интерфейса. '
                     'CSV в UTF-8 с BOM, колонки через точку с запятой.'),
    )
    parser.add_argument('-n', '--battles', type=int, default=1000,
                        help='число боёв (по умолчанию 1000)')
    parser.add_argument('-s', '--seed', type=int, default=1, help='базовый сид серии')
    parser.add_argument('-r', '--max-rounds', type=int, default=40,
                        help='лимит раундов на бой')
    parser.add_argument('-o', '--csv', required=True, help='куда записать CSV')
    parser.add_argument('--settings',
                        help='JSON настроек или снимок боя; без флага — орки против гоблинов')
    parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=1,
                        help='число процессов; 0 — по числу ядер')
    parser.add_argument('--crit', choices=('double_all', 'dice_only'), default='double_all')
    parser.add_argument('--no-auto-move', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('-q', '--quiet', action='store_true', help='не печатать прогресс')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        snapshot = load_snapshot(args.settings) if args.settings else demo_snapshot()
    except (OSError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        print(f'Не удалось прочитать сцену: {exc}', file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    cancel = threading.Event()

    def _stop(_signum, _frame):
        cancel.set()
        print('Останавливаюсь после текущего фрагмента…', file=sys.stderr)

    previous = signal.getsignal(signal.SIGINT)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, _stop)

    def progress(payload):
        if args.quiet:
            return
        done = payload['done']
        total = payload['total']
        if done == 1 or done == total or done % 50 == 0:
            print(f'{done}/{total}', file=sys.stderr)

    try:
        report = run_batch(
            snapshot, battles=args.battles, seed=args.seed, max_rounds=args.max_rounds,
            auto_move=not args.no_auto_move, crit_mode=args.crit, workers=args.workers,
            progress=progress, cancel=cancel)
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(str(exc), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    finally:
        signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, previous)
    write_csv(args.csv, report)
    print('\n'.join(summary_lines(report)))
    print(f'CSV: {args.csv}')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    # python -m запускает файл как __main__. Рабочий процесс должен импортировать
    # monte_carlo._chunk_task, поэтому считаем через обычный импорт модуля.
    from monte_carlo import cli as _cli
    raise SystemExit(_cli())



==================== observer.py ====================

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod


class Observer(ABC):
    """Абстрактный класс наблюдателя"""

    @abstractmethod
    def update(self, subject):
        """Метод для обновления состояния наблюдателя"""
        pass


class Subject:
    """Субъект, за которым наблюдают"""

    def __init__(self):
        self._observers = []

    def attach(self, observer):
        """Добавляет наблюдателя"""
        if observer not in self._observers:
            self._observers.append(observer)

    def detach(self, observer):
        """Удаляет наблюдателя"""
        try:
            self._observers.remove(observer)
        except ValueError:
            pass

    def notify(self):
        """Оповещает всех наблюдателей об изменении"""
        for observer in self._observers:
            observer.update(self)


==================== portraits.py ====================

"""Портреты существ: галерея dnd.su -> локальный кэш -> карточка отряда.

Чистый модуль без Qt: сеть и файловые операции блокирующие, поэтому вызываются
из QThread (qt_app._portrait_async). Индекс — battle_settings/portraits.json,
файлы — battle_cache/portraits/. Повторный запуск берёт картинку с диска.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import os
import random
import re

import requests

INDEX_FILE = os.path.join('battle_settings', 'portraits.json')
CACHE_DIR = os.path.join('battle_cache', 'portraits')
HEADERS = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) '
                         'AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120 Safari/537.36'}
EXT_RE = re.compile(r'\.(jpe?g|png|webp|gif)(?:\?|#|$)', re.I)
MAGIC = (b'\xff\xd8', b'\x89PNG', b'GIF8', b'RIFF', b'BM')


def _load_index():
    try:
        with open(INDEX_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _save_index(idx):
    try:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(INDEX_FILE), exist_ok=True)
        with open(INDEX_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(idx, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    except Exception:
        pass


def urls_for(type_name):
    return list((_load_index().get(type_name) or {}).get('urls') or [])


def set_urls(type_name, urls):
    """Запоминает галерею типа (дедуп с сохранением порядка)."""
    clean, seen = [], set()
    for u in urls or []:
        if u and u not in seen and EXT_RE.search(u):
            seen.add(u)
            clean.append(u)
    idx = _load_index()
    entry = idx.get(type_name) or {}
    entry['urls'] = clean
    entry['index'] = 0 if clean else None
    idx[type_name] = entry
    _save_index(idx)
    return clean


def pick(type_name, index=None):
    """Выбирает индекс картинки: заданный или случайный (не повторяя текущий)."""
    idx = _load_index()
    entry = idx.get(type_name) or {}
    urls = entry.get('urls') or []
    if not urls:
        return None
    if index is None:
        cur = entry.get('index')
        if len(urls) > 1:
            choices = [i for i in range(len(urls)) if i != cur]
            index = random.choice(choices)
        else:
            index = 0
    index = max(0, min(len(urls) - 1, int(index)))
    entry['index'] = index
    entry['urls'] = urls
    idx[type_name] = entry
    _save_index(idx)
    return index


def _cache_path(url):
    ext = (EXT_RE.search(url).group(1) if EXT_RE.search(url) else 'jpg').lower()
    if ext == 'jpeg':
        ext = 'jpg'
    return os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, hashlib.sha1(url.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest() + '.' + ext)


def local_path(type_name):
    """Уже скачанный файл выбранной картинки или None (без сети)."""
    entry = _load_index().get(type_name) or {}
    urls = entry.get('urls') or []
    i = entry.get('index')
    if not urls or i is None or not (0 <= i < len(urls)):
        return None
    p = _cache_path(urls[i])
    return p if os.path.exists(p) and os.path.getsize(p) > 0 else None


def download(url):
    path = _cache_path(url)
    if os.path.exists(path) and os.path.getsize(path) > 0:
        return path
    r = requests.get(url, headers=HEADERS, timeout=15)
    if r.status_code != 200:
        raise RuntimeError(f'Картинка отдала код {r.status_code}')
    data = r.content
    if not data or not any(data.startswith(m) for m in MAGIC):
        raise RuntimeError('По ссылке не изображение')
    os.makedirs(CACHE_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, 'wb') as f:
        f.write(data)
    return path


def ensure_local(type_name, index=None):
    """Блокирующая: выбирает картинку, качает при необходимости, отдаёт путь."""
    urls = urls_for(type_name)
    if not urls:
        return None
    i = pick(type_name, index)
    return {'path': download(urls[i]), 'index': i, 'total': len(urls)}


def find_and_cache(type_name):
    """Ищет монстра по имени на dnd.su и подтягивает его галерею (для пресетов)."""
    urls = urls_for(type_name)
    if not urls:
        import bestiary_core
        results = bestiary_core.search_monsters(bestiary_core.fetch_search(type_name), type_name)
        if not results:
            return None
        page = bestiary_core.fetch(results[0]['url'])
        urls = bestiary_core.extract_gallery(page, results[0]['url'])
        if not urls:
            return None
        set_urls(type_name, urls)
    return ensure_local(type_name, None)

def get_framing(type_name):
    """Сохранённый кадр типа (пустой dict -> возьмётся DEFAULT_FRAMING)."""
    entry = _load_index().get(type_name) or {}
    return dict(entry.get('framing') or {})


def set_framing(type_name, framing):
    idx = _load_index()
    entry = idx.get(type_name) or {}
    entry['framing'] = dict(framing)
    idx[type_name] = entry
    _save_index(idx)

def types_available():
    return list(EnemyFactory.CREATURE_DEFS) + list(EnemyFactory.CUSTOM_TYPES)


def clear_layout(lay):
    while lay.count():
        it = lay.takeAt(0)
        w = it.widget()
        if w is not None:
            w.setParent(None)
            w.deleteLater()
        elif it.layout() is not None:
            clear_layout(it.layout())


class Updater(Observer):
    def __init__(self, win):
        self.win = win

    def update(self, subject):
        self.win.dirty.add(subject)
        if not self.win.suspend:
            self.win.refresh_dirty()


class CardsHost(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, owner):
        super().__init__(owner)
        self.owner = owner
        self.grid = QGridLayout(self)
        self.grid.setSpacing(10)

    def resizeEvent(self, e):
        super().resizeEvent(e)
        self.owner.relayout_cards()

    # ================= вкладки =================
    def select_tab(self, idx):
        self.stack.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self.nav_buttons[idx].setChecked(True)
        clear_layout(self.sub_holder)
        tools = {
            0: [('Сохранить настройки', self.save_settings),
                ('Загрузить настройки', self.load_settings)],
            1: [('Показать статус', self.show_status),
                ('Очистить результаты', self.clear_log)],
            2: [('Применить настройки альянсов', self.apply_alliances)],
            3: [('🎲 Расставить случайно', self.place_random),
                ('🛡 Расставить строем', self.place_formation)],
        }[idx]
        for text, fn in tools:
            b = QPushButton(text)
            b.setProperty('variant', 'outline')
            b.setMinimumHeight(32)
            b.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            b.clicked.connect(fn)
            self.sub_holder.addWidget(b)

    def toggle_theme(self):
        name = self.theme.toggle()
        pal = self.theme.palette()
        self.theme_btn.set_kind('moon' if name == 'dark' else 'sun')
        for b in self._icon_buttons:
            b.set_palette(pal)
        for c in self.cards:
            c['frame'].set_palette(pal)
        self.map_view.set_palette(pal)
        self.status.showMessage(f'Тема: {"тёмная" if name == "dark" else "светлая"}')

    # ================= отмена / повтор =================
    def _install_history_controls(self):
        edit = self.menuBar().addMenu('Правка')
        self.undo_action = QAction('Отменить', self)
        self.undo_action.setObjectName('undo')
        self.undo_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence('Ctrl+Z'))
        self.undo_action.setShortcutContext(Qt.ShortcutContext.WindowShortcut)
        self.undo_action.triggered.connect(self.do_undo)
        self.redo_action = QAction('Повторить', self)
        self.redo_action.setObjectName('redo')
        self.redo_action.setShortcuts([
            QKeySequence('Ctrl+Shift+Z'),
            QKeySequence('Ctrl+Y'),
        ])
        self.redo_action.setShortcutContext(Qt.ShortcutContext.WindowShortcut)
        self.redo_action.triggered.connect(self.do_redo)
        edit.addAction(self.undo_action)
        edit.addAction(self.redo_action)
        self.addAction(self.undo_action)
        self.addAction(self.redo_action)

        self.btn_undo = QPushButton('Отменить')
        self.btn_redo = QPushButton('Повторить')
        for button, action in ((self.btn_undo, self.undo_action),
                               (self.btn_redo, self.redo_action)):
            button.setObjectName(action.objectName() + '_button')
            button.setMinimumHeight(32)
            button.setMinimumWidth(112)
            button.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            button.clicked.connect(action.trigger)

    def _sync_history_actions(self):
        undo = self.history.undo_label()
        redo = self.history.redo_label()
        self.undo_action.setEnabled(undo is not None)
        self.redo_action.setEnabled(redo is not None)
        self.undo_action.setText(f'Отменить: {undo}' if undo else 'Отменить')
        self.redo_action.setText(f'Повторить: {redo}' if redo else 'Повторить')
        self.undo_action.setToolTip(self.undo_action.text() if undo else 'Нечего отменять')
        self.redo_action.setToolTip(self.redo_action.text() if redo else 'Нечего повторять')
        self.undo_action.setStatusTip(self.undo_action.toolTip())
        self.redo_action.setStatusTip(self.redo_action.toolTip())
        self.btn_undo.setEnabled(self.undo_action.isEnabled())
        self.btn_redo.setEnabled(self.redo_action.isEnabled())
        self.btn_undo.setToolTip(self.undo_action.toolTip())
        self.btn_redo.setToolTip(self.redo_action.toolTip())

    def _install_batch_menu(self):
        menu = self.menuBar().addMenu('Симуляция')
        action = QAction('Пакетная симуляция…', self)
        action.setObjectName('batch_sim_action')
        action.triggered.connect(self.open_batch)
        menu.addAction(action)
        self.batch_action = action

    def _forget_batch_dialog(self, *_args):
        self._batch_dialog = None

    def open_batch(self):
        if not self.sides:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, 'Пакетная симуляция',
                'Сначала создайте армии на вкладке «Настройка армий».')
            return
        app = QApplication.instance()
        if app is not None:
            qt_async.install(app)
        dialog = getattr(self, '_batch_dialog', None)
        if dialog is not None:
            try:
                visible = dialog.isVisible()
            except RuntimeError:
                visible = False
                dialog = None
            if dialog is not None and visible:
                dialog.raise_()
                dialog.activateWindow()
                return
        from qt_batch import BatchDialog
        dialog = BatchDialog(
            self, capture_battle(self),
            auto_move=self.auto_move.isChecked(),
            crit_mode=self.crit_mode.currentData() or 'double_all')
        dialog.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_DeleteOnClose, True)
        dialog.destroyed.connect(self._forget_batch_dialog)
        self._batch_dialog = dialog
        dialog.show()

    def _commit(self, label):
        if not self._history_ready or self._suspend_history:
            return False
        after = capture_battle(self)
        if not self.history.commit(label, self._current, after):
            return False
        self._current = after
        self._sync_history_actions()
        return True

    def _note_selection(self):
        """Выбор токена не шаг истории, но повтор хода не должен его сбрасывать."""
        if not self._history_ready or self._suspend_history or not self._current:
            return
        uid = ensure_uid(self.selected) if self.selected is not None else None
        if self._current.get('selected_uid') == uid:
            return
        snap = dict(self._current)
        snap['selected_uid'] = uid
        self._current = snap

    def _latest_round_events(self):
        numbered = [event for event in self.battle_events if event.round]
        if not numbered:
            return []
        last = max(event.round for event in numbered)
        return [event for event in self.battle_events if event.round == last]

    def _sync_cards(self, rows):
        rows = list(rows or [])
        if len(rows) != len(self.cards):
            self._discard_cards()
            for row in rows:
                self.add_card(row.get('type'), row.get('strategy') or 'случайная',
                              row.get('name'),
                              row.get('count') if row.get('count') is not None else 5)
            return
        for card, row in zip(self.cards, rows):
            card['name'].setText(row.get('name') or '')
            new_type = row.get('type') or card['type']
            changed = new_type != card['type']
            card['type'] = new_type
            card['type_lbl'].setText(new_type)
            card['strat'].blockSignals(True)
            card['strat'].setCurrentText(row.get('strategy') or card['strat'].currentText())
            card['strat'].blockSignals(False)
            card['count'].blockSignals(True)
            card['count'].setValue(int(row.get('count') if row.get('count') is not None else 0))
            card['count'].blockSignals(False)
            if changed:
                self._show_cached_portrait(card)

    def _discard_cards(self):
        for card in list(self.cards):
            card['alive'] = False
            guard = card.get('guard')
            if guard is not None:
                guard.drop()
            for button in card['icons']:
                if button in self._icon_buttons:
                    self._icon_buttons.remove(button)
            card['frame'].setParent(None)
            card['frame'].deleteLater()
        self.cards = []
        self.cols = 0

    def _restore(self, snapshot):
        self._suspend_history = True
        try:
            info = apply_snapshot(snapshot, self)
            self._sync_cards(snapshot.get('setup') or [])
            if info.roster_changed:
                self.rebuild_battle_lists()
                self.log_panel.set_roster(self.sides, self.side_names)
            else:
                self.refresh_all_labels()
            self.refresh_alliances()
            if info.events_changed:
                self.log_panel.load_events(self.battle_events)
            self.selected = info.selected
            self.map_view.rebuild(self.battle_map, self.sides, self.selected)
            self.map_view.set_round_lines(
                map_marks(self._latest_round_events(), self._token_radius))
            self.map_view.set_focus_lines([])
            if self.selected is not None:
                pos = None if self.battle_map is None else self.battle_map.get_pos(self.selected)
                left = self.selected.speed - getattr(self.selected, 'moved_ft', 0)
                self.map_info.setText(
                    f'{self.selected.name}: HP {self.selected.hp}/{self.selected.max_hp}, '
                    f'AC {self.selected.ac}, клетка {pos}, скорость {self.selected.speed} фт, '
                    f'остаток {left} фт')
            else:
                self.map_info.setText('Токен не выбран.')
        finally:
            self._suspend_history = False
        self._current = snapshot
        self._sync_history_actions()

    def do_undo(self):
        if not self.history.can_undo():
            return
        label = self.history.undo_label()
        self._restore(self.history.undo())
        self.status.showMessage(f'Отменено: {label}')

    def do_redo(self):
        if not self.history.can_redo():
            return
        label = self.history.redo_label()
        self._restore(self.history.redo())
        self.status.showMessage(f'Повторено: {label}')

    # ================= настройка армий =================
    def _build_setup(self):
        page = QWidget()
        lay = QVBoxLayout(page)
        sa = QScrollArea()
        sa.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.cards_host = CardsHost(self)
        sa.setWidget(self.cards_host)
        lay.addWidget(sa, 1)
        row = QHBoxLayout()
        row.addStretch()
        create = QPushButton('⚔️  Создать армии')
        create.setProperty('variant', 'primary')
        create.setMinimumHeight(40)
        create.setMinimumWidth(220)
        create.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        create.clicked.connect(self.create_armies)
        row.addWidget(create)
        row.addStretch()
        lay.addLayout(row)
        return page

    def relayout_cards(self):
        if not hasattr(self, 'plus_btn'):
            return
        w = self.cards_host.width()
        if w < 100:
            return
        cols = max(1, min(8, w // 340))
        if cols == self.cols and self.cards_host.grid.count():
            return
        self.cols = cols
        for i in range(8):
            self.cards_host.grid.setColumnStretch(i, 1 if i < cols else 0)
        for i, c in enumerate(self.cards):
            self.cards_host.grid.addWidget(c['frame'], i // cols, i % cols)
        if len(self.cards) < MAX_SIDES:
            self.plus_btn.show()
            self.cards_host.grid.addWidget(self.plus_btn, len(self.cards) // cols,
                                           len(self.cards) % cols,
                                           Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        else:
            self.plus_btn.hide()

    def add_card(self, army_type=None, strategy='случайная', name=None, count=5):
        if len(self.cards) >= MAX_SIDES:
            return
        i = len(self.cards)
        types = types_available()
        army_type = army_type or types[0]
        pal = self.theme.palette()

        frame = PortraitCard(pal)
        frame.setMinimumWidth(300)
        outer = QGridLayout(frame)
        outer.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        outer.setSpacing(0)

        header = QWidget()
        header.setObjectName('cardHeader')
        header.setStyleSheet('#cardHeader { background: transparent; }')
        header.setFixedHeight(frame.header_height())
        frame.header = header
        hl = QGridLayout(header)
        hl.setContentsMargins(14, 8, 10, 6)
        hl.setSpacing(4)
        title = QLineEdit(f'Сторона {chr(65 + i)}' if name is None else name)
        title.setProperty('role', 'title')
        cross = IconButton('cross', pal, size=26, outline=True)
        cross.setToolTip('Удалить отряд')
        hl.addWidget(cross, 0, 2, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)
        hl.addWidget(title, 1, 0, 1, 2,
                     Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignBottom)
        hl.setRowStretch(0, 1)
        outer.addWidget(header, 0, 0)

        body = QWidget()
        body.setObjectName('cardBody')
        body.setStyleSheet('#cardBody { background: transparent; }')
        g = QGridLayout(body)
        g.setContentsMargins(14, 2, 12, 12)
        g.setVerticalSpacing(7)

        type_lbl = QLabel(army_type)
        type_lbl.setProperty('role', 'accent')
        type_lbl.setWordWrap(True)
        change = IconButton('swap', pal, size=28, outline=True)
        change.setToolTip('Изменить состав')
        g.addWidget(QLabel('Состав:'), 0, 0)
        g.addWidget(type_lbl, 0, 1)
        g.addWidget(change, 0, 2, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight)

        strat = QComboBox()
        strat.addItems(STRATS)
        strat.setCurrentText(strategy)
        g.addWidget(QLabel('Тактика:'), 1, 0)
        g.addWidget(strat, 1, 1, 1, 2)

        cnt = QSpinBox()
        cnt.setRange(0, 500)
        cnt.setValue(count)
        g.addWidget(QLabel('Количество:'), 2, 0)
        g.addWidget(cnt, 2, 1, 1, 2)

        prow = QHBoxLayout()
        prow.setSpacing(6)
        dice = IconButton('dice', pal, size=28, outline=True)
        dice.setToolTip('Другая картинка из галереи монстра')
        find = IconButton('image', pal, size=28, outline=True)
        find.setToolTip('Найти портрет на dnd.su по названию состава')
        pic_info = QLabel('портрет не загружен')
        pic_info.setProperty('role', 'muted')
        prow.addWidget(dice)
        prow.addWidget(find)
        prow.addWidget(pic_info, 1)
        g.addLayout(prow, 3, 0, 1, 3)
        g.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        outer.addWidget(body, 1, 0)
        outer.setRowStretch(2, 1)

        card = {'frame': frame, 'name': title, 'type': army_type, 'type_lbl': type_lbl,
                'strat': strat, 'count': cnt, 'icons': [cross, change, dice, find],
                'pic_info': pic_info, 'dice': dice, 'alive': True, 'busy': False,
                'guard': qt_async.Guard()}
        cross.clicked.connect(lambda: self.remove_card(card))
        change.clicked.connect(lambda: self.change_composition(card))
        dice.clicked.connect(lambda: self._portrait_async(card))
        find.clicked.connect(lambda: self._portrait_async(card, search=True))
        title.editingFinished.connect(lambda c=card: self._on_side_name(c))
        strat.currentTextChanged.connect(lambda _text, c=card: self._on_side_strategy(c))
        cnt.editingFinished.connect(lambda c=card: self._on_side_count(c))
        self.cards.append(card)

        if not hasattr(self, 'plus_btn'):
            self.plus_btn = IconButton('plus', pal, size=48, outline=True, round_shape=True)
            self.plus_btn.setToolTip('Добавить сторону')
            self.plus_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self.add_card())
            self._icon_buttons.append(self.plus_btn)
        for b in card['icons']:
            self._icon_buttons.append(b)
        self.cols = 0
        self.relayout_cards()
        self._show_cached_portrait(card)
        self._commit(f'добавление стороны {card["name"].text()}')

    def _on_side_name(self, card):
        if not card.get('alive', True):
            return
        self._commit(f'имя стороны {card["name"].text()}')

    def _on_side_strategy(self, card):
        if not card.get('alive', True):
            return
        self._commit(f'тактика: {card["name"].text()}')

    def _on_side_count(self, card):
        if not card.get('alive', True):
            return
        self._commit(f'число существ: {card["name"].text()}')

    # ---------- портреты ----------
    def _show_cached_portrait(self, card):
        path = portraits.local_path(card['type'])
        total = len(portraits.urls_for(card['type']))
        if path:
            pix = QPixmap(path)
            if not pix.isNull():
                card['frame'].set_portrait(pix)
                idx = portraits.pick(card['type'], self._current_index(card['type']))
                card['pic_info'].setText(f'портрет {(idx or 0) + 1} / {total}')
                card['dice'].setEnabled(total > 1)
                return
        card['frame'].set_portrait(None)
        card['pic_info'].setText(f'галерея: {total}' if total else 'портрет не загружен')
        card['dice'].setEnabled(total > 1)

    @staticmethod
    def _current_index(type_name):
        import json as _json
        try:
            with open(portraits.INDEX_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return (_json.load(f).get(type_name) or {}).get('index')
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _portrait_async(self, card, search=False, index=None, tries=0):
        if not card.get('alive') or card.get('busy'):
            return
        card['busy'] = True
        card['pic_info'].setText('загрузка портрета…')
        fn = portraits.find_and_cache if search else portraits.ensure_local
        args = (card['type'],) if search else (card['type'], index)
        qt_async.submit(fn, args,
                        on_ok=lambda res: self._portrait_done(card, res, tries),
                        on_fail=lambda msg: self._portrait_fail(card, msg),
                        guard=card.get('guard'))

    def _portrait_done(self, card, res, tries=0):
        if not card.get('alive'):
            return
        card['busy'] = False
        if not res:
            card['pic_info'].setText('галерея не найдена')
            card['frame'].set_portrait(None)
            return
        pix = QPixmap(res['path'])
        if pix.isNull():
            card['pic_info'].setText('файл не читается')
            return
        # качество: превью-мелочь не растягиваем — берём следующий кандидат
        if min(pix.width(), pix.height()) < 260 and res['total'] > 1 and tries < res['total'] - 1:
            card['pic_info'].setText('ищем вариант крупнее…')
            self._portrait_async(card, index=(res['index'] + 1) % res['total'], tries=tries + 1)
            return
        card['frame'].set_portrait(pix)
        card['pic_info'].setText(f"портрет {res['index'] + 1} / {res['total']}")
        card['dice'].setEnabled(res['total'] > 1)
        self.status.showMessage(f"Портрет загружен: {card['type']}")

    def _portrait_fail(self, card, msg):
        if not card.get('alive'):
            return
        card['busy'] = False
        card['pic_info'].setText('не удалось загрузить')
        self.status.showMessage(f'Портрет: {msg}')

    def remove_card(self, card):
        if len(self.cards) <= 1:
            QMessageBox.information(self, 'Отряд', 'Должна остаться хотя бы одна сторона.')
            return
        side_name = card['name'].text()
        if QMessageBox.question(self, 'Удалить отряд?', f'Удалить «{side_name}»?',
                                QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes |
                                QMessageBox.StandardButton.No) != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return
        card['alive'] = False
        if card.get('guard') is not None:
            card['guard'].drop()
        self.cards.remove(card)
        for b in card['icons']:
            if b in self._icon_buttons:
                self._icon_buttons.remove(b)
        card['frame'].setParent(None)
        card['frame'].deleteLater()
        self.cols = 0
        self.relayout_cards()
        self._commit(f'удаление стороны {side_name}')

    def change_composition(self, card):
        d = SideCreatorDialog(self, types_available(), mode='edit', current_type=card['type'])
        d.ready.connect(lambda res: self._apply_composition(card, res))
        d.exec()

    def _apply_composition(self, card, res):
        card['type'] = res['army_type']
        card['type_lbl'].setText(res['army_type'])
        card['strat'].blockSignals(True)
        card['strat'].setCurrentText(res['strategy'])
        card['strat'].blockSignals(False)
        self.status.showMessage(f'Состав обновлён: {res["army_type"]}')
        self._show_cached_portrait(card)
        self._commit(f'состав: {res["army_type"]}')
        if portraits.local_path(card['type']) is None:
            self._portrait_async(card, search=not portraits.urls_for(card['type']))

    def create_armies(self):
        try:
            self.sides, self.side_names = [], []
            strat_cls = {'случайная': RandomStrategy, 'агрессивная': AggressiveStrategy,
                         'защитная': DefensiveStrategy, 'поддержка': SupportStrategy}
            for i, c in enumerate(self.cards):
                name = c['name'].text().strip() or f'Сторона {chr(65 + i)}'
                self.side_names.append(name)
                cls = strat_cls[c['strat'].currentText()]
                side = []
                for j in range(c['count'].value()):
                    e = EnemyFactory.create_enemy(c['type'], f'{name}_{j + 1}', i)
                    e.strategy = cls()
                    side.append(e)
                self.sides.append(side)
            n = len(self.sides)
            max_side = max((len(s) for s in self.sides), default=0)
            map_w = max(24, 4 * n)
            zone_cols = max(1, map_w // n)
            map_h = max(16, (max_side + zone_cols - 1) // zone_cols + 2)
            self.battle_map = BattleMap(width=map_w, height=map_h)
            self.ignore_sides = {}
            self.selected = None
            self.round_no = 0
            self.battle_events = []
            self.battle_map.formation_placement(self.sides)
            self.rebuild_battle_lists()
            self.refresh_alliances()
            self.map_view.rebuild(self.battle_map, self.sides, self.selected)
            self.map_view.set_round_lines([])
            self.map_view.set_area_cells([])
            self.map_view.set_preview_cells([])
            self.map_view.set_focus_areas([])
            self.log_panel.clear()
            self.log_panel.set_roster(self.sides, self.side_names)
            total = sum(len(s) for s in self.sides)
            self.battle_events.extend(self.log_panel.add_note(
                f'Армии созданы: сторон {n}, существ {total}.'))
            self.status.showMessage(f'Армии созданы: {n} сторон, {total} существ.')
            self._commit('создание армий')
            self.select_tab(1)
        except Exception as e:
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            QMessageBox.critical(self, 'Ошибка', f'Ошибка при создании армий: {e}')

    # ================= битва =================
    def _build_battle(self):
        page = QWidget()
        lay = QVBoxLayout(page)
        self.battle_scroll = QScrollArea()
        self.battle_scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.battle_host = QWidget()
        self.battle_lay = QVBoxLayout(self.battle_host)
        self.battle_scroll.setWidget(self.battle_host)
        lay.addWidget(self.battle_scroll, 2)
        self.log_panel = CombatLogPanel()
        self.log_panel.entry_activated.connect(self.on_log_entry)
        lay.addWidget(self.log_panel, 3)
        row = QHBoxLayout()
        for text, fn in (('▶  Провести раунд боя', self.run_round),
                         ('Пакетная симуляция…', self.open_batch),
                         ('Массовое редактирование', self.mass_edit),
                         ('📋 Копировать лог', self.copy_log),
                         ('💾 Экспорт журнала', self.export_log),
                         ('🔍 Анализ сражения', self.open_analysis)):
            b = QPushButton(text)
            b.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            b.clicked.connect(fn)
            if text.startswith('▶'):
                b.setProperty('variant', 'primary')
                b.setMinimumHeight(38)
                self.round_btn = b
            if text.startswith('Пакетная'):
                b.setObjectName('batch_sim')
                self.batch_btn = b
            if 'Копировать' in text:
                self.copy_btn = b
            row.addWidget(b)
        row.addStretch()
        self.manual_toggle = QCheckBox('Ручное управление')
        self.manual_toggle.setObjectName('manual_toggle')
        self.manual_toggle.setToolTip(
            'На ходу выбранного существа или его стороны раунд останавливается: '
            'цель и область выбираете вы')
        self.manual_scope = QComboBox()
        self.manual_scope.setObjectName('manual_scope')
        self.manual_scope.addItems(['Выбранное существо', 'Сторона выбранного'])
        self.manual_toggle.toggled.connect(self._sync_manual_toggle)
        self.manual_scope.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._sync_manual_scope)
        row.addWidget(self.manual_toggle)
        row.addWidget(self.manual_scope)
        self.auto_move = QCheckBox('Автоперемещение в бою')
        self.auto_move.setChecked(True)
        row.addWidget(self.auto_move)
        row.addWidget(QLabel('Крит:'))
        self.crit_mode = QComboBox()
        self.crit_mode.addItem('Удваивать весь урон', 'double_all')
        self.crit_mode.addItem('Удваивать только кости', 'dice_only')
        self.crit_mode.setCurrentIndex(0)
        row.addWidget(self.crit_mode)
        lay.addLayout(row)
        return page

    def rebuild_battle_lists(self):
        clear_layout(self.battle_lay)
        self.enemy_labels, self.side_checks = {}, []
        if not self.sides:
            hint = QLabel('Сначала создайте армии на вкладке «Настройка армий».')
            hint.setProperty('role', 'muted')
            self.battle_lay.addWidget(hint)
            self.battle_lay.addStretch()
            return
        cols = min(len(self.sides), 3)
        grid = QGridLayout()
        grid.setSpacing(8)
        for i, (side, name) in enumerate(zip(self.sides, self.side_names)):
            box = QGroupBox(name)
            bl = QVBoxLayout(box)
            sa = QScrollArea()
            sa.setWidgetResizable(True)
            sa.setMinimumHeight(200)
            host = QWidget()
            hl = QVBoxLayout(host)
            hl.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
            all_cb = QCheckBox('Выделить все')
            hl.addWidget(all_cb)
            checks = []
            for e in side:
                e.attach(self.updater)
                r = QHBoxLayout()
                cb = QCheckBox()
                lab = QLabel(self._enemy_text(e))
                lab.setToolTip(self._enemy_tip(e))
                lab.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.ContextMenuPolicy.CustomContextMenu)
                lab.customContextMenuRequested.connect(
                    lambda pos, en=e, widget=lab: self.open_condition_menu(
                        en, widget.mapToGlobal(pos)))
                lab.mouseDoubleClickEvent = lambda ev, en=e: self.edit_one(en)
                r.addWidget(cb)
                r.addWidget(lab, 1)
                hl.addLayout(r)
                checks.append(cb)
                self.enemy_labels[e] = lab
            hl.addStretch()
            sa.setWidget(host)
            bl.addWidget(sa)
            all_cb.toggled.connect(lambda on, cs=checks: [c.setChecked(on) for c in cs])
            self.side_checks.append(checks)
            grid.addWidget(box, i // cols, i % cols)
        for c in range(cols):
            grid.setColumnStretch(c, 1)
        self.battle_lay.addLayout(grid)

    @staticmethod
    def _enemy_text(e):
        from conditions import condition_marks
        status = '🟢' if e.alive else '🔴'
        atk = ', '.join(f'{a.name}{"✦" if getattr(a, "magical", False) else ""}'
