        right = QVBoxLayout()
        right.setSpacing(7)
        name = QLabel(m['name'])
        name.setWordWrap(True)
        name.setStyleSheet(f"color: {p['fg']}; font-size: 16pt; font-weight: bold;")
        right.addWidget(name)
        meta = ' · '.join(str(x) for x in (m.get('size'), m.get('type')) if x)
        if m.get('cr'):
            meta += f" · CR {m['cr']}"
        if m.get('prof_bonus'):
            meta += f" · мастерство {m['prof_bonus']}"
        if meta:
            ml = QLabel(meta)
            ml.setWordWrap(True)
            ml.setStyleSheet(f"color: {p['fg_muted']};")
            right.addWidget(ml)

        chips = QHBoxLayout()
        chips.setSpacing(6)
        chips.addWidget(self._chip(f"❤ {m['hp']} HP", p['danger']))
        chips.addWidget(self._chip(f"🛡 {m['ac']} AC", p['accent']))
        chips.addWidget(self._chip(f"👟 {m.get('speed', 30)} фт", p['success']))
        if m.get('regen'):
            chips.addWidget(self._chip(f"💚 +{m['regen']}/раунд", p['warning']))
        chips.addStretch()
        right.addLayout(chips)

        sc, md = m.get('ability_scores') or {}, m.get('ability_mods') or {}
        if sc:
            ab = QHBoxLayout()
            ab.setSpacing(6)
            for k in ABILITY_ORDER:
                if k not in sc:
                    continue
                card = QFrame()
                card.setStyleSheet(
                    f"background: {p['field_bg']}; border: 1px solid {p['border']};"
                    f"border-radius: 9px;")
                cl = QVBoxLayout(card)
                cl.setContentsMargins(8, 5, 8, 5)
                cl.setSpacing(0)
                t = QLabel(ABILITY_LABEL[k])
                t.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
                t.setStyleSheet(f"color: {p['accent']}; font-size: 8pt; font-weight: bold; border: none;")
                s = QLabel(str(sc[k]))
                s.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
                s.setStyleSheet(f"color: {p['fg']}; font-size: 13pt; font-weight: bold; border: none;")
                mv = md.get(k, 0)
                d = QLabel(f"{'+' if mv >= 0 else ''}{mv}")
                d.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
                d.setStyleSheet(f"color: {p['fg_muted']}; font-size: 8pt; border: none;")
                cl.addWidget(t); cl.addWidget(s); cl.addWidget(d)
                ab.addWidget(card)
            ab.addStretch()
            right.addLayout(ab)

        prow = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_pic = QPushButton('🎲 Другая картинка')
        self.btn_pic.setProperty('variant', 'outline')
        self.btn_pic.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_pic.clicked.connect(lambda: self._load_portrait(self._pic_index + 1))
        self.pic_info = QLabel('')
        self.pic_info.setStyleSheet(f"color: {p['fg_muted']};")
        prow.addWidget(self.btn_pic)
        prow.addWidget(self.pic_info, 1)
        right.addLayout(prow)
        right.addStretch()
        head.addLayout(right, 1)
        self.preview_lay.addLayout(head)

        self._section('⚔️ Атаки')
        if m.get('multiattack'):
            ma = QLabel('Мультиатака: ' + ' + '.join(
                f"{s['attack']}×{s.get('count', 1)}" for s in m['multiattack']))
            ma.setWordWrap(True)
            ma.setStyleSheet(f"color: {p['warning']};")
            self.preview_lay.addWidget(ma)
        atks = m.get('attacks') or []
        table = QTableWidget(len(atks), 5)
        table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(['Атака', 'Попадание', 'Урон', 'Тип', 'Дальность'])
        table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        table.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        table.setSelectionMode(QTableWidget.SelectionMode.NoSelection)
        hh = table.horizontalHeader()
        hh.setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hh.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        for r, a in enumerate(atks):
            mark = ' ✦' if a.get('magical') else ''
            dmg = bestiary_core.format_damage(a)
            for c, val in enumerate((a['name'] + mark, f"{a['attack_bonus']:+d}", dmg,
                                     a.get('damage_type') or '—',
                                     f"{a.get('range_ft', 5)} фт")):
                table.setItem(r, c, QTableWidgetItem(str(val)))
        table.setFixedHeight(min(210, 30 * (len(atks) + 1) + 12))
        self.preview_lay.addWidget(table)

        legendary = m.get('legendary_actions') or None
        if legendary:
            self._section('👑 Легендарные действия')
            cap = QLabel(f"За раунд: {legendary.get('per_round')}. "
                         'В конце чужого хода тратится вариант, который бьёт известной атакой.')
            cap.setWordWrap(True)
            self.preview_lay.addWidget(cap)
            for option in legendary.get('options') or []:
                mark = ' · в бою' if option.get('usable') else ' · только в карточке'
                line = QLabel(f"{option.get('name')} (стоит {option.get('cost', 1)}){mark}. "
                              f"{option.get('desc', '')}")
                line.setWordWrap(True)
                self.preview_lay.addWidget(line)
        resist = m.get('legendary_resistance')
        if resist:
            self._kv('Легендарное сопротивление',
                     f"{resist.get('count')}/день. {resist.get('desc', '')}")
        lair = m.get('lair_actions') or None
        if lair and (lair.get('actions') or lair.get('intro')):
            self._section('🕳 Действия логова')
            hint = QLabel('Сохранены в карточке. Симулятор их не разыгрывает.')
            hint.setWordWrap(True)
            hint.setProperty('role', 'muted')
            self.preview_lay.addWidget(hint)
            for action in (lair.get('actions') or [])[:6]:
                line = QLabel(action)
                line.setWordWrap(True)
                self.preview_lay.addWidget(line)
        specials = m.get('special_actions') or []
        if specials:
            self._section('Особые действия')
            hint = QLabel('Нет броска атаки — в раунде не выполняются, текст сохранён.')
            hint.setWordWrap(True)
            hint.setProperty('role', 'muted')
            self.preview_lay.addWidget(hint)
            for action in specials:
                line = QLabel(f"{action.get('name')}. {action.get('desc', '')}")
                line.setWordWrap(True)
                self.preview_lay.addWidget(line)

        self._section('🛡 Защиты и чувства')
        self._kv('Уязвимости', ', '.join(m.get('vulnerabilities') or []))
        self._kv('Сопротивления', ', '.join(m.get('resistances') or []))
        self._kv('Иммунитеты', ', '.join(m.get('immunities') or []))
        self._kv('Иммун. к условиям', ', '.join(m.get('condition_immunities') or []))
        self._kv('Спасброски', ', '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in (m.get('saves') or {}).items()))
        self._kv('Навыки', ', '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in (m.get('skills') or {}).items()))
        self._kv('Чувства', ', '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in (m.get('senses') or {}).items()))
        self._kv('Языки', ', '.join(m.get('languages') or []))

        traits = m.get('traits') or []
        if traits:
            self._section('✨ Особенности')
            for tr in traits:
                t = QLabel(f"<b>{tr.get('name', '')}.</b> {tr.get('desc', '')}")
                t.setWordWrap(True)
                t.setTextFormat(Qt.TextFormat.RichText)
                self.preview_lay.addWidget(t)
        self.preview_lay.addStretch()
        self._pic_index = 0
        self._load_portrait(0)

    # ================= портрет =================
    def _load_portrait(self, index, tries=0):
        m = self._monster or {}
        urls = m.get('portraits') or []
        if not urls:
            self.btn_pic.setEnabled(False)
            self.pic_info.setText('галерея пуста')
            self.pic.setText('нет портрета')
            return
        i = index % len(urls)
        self._pic_index = i
        self._pic_req += 1
        token = self._pic_req
        self.btn_pic.setEnabled(len(urls) > 1)
        self.pic_info.setText(f'картинка {i + 1} / {len(urls)} · загрузка…')
        self.pic.setText('⏳')
        qt_async.submit(portraits.download, (urls[i],),
                        on_ok=lambda path: self._got_pic(path, i, len(urls), token, tries),
                        on_fail=lambda msg: self._pic_failed(msg, token),
                        guard=self._guard)

    def _got_pic(self, path, index, total, token, tries):
        if token != self._pic_req:
            return
        pix = QPixmap(path)
        if pix.isNull():
            self.pic.setText('не читается')
            self.pic_info.setText(f'картинка {index + 1} / {total}')
            return
        if min(pix.width(), pix.height()) < 240 and total > 1 and tries < total - 1:
            self._load_portrait(index + 1, tries + 1)
            return
        self.pic.setPixmap(pix.scaled(self.pic.width() - 8, self.pic.height() - 8,
                                      Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio,
                                      Qt.TransformationMode.SmoothTransformation))
        self.pic_info.setText(f'картинка {index + 1} / {total} · {pix.width()}×{pix.height()}')

    def _pic_failed(self, msg, token):
        if token != self._pic_req:
            return
        self.pic.setText('нет портрета')
        self.pic_info.setText('картинка не загрузилась')

    # ================= импорт =================
    def do_import(self):
        m = self._monster
        if not m:
            return
        chars = {k: v for k, v in m.items() if k in CHAR_KEYS}
        EnemyFactory.register_type(
            m['name'], hp=m['hp'], ac=m['ac'], attacks=m['attacks'],
            speed=m.get('speed', 30), regen=m.get('regen', 0),
            multiattack=m.get('multiattack'), **chars)
        urls = m.get('portraits') or []
        portraits.set_urls(m['name'], urls)
        if urls:
            portraits.pick(m['name'], self._pic_index)
        self.imported.emit(m['name'])
        self.accept()

    # ================= жизненный цикл =================
    def closeEvent(self, e):
        self._guard.drop()
        self._timer.stop()
        super().closeEvent(e)

    def reject(self):
        self._guard.drop()
        self._timer.stop()
        super().reject()

    def accept(self):
        self._guard.drop()
        self._timer.stop()
        super().accept()

==================== qt_combat_log.py ====================

"""Панель журнала боя: модель + фильтр, без пересборки текста каждый раунд.

Дерево «раунд → события» дописывается пачкой (beginInsertRows). Фильтр — прокси,
поэтому поиск и отбор по стороне / существу / типу не пересоздают строки.
"""
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QModelIndex, QAbstractItemModel, QSortFilterProxyModel, pyqtSignal
from PyQt6.QtGui import QBrush, QColor, QFont
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QAbstractItemView, QComboBox, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
                             QLineEdit, QTreeView, QVBoxLayout, QWidget)

from combat_log import event_visible

KIND_FILTERS = (
    ('all', 'Все события'),
    ('hit', 'Попадания'),
    ('crit', 'Криты'),
    ('miss', 'Промахи'),
    ('nat1', 'Натуральные 1'),
    ('death', 'Убийства'),
    ('heal', 'Лечение'),
    ('move', 'Движение'),
    ('legendary', 'Легендарные'),
    ('initiative', 'Инициатива'),
    ('conditions', 'Состояния и спасброски'),
)


def display_text(event) -> str:
    compact = ' '.join((event.text or '').split())
    if event.result == 'nat1' and 'натуральн' not in compact.casefold():
        compact = f'{compact} · натуральная 1' if compact else 'натуральная 1'
    return compact


def color_of(event):
    if event.result == 'crit':
        return '#d4a017'
    if event.result == 'nat1':
        return '#8e44ad'
    if event.kind == 'death' or event.result == 'death':
        return '#c0392b'
    if event.result == 'hit':
        return '#d35400'
    if event.result == 'miss':
        return '#7f8c8d'
    if event.kind == 'heal' or event.result == 'heal':
        return '#1e8449'
    if event.legendary or event.kind == 'legendary':
        return '#6c3483'
    if event.kind == 'move':
        return '#2471a3'
    if event.kind in ('round_start', 'initiative', 'round_end'):
        return '#3d4db7'
    if event.kind == 'save':
        return '#0e6655' if event.result == 'success' else '#6c3483'
    if event.kind in ('condition', 'recharge', 'lair'):
        return '#1a5276'
    if event.result == 'victory':
        return '#d4a017'
    return None


def tooltip_of(event) -> str:
    from combat_log import detail_lines
    parts = []
    raw = (event.text or '').strip()
    if raw:
        parts.append(raw)
    parts.extend(detail_lines(event))
    return '\n'.join(parts)


class _Node:
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        self.parent = parent
        self.children = []
        self.event = None
        self.title = ''
        self.color = None
        self.bold = False
        self.tip = ''


class CombatLogModel(QAbstractItemModel):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.root = _Node()

    def index(self, row, column, parent=QModelIndex()):
        node = parent.internalPointer() if parent.isValid() else self.root
        if row < 0 or column != 0 or row >= len(node.children):
            return QModelIndex()
        return self.createIndex(row, column, node.children[row])

    def parent(self, index):
        if not index.isValid():
            return QModelIndex()
        node = index.internalPointer()
        if node is None or node.parent is None or node.parent is self.root:
            return QModelIndex()
        grand = node.parent.parent
        row = grand.children.index(node.parent)
        return self.createIndex(row, 0, node.parent)

    def rowCount(self, parent=QModelIndex()):
        node = parent.internalPointer() if parent.isValid() else self.root
        return len(node.children)

    def columnCount(self, parent=QModelIndex()):
        return 1

    def data(self, index, role=Qt.ItemDataRole.DisplayRole):
        if not index.isValid():
            return None
        node = index.internalPointer()
        if role == Qt.ItemDataRole.DisplayRole:
            return node.title
        if role == Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole:
            return node.event
        if role == Qt.ItemDataRole.ForegroundRole and node.color:
            return QBrush(QColor(node.color))
        if role == Qt.ItemDataRole.ToolTipRole and node.tip:
            return node.tip
        if role == Qt.ItemDataRole.FontRole and node.bold:
            font = QFont()
            font.setBold(True)
            return font
        return None

    def flags(self, index):
        if not index.isValid():
            return Qt.ItemFlag.NoItemFlags
        return Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEnabled | Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable

    def clear(self):
        self.beginResetModel()
        self.root = _Node()
        self.endResetModel()

    def append_group(self, title, events, color='#3d4db7'):
        rows = [event for event in events
                if event is not None and event.kind != 'spacer' and event.text]
        if not rows:
            return
        parent_row = len(self.root.children)
        self.beginInsertRows(QModelIndex(), parent_row, parent_row)
        node = _Node(self.root)
        node.title = title
        node.bold = True
        node.color = color
        for event in rows:
            child = self._leaf(node, event)
            node.children.append(child)
        self.root.children.append(node)
        self.endInsertRows()

    def append_round(self, events):
        number = next((event.round for event in events if getattr(event, 'round', None)), None)
        title = f'Раунд {number}' if number else 'Раунд'
        self.append_group(title, events)

    def append_notes(self, events):
        rows = [event for event in events if event is not None and event.text]
        if not rows:
            return
        first = len(self.root.children)
        self.beginInsertRows(QModelIndex(), first, first + len(rows) - 1)
        for event in rows:
            self.root.children.append(self._leaf(self.root, event))
        self.endInsertRows()

    @staticmethod
    def _leaf(parent, event):
        node = _Node(parent)
        node.event = event
        node.title = display_text(event)
        node.color = color_of(event)
        node.bold = event.result == 'crit' or event.kind == 'death'
        node.tip = tooltip_of(event)
        return node


class LogFilterProxy(QSortFilterProxyModel):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.side = None
        self.creature_id = None
        self.kind = 'all'
        self.query = ''

    def filterAcceptsRow(self, source_row, source_parent):
        model = self.sourceModel()
        index = model.index(source_row, 0, source_parent)
        if self._row_ok(index):
            return True
        # Заголовок раунда остаётся, если под ним есть хоть одна подходящая строка.
        for row in range(model.rowCount(index)):
            if self.filterAcceptsRow(row, index):
                return True
        return False

    def _row_ok(self, index):
        event = index.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        return event_visible(
            event, side=self.side, creature_id=self.creature_id,
            kind=self.kind, query=self.query)


class CombatLogPanel(QWidget):
    entry_activated = pyqtSignal(object)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._views = []
        self._quiet = False
        self.model = CombatLogModel(self)
        self.proxy = LogFilterProxy(self)
        self.proxy.setSourceModel(self.model)

        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        lay.setSpacing(6)
        cap = QLabel('Журнал боя')
        cap.setProperty('role', 'title')
        lay.addWidget(cap)

        filters = QHBoxLayout()
        filters.setSpacing(6)
        self.side = QComboBox()
        self.creature = QComboBox()
        self.kind = QComboBox()
        for box in (self.side, self.creature, self.kind):
            box.setSizeAdjustPolicy(
                QComboBox.SizeAdjustPolicy.AdjustToMinimumContentsLengthWithIcon)
            box.setMinimumContentsLength(12)
        self.kind.setMinimumWidth(230)
        self.kind.setMaximumWidth(280)
        self.side.setMaximumWidth(180)
        for key, label in KIND_FILTERS:
            self.kind.addItem(label, key)
        self.search = QLineEdit()
        self.search.setPlaceholderText('Поиск по журналу')
        self.search.setClearButtonEnabled(True)
        filters.addWidget(self.side)
        filters.addWidget(self.creature)
        filters.addWidget(self.kind)
        filters.addWidget(self.search, 1)
        lay.addLayout(filters)

        self.view = self._make_view()
        lay.addWidget(self.view, 1)
        self.set_roster([], [])

        self.side.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._push_filter)
        self.creature.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._push_filter)
        self.kind.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._push_filter)
        self.search.textChanged.connect(self._push_filter)

    def _make_view(self):
        view = QTreeView()
        view.setModel(self.proxy)
        view.setHeaderHidden(True)
        view.setUniformRowHeights(True)
        view.setAnimated(False)
        view.setIndentation(14)
        view.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.SelectionMode.SingleSelection)
        view.setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        font = QFont('DejaVu Sans Mono', 9)
        font.setStyleHint(QFont.StyleHint.Monospace)
        view.setFont(font)
        view.setStyleSheet(
            'QTreeView::item:selected { background: #5865f2; color: #ffffff; }')
        self._views.append(view)
        view.selectionModel().currentChanged.connect(self._activate_current)
        return view

    def attach_mirror(self, layout):
        """Второе дерево на той же модели: клики на карте не пересобирают журнал."""
        note = QLabel('Журнал боя — клик центрирует токены. '
                      'Фильтры те же, что на вкладке «Битва».')
        note.setProperty('role', 'muted')
        note.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(note)
        view = self._make_view()
        layout.addWidget(view, 1)
        return view

    def set_roster(self, sides, side_names):
        self._quiet = True
        self.side.blockSignals(True)
        self.creature.blockSignals(True)
        self.side.clear()
        self.side.addItem('Все стороны', None)
        for index, name in enumerate(side_names):
            self.side.addItem(name, index)
        self.creature.clear()
        self.creature.addItem('Все существа', None)
        for side in sides or []:
            for enemy in side:
                self.creature.addItem(enemy.name, id(enemy))
        self.side.setCurrentIndex(0)
        self.creature.setCurrentIndex(0)
        self.kind.blockSignals(True)
        self.kind.setCurrentIndex(0)
        self.kind.blockSignals(False)
        self.search.blockSignals(True)
        self.search.clear()
        self.search.blockSignals(False)
        self.side.blockSignals(False)
        self.creature.blockSignals(False)
        self.proxy.side = None
        self.proxy.creature_id = None
        self.proxy.kind = 'all'
        self.proxy.query = ''
        self.proxy.invalidate()
        self._quiet = False

    def set_kind(self, key):
        index = self.kind.findData(key)
        if index >= 0:
            self.kind.setCurrentIndex(index)

    def set_search(self, text):
        self.search.setText(text)

    def _push_filter(self, *_args):
        if self._quiet:
            return
        self.proxy.side = _as_int(self.side.currentData())
        self.proxy.creature_id = _as_int(self.creature.currentData())
        self.proxy.kind = self.kind.currentData() or 'all'
        self.proxy.query = self.search.text()
        self._quiet = True
        self.proxy.invalidate()
        for view in self._views:
            view.expandAll()
        self._quiet = False

    def append_round(self, events):
        self.model.append_round(events)
        self._reveal_last()

    def add_note(self, text):
        from combat_log import CombatEvent
        events = []
        for line in str(text).splitlines():
            if line.strip():
                events.append(CombatEvent(kind='note', text=line))
        self.model.append_notes(events)
        self._reveal_last()
        return events

    def clear(self):
        self.model.clear()

    def load_events(self, events):
        """Пересобирает дерево из плоского журнала: раунд — папка, заметки — строки.

        Тихий режим, чтобы перерисовка не считалась кликом по записи.
        """
        self._quiet = True
        self.model.clear()
        batch = []
        kind = None

        def flush():
            nonlocal batch, kind
            if not batch:
                return
            if kind == 'note':
                self.model.append_notes(batch)
            else:
                self.model.append_round(batch)
            batch = []

        for event in events or []:
            if event is None:
                continue
            key = 'note' if event.kind == 'note' or event.round is None else event.round
            if batch and key != kind:
                flush()
            kind = key
            batch.append(event)
        flush()
        self._reveal_last()
        for view in self._views:
            view.expandAll()
        self._quiet = False

    def _reveal_last(self):
        if self.model.rowCount() == 0:
            return
        source = self.model.index(self.model.rowCount() - 1, 0)
        proxy_index = self.proxy.mapFromSource(source)
        for view in self._views:
            if self.model.rowCount(source):
                view.expand(proxy_index)
            view.scrollTo(proxy_index, QAbstractItemView.ScrollHint.PositionAtBottom)

    def _activate_current(self, current, _previous):
        if self._quiet or not current.isValid():
            return
        event = current.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if event is not None:
            self.entry_activated.emit(event)

    def visible_events(self):
        found = []

        def walk(parent):
            for row in range(self.proxy.rowCount(parent)):
                index = self.proxy.index(row, 0, parent)
                event = index.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
                if event is not None:
                    found.append(event)
                walk(index)

        walk(QModelIndex())
        return found


def _as_int(value):
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        return int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None



==================== qt_editors.py ====================

"""Qt-редакторы: атака (с флагом магичности), характеристики, существо, создатель стороны."""
import re

from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGridLayout,
                             QLabel, QLineEdit, QSpinBox, QComboBox, QPushButton,
                             QListWidget, QPlainTextEdit, QToolButton, QScrollArea,
                             QCheckBox, QMessageBox, QStackedWidget)

from factory import EnemyFactory

ABILITY_ROWS = [('str', 'СИЛ'), ('dex', 'ЛОВ'), ('con', 'ТЕЛ'),
                ('int', 'ИНТ'), ('wis', 'МДР'), ('cha', 'ХАР')]
DICT_FIELDS = [('saves', 'Спасброски'), ('skills', 'Навыки'), ('senses', 'Чувства')]
LIST_FIELDS = [('vulnerabilities', 'Уязвимости к урону'),
               ('resistances', 'Сопротивления урону'),
               ('immunities', 'Иммунитеты к урону'),
               ('condition_immunities', 'Иммунитеты к условиям'),
               ('languages', 'Языки')]
SCALAR_FIELDS = [('cr', 'Опасность (CR)'), ('prof_bonus', 'Бонус мастерства'),
                 ('size', 'Размер'), ('type', 'Тип')]
DICE = ['d4', 'd6', 'd8', 'd10', 'd12', 'd20']
STRATS = ['случайная', 'агрессивная', 'защитная', 'поддержка']
CHAR_KEYS = ('ability_scores', 'ability_mods', 'saves', 'skills', 'senses',
             'vulnerabilities', 'resistances', 'immunities', 'condition_immunities',
             'languages', 'cr', 'prof_bonus', 'size', 'type', 'traits')


def _int(s):
    s = (s or '').strip()
    if not s:
        return None
    try:
        return int(s)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def parse_pairs(seg):
    res = {}
    for part in [p.strip() for p in seg.replace('\n', ',').split(',') if p.strip()]:
        m = re.match(r'^(.+?)\s*[:\s]\s*([+-]?\d+)$', part)
        if m:
            res[m.group(1).strip()] = m.group(2)
        elif ' ' in part:
            k, v = part.rsplit(' ', 1)
            res[k.strip()] = v.strip()
    return res


def parse_list(seg):
    return [p.strip() for p in seg.replace('\n', ',').split(',') if p.strip()]


def parse_traits(seg):
    out = []
    for line in seg.split('\n'):
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        m = re.match(r'^([^.!?:]{1,60}?)\.\s+(.*)$', line)
        if m:
            out.append({'name': m.group(1).strip(' *'), 'desc': m.group(2).strip()})
        else:
            out.append({'name': line, 'desc': ''})
    return out


def attack_line(a):
    mark = ' ✦маг.' if a.get('magical') else ''
    return (f"{a['name']}{mark} ({a['attack_bonus']:+d}, {a['damage_dice_count']}"
            f"{a['damage_dice_type']}{a['damage_modifier']:+d}) "
            f"{a.get('damage_type') or '—'} · {a.get('range_ft', 5)} фт")


def attack_dict(a):
    data = {'name': a.name, 'attack_bonus': a.attack_bonus,
            'damage_dice_count': a.damage_dice_count,
            'damage_dice_type': a.damage_dice_type,
            'damage_modifier': a.damage_modifier,
            'damage_type': getattr(a, 'damage_type', ''),
            'range_ft': getattr(a, 'range_ft', 5),
            'magical': bool(getattr(a, 'magical', False))}
    extra = getattr(a, 'extra_damage', None)
    if extra:
        data['extra_damage'] = [dict(part) for part in extra]
    return data


class Section(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, title, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 4, 0, 0)
        lay.setSpacing(2)
        head = QHBoxLayout()
        self.arrow = QToolButton()
        self.arrow.setCheckable(True)
        self.arrow.setArrowType(Qt.ArrowType.RightArrow)
        cap = QToolButton()
        cap.setText(title)
        cap.setCheckable(True)
        cap.setStyleSheet('font-weight: bold; font-size: 11pt;')
        cap.toggled.connect(self.arrow.setChecked)
        self.arrow.toggled.connect(cap.setChecked)
        self.arrow.toggled.connect(self._toggle)
        head.addWidget(self.arrow)
        head.addWidget(cap)
        head.addStretch()
        lay.addLayout(head)
        self.body = QWidget()
        self.body.setVisible(False)
        self.body_lay = QVBoxLayout(self.body)
        self.body_lay.setContentsMargins(18, 0, 0, 4)
        lay.addWidget(self.body)

    def _toggle(self, on):
        self.arrow.setArrowType(Qt.ArrowType.DownArrow if on else Qt.ArrowType.RightArrow)
        self.body.setVisible(on)


class AttackDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, init=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle('Атака')
        self.resize(400, 470)
        init = dict(init or {'name': 'Атака', 'attack_bonus': 0, 'damage_dice_count': 1,
                             'damage_dice_type': 'd6', 'damage_modifier': 0,
                             'damage_type': '', 'range_ft': 5, 'magical': False})
        self._kept_extra = list(init.get('extra_damage') or [])
        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.name = QLineEdit(init['name'])
        self.dtype = QLineEdit(init.get('damage_type', ''))
        lay.addWidget(QLabel('Название'))
        lay.addWidget(self.name)
        lay.addWidget(QLabel('Тип урона'))
        lay.addWidget(self.dtype)
        self.spins = {}
        for key, lab, lo, hi in (('attack_bonus', 'Бонус к попаданию', -10, 30),
                                 ('damage_dice_count', 'Кол-во костей', 1, 20),
                                 ('damage_modifier', 'Модификатор урона', -20, 50),
                                 ('range_ft', 'Дальность (фт)', 5, 600)):
            lay.addWidget(QLabel(lab))
            s = QSpinBox()
            s.setRange(lo, hi)
            s.setValue(int(init.get(key, 0)))
            self.spins[key] = s
            lay.addWidget(s)
        lay.addWidget(QLabel('Тип кости'))
        self.dice = QComboBox()
        self.dice.addItems(DICE)
        self.dice.setCurrentText(init.get('damage_dice_type', 'd6'))
        lay.addWidget(self.dice)
        self.magical = QCheckBox('✦ Магическое оружие (пробивает иммунитет к немагическому)')
        self.magical.setChecked(bool(init.get('magical', False)))
        lay.addWidget(self.magical)
        lay.addStretch()
        row = QHBoxLayout()
        cancel = QPushButton('Отмена')
        cancel.setProperty('variant', 'outline')
        cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        ok = QPushButton('Сохранить')
        ok.setProperty('variant', 'primary')
        ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        row.addStretch()
        row.addWidget(cancel)
        row.addWidget(ok)
        lay.addLayout(row)

    def data(self):
        d = {'name': self.name.text().strip() or 'Атака',
             'damage_type': self.dtype.text().strip(),
             'damage_dice_type': self.dice.currentText(),
             'magical': self.magical.isChecked()}
        d.update({k: s.value() for k, s in self.spins.items()})
        if self._kept_extra:
            d['extra_damage'] = self._kept_extra
        return d


class CharacteristicsEditor(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, initial=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.entries, self.texts = {}, {}

        stats = Section('📊 Характеристики')
        lay.addWidget(stats)
        grid = QGridLayout()
        for r, (key, label) in enumerate(ABILITY_ROWS):
            lab = QLabel(label)
            lab.setProperty('role', 'accent')
            grid.addWidget(lab, r, 0)
            sc, md = QLineEdit(), QLineEdit()
            sc.setFixedWidth(56)
            md.setFixedWidth(56)
            grid.addWidget(sc, r, 1)
            grid.addWidget(QLabel('('), r, 2)
            grid.addWidget(md, r, 3)
            grid.addWidget(QLabel(')'), r, 4)
            self.entries['score_' + key] = sc
            self.entries['mod_' + key] = md
        grid.setColumnStretch(5, 1)
        stats.body_lay.addLayout(grid)
        for key, label in DICT_FIELDS:
            stats.body_lay.addWidget(QLabel(label + ':'))
            t = QPlainTextEdit()
            t.setFixedHeight(46)
            t.setPlaceholderText('Сил +3, Лов +2')
            self.texts[key] = t
            stats.body_lay.addWidget(t)

        df = Section('🛡 Особенности и защита')
        lay.addWidget(df)
        sg = QGridLayout()
        for i, (key, label) in enumerate(SCALAR_FIELDS):
            sg.addWidget(QLabel(label + ':'), i // 2, (i % 2) * 2)
            e = QLineEdit()
            e.setFixedWidth(120)
            self.entries[key] = e
            sg.addWidget(e, i // 2, (i % 2) * 2 + 1)
        sg.setColumnStretch(4, 1)
        df.body_lay.addLayout(sg)
        for key, label in LIST_FIELDS:
            df.body_lay.addWidget(QLabel(label + ':'))
            t = QPlainTextEdit()
            t.setFixedHeight(34)
            t.setPlaceholderText('огонь, холод  ·  либо: дробящий от немагического оружия')
            self.texts[key] = t
            df.body_lay.addWidget(t)
        df.body_lay.addWidget(QLabel('Особенности (Имя. Описание — по строкам):'))
        t = QPlainTextEdit()
        t.setFixedHeight(90)
        t.setPlaceholderText('Магическое оружие. Атаки оружием считаются магическими.')
        self.texts['traits'] = t
        df.body_lay.addWidget(t)
        self.set_data(initial or {})

    def set_data(self, d):
        sc, md = d.get('ability_scores') or {}, d.get('ability_mods') or {}
        for key, _ in ABILITY_ROWS:
            self.entries['score_' + key].setText('' if key not in sc else str(sc[key]))
            v = md.get(key)
            self.entries['mod_' + key].setText('' if v is None else
                                               (f'+{v}' if v >= 0 else str(v)))
        for key, _ in SCALAR_FIELDS:
            v = d.get(key)
            self.entries[key].setText('' if v is None else str(v))
        for key, _ in DICT_FIELDS:
            self.texts[key].setPlainText(', '.join(
                f'{k} {v}'.strip() for k, v in (d.get(key) or {}).items()))
        for key, _ in LIST_FIELDS:
            self.texts[key].setPlainText(', '.join(str(x) for x in (d.get(key) or [])))
        self.texts['traits'].setPlainText('\n'.join(
            f"{t.get('name', '')}. {t.get('desc', '')}".strip('. ')
            for t in (d.get('traits') or [])))

    def get_data(self):
        out = {'ability_scores': {}, 'ability_mods': {}, 'saves': {}, 'skills': {},
               'senses': {}, 'vulnerabilities': [], 'resistances': [], 'immunities': [],
               'condition_immunities': [], 'languages': [], 'cr': None,
               'prof_bonus': None, 'size': None, 'type': None, 'traits': []}
        for key, _ in ABILITY_ROWS:
            s = _int(self.entries['score_' + key].text())
            m = _int(self.entries['mod_' + key].text())
            if s is not None:
                out['ability_scores'][key] = s
            if m is not None:
                out['ability_mods'][key] = m
        for key, _ in SCALAR_FIELDS:
            out[key] = self.entries[key].text().strip() or None
        for key, _ in DICT_FIELDS:
            out[key] = parse_pairs(self.texts[key].toPlainText())
        for key, _ in LIST_FIELDS:
            out[key] = parse_list(self.texts[key].toPlainText())
        out['traits'] = parse_traits(self.texts['traits'].toPlainText())
        return out


class AttackListEditor(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, attacks=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.attacks = [dict(a) for a in (attacks or [])]
        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.box = QListWidget()
        self.box.setFixedHeight(110)
        self.box.itemDoubleClicked.connect(lambda *_: self.edit())
        lay.addWidget(self.box)
        row = QHBoxLayout()
        for text, fn in (('＋ атака', self.add), ('✎ изменить', self.edit),
                         ('✕ удалить', self.remove)):
            b = QPushButton(text)
            b.setProperty('variant', 'outline')
            b.clicked.connect(fn)
            row.addWidget(b)
        row.addStretch()
        lay.addLayout(row)
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self):
        self.box.clear()
        for a in self.attacks:
            self.box.addItem(attack_line(a))

    def add(self):
        d = AttackDialog(self)
        if d.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            self.attacks.append(d.data())
            self.refresh()

    def edit(self):
        r = self.box.currentRow()
        if r < 0:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, 'Внимание', 'Выберите атаку!')
            return
        d = AttackDialog(self, self.attacks[r])
        if d.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            self.attacks[r] = d.data()
            self.refresh()

    def remove(self):
        r = self.box.currentRow()
        if r >= 0:
            del self.attacks[r]
            self.refresh()


def _scroll(inner):
    sa = QScrollArea()
    sa.setWidgetResizable(True)
    sa.setWidget(inner)
    return sa


class CreatureEditDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent, enemies):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.enemies = enemies
        e0 = enemies[0]
        self.setWindowTitle(f'Редактирование {len(enemies)} существ')
        self.resize(640, 800)
        outer = QVBoxLayout(self)
        inner = QWidget()
        lay = QVBoxLayout(inner)
        title = QLabel(f'Правки применятся ко всем выбранным: {len(enemies)}')
        title.setProperty('role', 'title')
        lay.addWidget(title)

        grid = QGridLayout()
        self.name = QLineEdit(e0.name)
        grid.addWidget(QLabel('Имя:'), 0, 0)
        grid.addWidget(self.name, 0, 1)
        self.hp = QSpinBox(); self.hp.setRange(1, 2000); self.hp.setValue(e0.max_hp)
        self.ac = QSpinBox(); self.ac.setRange(1, 40); self.ac.setValue(e0.ac)
        self.spd = QSpinBox(); self.spd.setRange(0, 300); self.spd.setValue(getattr(e0, 'speed', 30))
        self.regen = QSpinBox(); self.regen.setRange(0, 200); self.regen.setValue(getattr(e0, 'regen', 0))
        for r, (lab, w) in enumerate((('Хиты:', self.hp), ('Класс доспеха:', self.ac),
                                      ('Скорость (фт):', self.spd),
                                      ('Регенерация:', self.regen)), start=1):
            grid.addWidget(QLabel(lab), r, 0)
            grid.addWidget(w, r, 1)
        grid.setColumnStretch(2, 1)
        lay.addLayout(grid)

        lay.addWidget(QLabel('Атаки (двойной клик — изменить):'))
        self.atk = AttackListEditor(attacks=[attack_dict(a) for a in e0.attacks])
        lay.addWidget(self.atk)
        self.chars = CharacteristicsEditor(initial={k: getattr(e0, k, None) for k in CHAR_KEYS})
        lay.addWidget(self.chars)
        from conditions import MANUAL_IDS, active_conditions, condition_name, exhaustion_level
        lay.addWidget(QLabel('Состояния (висят, пока не сняты):'))
        grid_c = QGridLayout()
        present = {item.get('id') for item in active_conditions(e0) if isinstance(item, dict)}
        self.cond_boxes = {}
        for index, condition_id in enumerate(MANUAL_IDS):
            box = QCheckBox(condition_name(condition_id))
            box.setChecked(condition_id in present)
            self.cond_boxes[condition_id] = box
            grid_c.addWidget(box, index // 3, index % 3)
        lay.addLayout(grid_c)
        exh = QHBoxLayout()
        exh.addWidget(QLabel('Истощение (0–6):'))
        self.exhaustion = QSpinBox()
        self.exhaustion.setRange(0, 6)
        self.exhaustion.setValue(exhaustion_level(e0))
        exh.addWidget(self.exhaustion)
        exh.addStretch()
        lay.addLayout(exh)
        lay.addStretch()
        outer.addWidget(_scroll(inner), 1)

        row = QHBoxLayout()
        cancel = QPushButton('Отмена')
        cancel.setProperty('variant', 'outline')
        cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        save = QPushButton('💾 Сохранить')
        save.setProperty('variant', 'primary')
        save.clicked.connect(self.apply)
        row.addStretch()
        row.addWidget(cancel)
        row.addWidget(save)
        outer.addLayout(row)

    def apply(self):
        from entities import Attack
        if not self.atk.attacks:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, 'Внимание', 'Нужна хотя бы одна атака!')
            return
        from conditions import sync_manual_conditions
        chars = self.chars.get_data()
        marked = [cid for cid, box in self.cond_boxes.items() if box.isChecked()]
        for e in self.enemies:
            e.name = self.name.text().strip() or e.name
            e.max_hp = self.hp.value()
            e.hp = self.hp.value()
            e.ac = self.ac.value()
            e.speed = self.spd.value()
            e.regen = self.regen.value()
            e.alive = e.hp > 0
            previous = {attack.name: attack for attack in e.attacks}
            built = []
            for raw in self.atk.attacks:
                old = previous.get(raw['name'])
                extra = raw.get('extra_damage')
                riders = raw.get('riders')
                if extra is None and old is not None:
                    extra = getattr(old, 'extra_damage', None)
                if riders is None and old is not None:
                    riders = getattr(old, 'riders', None)
                built.append(Attack(
                    raw['name'], raw['attack_bonus'], raw['damage_dice_count'],
                    raw['damage_dice_type'], raw['damage_modifier'],
                    raw.get('damage_type', ''), raw.get('range_ft', 5),
                    raw.get('magical', False), extra, riders))
            e.attacks = built
            for k, v in chars.items():
                setattr(e, k, v)
            sync_manual_conditions(e, marked, self.exhaustion.value())
        self.accept()


class SideCreatorDialog(QDialog):
    ready = pyqtSignal(dict)

    def __init__(self, parent, types, mode='create', current_type=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.types = types
        self.mode = mode
        self.multiattack_data = None
        self.setWindowTitle('Кого ведём в бой?' if mode == 'create' else 'Изменить состав')
        self.resize(620, 780)
        outer = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.stack = QStackedWidget()
        outer.addWidget(self.stack, 1)

        choice = QWidget()
        cl = QVBoxLayout(choice)
        cl.addWidget(QLabel('Тактика отряда:'))
        self.strat = QComboBox()
        self.strat.addItems(STRATS)
        cl.addWidget(self.strat)
        hint = QLabel('Выберите, откуда взять существ:')
        hint.setProperty('role', 'title')
        cl.addWidget(hint)
        b1 = QPushButton('📖  Импорт из бестиария\n(тянем статблок с dnd.su)')
        b1.setMinimumHeight(66)
        b1.clicked.connect(self.start_import)
        b2 = QPushButton('🛠  Своё существо\n(характеристики вручную)')
        b2.setMinimumHeight(66)
        b2.clicked.connect(lambda: self.stack.setCurrentIndex(1))
        cl.addWidget(b1)
        cl.addWidget(b2)
        if current_type:
            cur = QLabel(f'Текущий состав: {current_type}')
            cur.setProperty('role', 'muted')
            cl.addWidget(cur)
        cl.addStretch()
        self.stack.addWidget(choice)

        form = QWidget()
        fl = QVBoxLayout(form)
        prow = QHBoxLayout()
        self.preset = QComboBox()
        self.preset.addItems(types)
        load = QPushButton('Загрузить пресет в поля')
        load.setProperty('variant', 'outline')
        load.clicked.connect(self.load_preset)
        prow.addWidget(QLabel('Пресет:'))
        prow.addWidget(self.preset, 1)
        prow.addWidget(load)
        fl.addLayout(prow)

        inner = QWidget()
        il = QVBoxLayout(inner)
        g = QGridLayout()
        self.cname = QLineEdit('')
        g.addWidget(QLabel('Имя существа:'), 0, 0)
        g.addWidget(self.cname, 0, 1)
        self.chp = QSpinBox(); self.chp.setRange(1, 2000); self.chp.setValue(10)
        self.cac = QSpinBox(); self.cac.setRange(1, 40); self.cac.setValue(10)
        self.cspd = QSpinBox(); self.cspd.setRange(0, 300); self.cspd.setValue(30)
        self.cregen = QSpinBox(); self.cregen.setRange(0, 200); self.cregen.setValue(0)
        for r, (lab, w) in enumerate((('Хиты:', self.chp), ('Класс доспеха:', self.cac),
                                      ('Скорость (фт):', self.cspd),
                                      ('Регенерация:', self.cregen)), start=1):
            g.addWidget(QLabel(lab), r, 0)
            g.addWidget(w, r, 1)
        g.setColumnStretch(2, 1)
        il.addLayout(g)
        il.addWidget(QLabel('Атаки:'))
        self.atk = AttackListEditor()
        il.addWidget(self.atk)
        self.chars = CharacteristicsEditor()
        il.addWidget(self.chars)
        il.addStretch()
        fl.addWidget(_scroll(inner), 1)

        bottom = QHBoxLayout()
        back = QPushButton('← Назад')
        back.setProperty('variant', 'outline')
        back.clicked.connect(lambda: self.stack.setCurrentIndex(0))
        create = QPushButton('✅ Создать сторону')
        create.setProperty('variant', 'primary')
        create.clicked.connect(self.confirm)
        bottom.addWidget(back)
        bottom.addStretch()
        bottom.addWidget(create)
        fl.addLayout(bottom)
        self.stack.addWidget(form)

    def start_import(self):
        from qt_bestiary import BestiaryDialog
        d = BestiaryDialog(self)
        d.imported.connect(self._imported)
        d.exec()

    def _imported(self, type_name):
        self.ready.emit({'army_type': type_name, 'strategy': self.strat.currentText()})
        self.accept()

    def load_preset(self):
        tmp = EnemyFactory.create_enemy(self.preset.currentText(), '_preset_', 0)
        self.multiattack_data = getattr(tmp, 'multiattack', None)
        self.cname.setText(self.preset.currentText())
        self.chp.setValue(tmp.max_hp)
        self.cac.setValue(tmp.ac)
        self.cspd.setValue(getattr(tmp, 'speed', 30))
        self.cregen.setValue(getattr(tmp, 'regen', 0))
        self.atk.attacks = [attack_dict(a) for a in tmp.attacks]
        self.atk.refresh()
        self.chars.set_data({k: getattr(tmp, k, None) for k in CHAR_KEYS})
        self._kept_card = {
            'legendary_actions': getattr(tmp, 'legendary_actions', None),
            'legendary_resistance': getattr(tmp, 'legendary_resistance', None),
            'lair_actions': getattr(tmp, 'lair_actions', None),
            'special_actions': list(getattr(tmp, 'special_actions', None) or []),
            'bonus_actions': list(getattr(tmp, 'bonus_actions', None) or []),
            'reactions': list(getattr(tmp, 'reactions', None) or []),
        }

    def confirm(self):
        name = self.cname.text().strip()
        if not name:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, 'Внимание', 'Дайте существу имя!')
            return
        if not self.atk.attacks:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, 'Внимание', 'Добавьте хотя бы одну атаку!')
            return
        kept = {key: value for key, value in getattr(self, '_kept_card', {}).items() if value}
        EnemyFactory.register_type(name, hp=self.chp.value(), ac=self.cac.value(),
                                   attacks=self.atk.attacks, speed=self.cspd.value(),
                                   regen=self.cregen.value(),
                                   multiattack=self.multiattack_data,
                                   **self.chars.get_data(), **kept)
        self.ready.emit({'army_type': name, 'strategy': self.strat.currentText()})
        self.accept()


==================== qt_map_spike.py ====================

"""Пробный просмотр карты боя поверх ядра (не основное окно).

Что показывает:
  1. Модель поля (BattleMap) и фабрика (EnemyFactory) не знают о GUI.
  2. Зум колесом, панорамирование и сглаживание даёт QGraphicsView.

Запуск из корня проекта:  python qt_map_spike.py
Управление: колесо - зум к курсору, тянуть пустое место - панорама,
клик по токену - данные существа внизу окна.
Основное приложение: python main.py
"""
import sys

from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt6.QtGui import QBrush, QColor, QFont, QPainter, QPen
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QApplication, QGraphicsEllipseItem, QGraphicsScene,
                             QGraphicsView, QLabel, QVBoxLayout, QWidget)

from battle_map_model import BattleMap
from factory import EnemyFactory

CELL = 28
SIDE_COLORS = ['#e74c3c', '#3498db', '#2ecc71', '#f1c40f', '#9b59b6', '#e67e22']
BG = '#2b2d31'
GRID = '#383b40'


class MapView(QGraphicsView):
    """Холст: зум колесом к курсору, панорама по пустому месту, клик по токену."""

    def __init__(self, scene, on_pick):
        super().__init__(scene)
        self.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        self.setTransformationAnchor(
            QGraphicsView.ViewportAnchor.AnchorUnderMouse)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(QColor(BG))
        self._on_pick = on_pick
        self._panning = False
        self._pan_last = None
        self._selected = None

    def wheelEvent(self, event):
        factor = 1.15 if event.angleDelta().y() > 0 else 1.0 / 1.15
        self.scale(factor, factor)          # зум к курсору (якорь выше)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            item = self.itemAt(event.pos())
            if item is not None:
                item = item.topLevelItem()  # клик по подписи = клик по токену
                if item.data(0) is not None:
                    self._select(item)
                    return
            self._panning = True            # пустое место - панорама
            self._pan_last = event.pos()
            self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ClosedHandCursor)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._panning:
            d = event.pos() - self._pan_last
            self._pan_last = event.pos()
            self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(
                self.horizontalScrollBar().value() - d.x())
            self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(
                self.verticalScrollBar().value() - d.y())
            return
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        self._panning = False
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ArrowCursor)
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

    def _select(self, item):
        if self._selected is not None:
            self._selected.setPen(QPen(QColor('black'), 1))
        self._selected = item
        item.setPen(QPen(QColor('white'), 2))
        self._on_pick(item.data(0))


def build_scene(bm, sides):
    scene = QGraphicsScene(0, 0, bm.width * CELL, bm.height * CELL)
    scene.setBackgroundBrush(QColor(BG))
    grid_pen = QPen(QColor(GRID), 1)
    for x in range(bm.width + 1):
        scene.addLine(x * CELL, 0, x * CELL, bm.height * CELL, grid_pen)
    for y in range(bm.height + 1):
        scene.addLine(0, y * CELL, bm.width * CELL, y * CELL, grid_pen)

    font = QFont('Arial', 8, QFont.Weight.Bold)
    for i, side in enumerate(sides):
        color = QColor(SIDE_COLORS[i % len(SIDE_COLORS)])
        for enemy in side:
            pos = bm.get_pos(enemy)
            if not pos:
                continue
            r = CELL // 2 - 3
            el = QGraphicsEllipseItem(-r, -r, 2 * r, 2 * r)
            el.setPos(pos[0] * CELL + CELL // 2, pos[1] * CELL + CELL // 2)
            el.setBrush(QBrush(color if enemy.alive else QColor('#555555')))
            el.setPen(QPen(QColor('black'), 1))
            el.setData(0, enemy)            # токен несёт своё существо
            scene.addItem(el)
            txt = scene.addSimpleText(enemy.name.split('_')[-1][:2], font)
            txt.setParentItem(el)
            txt.setBrush(QBrush(QColor('white')))
            tb = txt.boundingRect()
            txt.setPos(-tb.width() / 2, -tb.height() / 2)
    return scene


def build_demo():
    bm = BattleMap(width=24, height=16)
    comp = [('гоблин', 10), ('орк', 8), ('скелет', 8)]
    sides = [[EnemyFactory.create_enemy(t, f'Сторона {chr(65 + i)}_{j + 1}', i)
              for j in range(n)] for i, (t, n) in enumerate(comp)]
    bm.formation_placement(sides)
    return bm, sides


def main():
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    bm, sides = build_demo()
    scene = build_scene(bm, sides)
    info = QLabel('Клик по токену - данные существа; колесо - зум; '
                  'тяни пустое место - панорама.')
    info.setStyleSheet('padding: 6px;')

    def on_pick(enemy):
        info.setText(f'{enemy.name}: HP {enemy.hp}/{enemy.max_hp}, AC {enemy.ac}, '
                     f'клетка {bm.get_pos(enemy)}, атак: {len(enemy.attacks)}')

    view = MapView(scene, on_pick)
    win = QWidget()
    lay = QVBoxLayout(win)
    lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
    lay.addWidget(view, 1)
    lay.addWidget(info, 0)
    win.resize(900, 640)
    win.setWindowTitle('Qt-спайк: карта боя на QGraphicsView')
    win.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec())


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


==================== qt_map_view.py ====================

"""Карта боя на QGraphicsView: фон, зум, панорама, токены по размеру существ.

Токен занимает столько клеток, сколько указано в статблоке: крошечный — четверть
клетки, большой — 2×2, огромный — 3×3, громадный — 4×4. Подсветка досягаемости
строится по пятну, а не по одной клетке.
"""
import math
import os

from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal, QRectF, QPointF
from PyQt6.QtGui import (QBrush, QColor, QFont, QImage, QPainter, QPainterPath,
                          QPen, QPixmap, QPolygonF, QRadialGradient)
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QGraphicsEllipseItem, QGraphicsPixmapItem, QGraphicsRectItem,
                             QGraphicsScene, QGraphicsView)

CELL = 28

SIDE_PALETTES = {
    'classic': ['#e74c3c', '#3498db', '#2ecc71', '#f1c40f', '#9b59b6', '#e67e22'],
    'neon':    ['#ff2e63', '#08d9d6', '#a6ff00', '#ff9f1c', '#b14aed', '#ff5d8f'],
    'parchment': ['#a83232', '#2f5d8a', '#4a7c3f', '#b8860b', '#6b4a8a', '#c0662a'],
}

BG_MODES = ['stretch', 'fit', 'cover', 'tile']
IMAGE_EXT = ('.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.bmp', '.webp', '.gif', '.tif', '.tiff')

DEFAULT_OPTS = {
    'cell': 28, 'zoom': 100, 'grid': True, 'reach': True,
    'labels': 'number', 'dead_alpha': 45, 'stroke': 1,
    'glow': True, 'sides': 'classic',
    'bg_opacity': 100, 'bg_mode': 'stretch',
    'attack_lines': True,
}

# Стиль отрезка атаки: цвет, толщина, штрих. Координаты приходят в клетках.
ATTACK_STYLES = {
    'hit': ('#e74c3c', 2.6, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine),
    'crit': ('#f1c40f', 3.8, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine),
    'miss': ('#95a5a6', 1.8, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine),
    'nat1': ('#9b59b6', 2.0, Qt.PenStyle.DotLine),
    'heal': ('#2ecc71', 2.6, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine),
    'move': ('#3498db', 1.8, Qt.PenStyle.DashDotLine),
}


class MapView(QGraphicsView):
    token_clicked = pyqtSignal(object)
    token_menu = pyqtSignal(object, object)
    cell_clicked = pyqtSignal(float, float)
    token_dragged = pyqtSignal(object, float, float)
    image_dropped = pyqtSignal(str)
    aim_moved = pyqtSignal(float, float)

    def __init__(self, palette, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.pal = palette
        self.opts = dict(DEFAULT_OPTS)
        self.setScene(QGraphicsScene(self))
        self.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        self.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.SmoothPixmapTransform)
        self.setTransformationAnchor(QGraphicsView.ViewportAnchor.AnchorUnderMouse)
        self.setDragMode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.NoDrag)
        self.setMinimumHeight(240)
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self._press = None
        self._last = None
        self._dragged = False
        self._token_drag = None
        self._token_press = None
        self._token_moved = False
        self._bm = None
        self._sides = []
        self._selected = None
        self._bg_path = None
        self._bg_pix = None
        self._bg_cache = {}
        self._round_lines = []
        self._focus_lines = []
        self._focus_enemies = []
        self._attack_items = []
        self._focus_items = []
        self._area_cells = []
        self._preview_cells = []
        self._focus_cells = []
        self._area_items = []
        self._aiming = False

    # ---------- фон ----------
    def set_background(self, path):
        pix = QPixmap(path)
        if pix.isNull():
            return False
        self._bg_path, self._bg_pix = path, pix
        self._bg_cache.clear()
        self.rebuild(self._bm, self._sides, self._selected)
        return True

    def clear_background(self):
        self._bg_path = self._bg_pix = None
        self._bg_cache.clear()
        self.rebuild(self._bm, self._sides, self._selected)

    @property
    def background_path(self):
        return self._bg_path

    def background_info(self):
        if self._bg_pix is None or self._bg_pix.isNull():
            return None
        return (os.path.basename(self._bg_path or 'изображение'),
                self._bg_pix.width(), self._bg_pix.height())

    def _scaled_bg(self, w, h, mode):
        W, H = max(1, int(w)), max(1, int(h))
        key = (W, H, mode)
        hit = self._bg_cache.get(key)
        if hit is not None:
            return hit
        aspect = {'stretch': Qt.AspectRatioMode.IgnoreAspectRatio,
                  'fit': Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio,
                  'cover': Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatioByExpanding}[mode]
        pix = self._bg_pix.scaled(W, H, aspect, Qt.TransformationMode.SmoothTransformation)
        if mode == 'cover':
            cx = max(0, (pix.width() - W) // 2)
            cy = max(0, (pix.height() - H) // 2)
            pix = pix.copy(cx, cy, min(W, pix.width()), min(H, pix.height()))
        if len(self._bg_cache) > 12:
            self._bg_cache.clear()
        self._bg_cache[key] = pix
        return pix

    def _draw_background(self, sc, w, h):
        if self._bg_pix is None or self._bg_pix.isNull():
            return
        mode = self.opts['bg_mode']
        op = max(0.05, min(1.0, self.opts['bg_opacity'] / 100.0))
        if mode == 'tile':
            tile = QGraphicsRectItem(0, 0, w, h)
            tile.setBrush(QBrush(self._bg_pix))
            tile.setPen(QPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen))
            tile.setOpacity(op)
            tile.setZValue(-10)
            sc.addItem(tile)
            return
        pix = self._scaled_bg(w, h, mode)
        item = QGraphicsPixmapItem(pix)
        item.setTransformationMode(Qt.TransformationMode.SmoothTransformation)
        item.setOpacity(op)
        item.setZValue(-10)
        item.setPos(0 if mode == 'stretch' else (w - pix.width()) / 2.0,
                    0 if mode == 'stretch' else (h - pix.height()) / 2.0)
        sc.addItem(item)

    # ---------- drag & drop ----------
    def dragEnterEvent(self, e):
        if e.mimeData().hasUrls():
            for u in e.mimeData().urls():
                if u.toLocalFile().lower().endswith(IMAGE_EXT):
                    e.acceptProposedAction()
                    return
        super().dragEnterEvent(e)

    def dragMoveEvent(self, e):
        if e.mimeData().hasUrls():
            e.acceptProposedAction()
            return
        super().dragMoveEvent(e)

    def dropEvent(self, e):
        for u in e.mimeData().urls():
            path = u.toLocalFile()
            if path.lower().endswith(IMAGE_EXT):
                self.image_dropped.emit(path)
                e.acceptProposedAction()
                return
        super().dropEvent(e)

    # ---------- настройки ----------
    def set_opt(self, key, value):
        self.opts[key] = value
        if key == 'zoom':
            self.resetTransform()
            f = max(10, value) / 100.0
            self.scale(f, f)
            return
        if key == 'attack_lines':
            self._paint_attack_lines()
            return
        self.rebuild(self._bm, self._sides, self._selected)

    def reset_opts(self):
        self.opts = dict(DEFAULT_OPTS)
        self.resetTransform()
        self.rebuild(self._bm, self._sides, self._selected)

    def set_palette(self, palette):
        self.pal = palette
        self.rebuild(self._bm, self._sides, self._selected)

    def export_png(self, path):
        rect = self.scene().sceneRect()
        if rect.isEmpty():
            rect = QRectF(0, 0, 400, 300)
        img = QImage(max(1, int(rect.width())), max(1, int(rect.height())),
                     QImage.Format.Format_ARGB32_Premultiplied)
        img.fill(QColor(self.pal['canvas_map']))
        p = QPainter(img)
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.SmoothPixmapTransform)
        self.scene().render(p, QRectF(img.rect()), rect)
        p.end()
        return img.save(path)

    # ---------- навигация ----------
    def wheelEvent(self, e):
        step = 10 if e.angleDelta().y() > 0 else -10
        self.opts['zoom'] = max(25, min(300, self.opts['zoom'] + step))
        self.resetTransform()
        f = self.opts['zoom'] / 100.0
        self.scale(f, f)

    def _token_at(self, pos):
        it = self.itemAt(pos)
        while it is not None and it.data(0) is None and it.parentItem() is not None:
            it = it.parentItem()
        if it is not None and it.data(0) is not None:
            return it.data(0)
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _cell_at(scene_pos, cell):
        return (round(scene_pos.x() / cell * 2) / 2,
                round(scene_pos.y() / cell * 2) / 2)

    def mousePressEvent(self, e):
        if e.button() == Qt.MouseButton.RightButton:
            enemy = self._token_at(e.pos())
            if enemy is not None:
                point = e.globalPosition().toPoint()
                self.token_menu.emit(enemy, point)
                e.accept()
                return
        if e.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            enemy = self._token_at(e.pos())
            if enemy is not None and not self._aiming:
                self._token_drag = enemy
                self._token_press = e.pos()
                self._token_moved = False
                self.token_clicked.emit(enemy)
                e.accept()
                return
            if enemy is not None and self._aiming:
                self.token_clicked.emit(enemy)
                e.accept()
                return
            self._press = e.pos()
            self._last = e.pos()
            self._dragged = False
            self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ClosedHandCursor)
        super().mousePressEvent(e)

    def set_aiming(self, on):
        """Клик и движение мыши выбирают клетку прицела, а не тащат карту."""
        self._aiming = bool(on)
        self.setMouseTracking(bool(on))
        if not on:
            self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ArrowCursor)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, e):
        if self._aiming and self._token_drag is None:
            sp = self.mapToScene(e.pos())
            x, y = self._cell_at(sp, self.opts['cell'])
            self.aim_moved.emit(x, y)
            if self._press is not None:
                e.accept()
                return
        if self._token_drag is not None and self._token_press is not None:
            if (e.pos() - self._token_press).manhattanLength() > 6:
                self._token_moved = True
                self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ClosedHandCursor)
            e.accept()
            return
        if self._press is not None:
            if (e.pos() - self._press).manhattanLength() > 4:
                self._dragged = True
            if self._dragged:
                d = e.pos() - self._last
                self._last = e.pos()
                self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(self.horizontalScrollBar().value() - d.x())
                self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(self.verticalScrollBar().value() - d.y())
            return
        super().mouseMoveEvent(e)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, e):
        if self._token_drag is not None:
            enemy = self._token_drag
            moved = self._token_moved
            press = self._token_press
            self._token_drag = None
            self._token_press = None
            self._token_moved = False
            self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ArrowCursor)
            if moved and press is not None:
                sp = self.mapToScene(e.pos())
                x, y = self._cell_at(sp, self.opts['cell'])
                self.token_dragged.emit(enemy, x, y)
            e.accept()
            return
        if self._press is not None:
            if self._aiming or not self._dragged:
                sp = self.mapToScene(e.pos() if self._aiming else self._press)
                x, y = self._cell_at(sp, self.opts['cell'])
                self.cell_clicked.emit(x, y)
            self._press = None
            if not self._aiming:
                self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ArrowCursor)
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(e)

    # ---------- отрисовка ----------
    def _reach_cells(self, bm, enemy, c):
        pos = bm.get_pos(enemy)
        if not pos:
            return set()
        n = bm.span_of(enemy)
        step = 1 if n == 1 else 2
        left = max(0, enemy.speed - getattr(enemy, 'moved_ft', 0))
        radius_h = int(left // 2.5)
        ax, ay = int(round(pos[0] * 2)), int(round(pos[1] * 2))
        covered = set()
        for dy in range(-radius_h, radius_h + 1, step):
            for dx in range(-radius_h, radius_h + 1, step):
                hx, hy = ax + dx, ay + dy
                if not bm._area_free(hx, hy, n, ignore_id=id(enemy)):
                    continue
                for yy in range(hy, hy + n):
                    for xx in range(hx, hx + n):
                        covered.add((xx, yy))
        return covered

    def set_area_cells(self, cells):
        """Клетки уже сыгранной области. Сцену не пересобирает."""
        self._area_cells = [tuple(cell) for cell in cells or []]
        self._paint_areas()

    def set_preview_cells(self, cells):
        """Живой прицел: клетки, которые накроет выбранное направление."""
        self._preview_cells = [tuple(cell) for cell in cells or []]
        self._paint_areas()

    def set_focus_areas(self, cells):
        """Клетки одной записи журнала поверх области раунда."""
        self._focus_cells = [tuple(cell) for cell in cells or []]
        self._paint_areas()

    def set_round_lines(self, lines):
        """Линии последнего раунда. Снимает прежние стрелки, сцену не чистит."""
        self._round_lines = list(lines or [])
        self._focus_lines = []
        self._focus_enemies = []
        self._paint_attack_lines()
        self._paint_focus_rings()

    def set_focus_lines(self, lines):
        """Подсветка одной записи поверх линий раунда, без пересборки сцены."""
        self._focus_lines = list(lines or [])
        self._paint_attack_lines()

    def focus_creatures(self, enemies):
        """Кольца вокруг существ и центр кадра. Токены не перерисовываются."""
        self._focus_enemies = [enemy for enemy in enemies if enemy is not None]
        self._paint_focus_rings()
        if self._bm is None or not self._focus_enemies:
            return
        rect = None
        cell = self.opts['cell']
        for enemy in self._focus_enemies:
            pos = self._bm.get_pos(enemy)
            if not pos:
                continue
            side = self._bm.cells_of(enemy) * cell
            piece = QRectF(pos[0] * cell, pos[1] * cell, side, side)
            rect = piece if rect is None else rect.united(piece)
        if rect is None:
            return
        margin = cell * 1.5
        self.ensureVisible(rect.adjusted(-margin, -margin, margin, margin), 20, 20)
        self.centerOn(rect.center())

    def _paint_attack_lines(self):
        scene = self.scene()
        for item in self._attack_items:
            scene.removeItem(item)
        self._attack_items = []
        if not self.opts.get('attack_lines', True) or self._bm is None:
            return
        dim = bool(self._focus_lines)
        for spec in self._round_lines:
            self._add_attack_line(spec, 0.35 if dim else 0.95, 1.0)
        for spec in self._focus_lines:
            self._add_attack_line(spec, 1.0, 1.45)

    def _add_attack_line(self, spec, opacity, width_scale):
        cell = self.opts['cell']
        r1 = float(spec.get('r1') or 0.45)
        r2 = float(spec.get('r2') or 0.45)
        x1 = (float(spec['x1']) + r1) * cell
        y1 = (float(spec['y1']) + r1) * cell
        x2 = (float(spec['x2']) + r2) * cell
        y2 = (float(spec['y2']) + r2) * cell
        dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
        length = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        if length < 2:
            return
        ux, uy = dx / length, dy / length
        pad1, pad2 = r1 * cell * 0.92, r2 * cell * 0.92
        if length <= pad1 + pad2 + 4:
            pad1 = pad2 = 0
        sx, sy = x1 + ux * pad1, y1 + uy * pad1
        ex, ey = x2 - ux * pad2, y2 - uy * pad2
        color_hex, width, pen_style = ATTACK_STYLES.get(spec.get('style'), ATTACK_STYLES['hit'])
        color = QColor(color_hex)
        pen = QPen(color, max(1.0, width * width_scale), pen_style)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        line = self.scene().addLine(sx, sy, ex, ey, pen)
        line.setOpacity(opacity)
        line.setZValue(6)
        line.setAcceptedMouseButtons(Qt.MouseButton.NoButton)
        self._attack_items.append(line)
        head = 11 * width_scale
        spread = 4.5 * width_scale
        polygon = QPolygonF([
            QPointF(ex, ey),
            QPointF(ex - ux * head - uy * spread, ey - uy * head + ux * spread),
            QPointF(ex - ux * head + uy * spread, ey - uy * head - ux * spread),
        ])
        arrow = self.scene().addPolygon(polygon, QPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen), QBrush(color))
        arrow.setOpacity(opacity)
        arrow.setZValue(6)
        arrow.setAcceptedMouseButtons(Qt.MouseButton.NoButton)
        self._attack_items.append(arrow)

    def _paint_focus_rings(self):
        scene = self.scene()
        for item in self._focus_items:
            scene.removeItem(item)
        self._focus_items = []
        if self._bm is None:
            return
        cell = self.opts['cell']
        pen = QPen(QColor('#f1c40f'), 3)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        for enemy in self._focus_enemies:
            pos = self._bm.get_pos(enemy)
            if not pos:
                continue
            side = self._bm.cells_of(enemy) * cell
            pad = max(2, side * 0.08)
            ring = scene.addEllipse(
                pos[0] * cell - pad, pos[1] * cell - pad, side + 2 * pad, side + 2 * pad,
                pen, QBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush))
            ring.setZValue(7)
            ring.setAcceptedMouseButtons(Qt.MouseButton.NoButton)
            self._focus_items.append(ring)

    def _paint_areas(self):
        scene = self.scene()
        for item in self._area_items:
            scene.removeItem(item)
        self._area_items = []
        if self._bm is None:
            return
        cell = self.opts['cell']
        layers = (
            (self._area_cells, QColor(230, 126, 34, 110), QColor(192, 86, 12, 180), -1),
            (self._preview_cells, QColor(241, 196, 15, 150), QColor(241, 196, 15, 230), 0),
            (self._focus_cells, QColor(241, 196, 15, 80), QColor(255, 230, 120, 240), 5),
        )
        for cells, fill, edge, z_value in layers:
            pen = QPen(edge, 1.4)
            pen.setCosmetic(True)
            brush = QBrush(fill)
            for cx, cy in cells:
                try:
                    left, top = float(cx) * cell, float(cy) * cell
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    continue
                rect = scene.addRect(left + 1, top + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2, pen, brush)
                rect.setZValue(z_value)
                rect.setAcceptedMouseButtons(Qt.MouseButton.NoButton)
                self._area_items.append(rect)

    def rebuild(self, bm, sides, selected=None):
        lines = list(self._round_lines)
        focus_lines = list(self._focus_lines)
        focus_enemies = [enemy for enemy in self._focus_enemies if enemy is not None]
        area_cells = list(self._area_cells)
        preview_cells = list(self._preview_cells)
        focus_cells = list(self._focus_cells)
        self._attack_items = []
        self._focus_items = []
        self._area_items = []
        self._bm, self._sides, self._selected = bm, sides or [], selected
        sc = self.scene()
        sc.clear()
        self.setBackgroundBrush(QColor(self.pal['canvas_map']))
        self._round_lines = lines
        self._focus_lines = focus_lines
        self._focus_enemies = focus_enemies
        self._area_cells = area_cells
        self._preview_cells = preview_cells
        self._focus_cells = focus_cells
        if bm is None:
            return
        o = self.opts
        c = o['cell']
        w, h = bm.width * c, bm.height * c
        sc.setSceneRect(0, 0, w, h)
        self._draw_background(sc, w, h)

        if o['grid']:
            gc = QColor(self.pal['canvas_grid'])
            if self._bg_pix is not None:
                gc.setAlpha(150)
            gp = QPen(gc, 1)
            for x in range(bm.width + 1):
                sc.addLine(x * c, 0, x * c, h, gp).setZValue(-5)
            for y in range(bm.height + 1):
                sc.addLine(0, y * c, w, y * c, gp).setZValue(-5)
            half = QColor(gc)
            half.setAlpha(70)
            hp = QPen(half, 1, Qt.PenStyle.DotLine)
            for x in range(bm.width):
                sc.addLine(x * c + c / 2, 0, x * c + c / 2, h, hp).setZValue(-6)
            for y in range(bm.height):
                sc.addLine(0, y * c + c / 2, w, y * c + c / 2, hp).setZValue(-6)

        if o['reach'] and selected is not None and selected.alive:
            brush = QBrush(QColor(self.pal['canvas_reach']))
            alpha = 0.5 if self._bg_pix is not None else 1.0
            for hx, hy in self._reach_cells(bm, selected, c):
                r = sc.addRect(hx * c / 2 + 1, hy * c / 2 + 1, c / 2 - 2, c / 2 - 2,
                               QPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen), brush)
                r.setOpacity(alpha)
                r.setZValue(-2)

        colors = SIDE_PALETTES[o['sides']]
        no_pen = QPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
        for i, side in enumerate(self._sides):
            color = QColor(colors[i % len(colors)])
            for en in sorted(side, key=lambda e: (e.alive, bm.cells_of(e))):
                pos = bm.get_pos(en)
                if not pos:
                    continue
                cells = bm.cells_of(en)
                side_px = cells * c
                pad = max(1.5, side_px * 0.09)
                x0, y0 = pos[0] * c + pad, pos[1] * c + pad
                box = side_px - 2 * pad
                cx, cy = pos[0] * c + side_px / 2, pos[1] * c + side_px / 2
                is_sel = en is selected

                if is_sel and o['glow']:
                    R = side_px * 0.95
                    grad = QRadialGradient(cx, cy, R)
                    hot = QColor(self.pal['accent']); hot.setAlpha(165)
                    mid = QColor(self.pal['accent']); mid.setAlpha(80)
                    cold = QColor(self.pal['accent']); cold.setAlpha(0)
                    grad.setColorAt(0.0, hot)
                    grad.setColorAt(0.45, mid)
                    grad.setColorAt(1.0, cold)
                    halo = sc.addEllipse(cx - R, cy - R, 2 * R, 2 * R, no_pen, QBrush(grad))
                    halo.setZValue(1)

                fill = QBrush(color if en.alive else QColor('#555555'))
                pen = QPen(QColor('#ffffff' if is_sel else '#000000'),
                           max(1, o['stroke'] + (1 if is_sel else 0)))
                if cells >= 2:
                    path = QPainterPath()
                    radius = max(3.0, box * 0.14)
                    path.addRoundedRect(QRectF(x0, y0, box, box), radius, radius)
                    item = sc.addPath(path, pen, fill)
                else:
                    item = sc.addEllipse(x0, y0, box, box, pen, fill)
                if not en.alive:
                    item.setOpacity(max(0.1, o['dead_alpha'] / 100.0))
                item.setData(0, en)
                item.setZValue(2 + cells * 0.01)

                if o['labels'] != 'none':
                    txt = (en.name.split('_')[0][:6] if o['labels'] == 'name'
                           else en.name.split('_')[-1][:3])
                    font = QFont('Arial', max(6, int(min(20, box * 0.34))), QFont.Weight.Bold)
                    t = sc.addSimpleText(txt, font)
                    t.setParentItem(item)
                    t.setBrush(QBrush(QColor('#ffffff')))
                    b = t.boundingRect()
                    t.setPos(x0 + (box - b.width()) / 2, y0 + (box - b.height()) / 2)
                    if not en.alive:
                        t.setOpacity(max(0.1, o['dead_alpha'] / 100.0))
                self._paint_condition_badges(item, en, x0, y0, box)
        self._paint_areas()
        self._paint_attack_lines()
        self._paint_focus_rings()

    def _paint_condition_badges(self, item, enemy, x0, y0, box):
        from conditions import (active_conditions, condition_color, condition_short,
                                condition_tooltip)
        item.setToolTip(condition_tooltip(enemy))
        badges = [cond for cond in active_conditions(enemy) if isinstance(cond, dict)]
        if not badges:
            return
        font = QFont('Arial', max(6, int(min(11, box * 0.2))), QFont.Weight.Bold)
        gap = max(1.0, box * 0.04)
        badge_w = max(16.0, min(box * 0.36, 30.0))
        badge_h = max(11.0, min(box * 0.24, 16.0))
        total = len(badges) * badge_w + max(0, len(badges) - 1) * gap
        left = x0 + max(0.0, (box - total) / 2)
        top = y0 + box - badge_h - 1
        for index, cond in enumerate(badges[:6]):
            color = QColor(condition_color(cond.get('id')))
            badge = self.scene().addRect(
                left + index * (badge_w + gap), top, badge_w, badge_h,
                QPen(QColor('#ffffff'), 0.6), QBrush(color))
            badge.setParentItem(item)
            badge.setZValue(3)
            label = self.scene().addSimpleText(
                condition_short(cond.get('id'), cond.get('level')), font)
            label.setParentItem(item)
            label.setBrush(QBrush(QColor('#ffffff')))
            bounds = label.boundingRect()
            label.setPos(left + index * (badge_w + gap) + (badge_w - bounds.width()) / 2,
                         top + (badge_h - bounds.height()) / 2)
            label.setZValue(4)


==================== qt_portrait.py ====================
